4a1(rgyud): @##////dang po'i rdo rje bcom ldan 'das la mnos pa/snying po bzlas so/
4a2(rgyud): yang na bcom ldan 'das mthong bar 'tshal nas/rang nyid kyis gsol cig 
4a3(rgyud): ces brjod la/rang gis blang bar bya'o/de nas skad cig dngos grub mchog yid
4b1(rgyud): la 'dod pa 'grub par 'gyur ro /yang na bdag nyid kyis zla ba drug gis yid la 'dod pa'i 
4b2(rgyud): dngos grub mchog 'grub par 'gyur ro//zhes bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rjes gsungs 
4b3(rgyud): so;de nas ming gi dbang bskur ba yang de bzhin no//de nas byin gyis brlabs pa la sogs 
7a1(rgyud): @##//dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul du sems dpa' chen po brgyad bri bar bya'o//de nas zla ba nya nas brtsams te/phyag rgya chen por brtsams la/de bzhin du sngon du bsnyen pa'i tshul ltar bzlas 
7a2(rgyud): nas/zla ba nya'i mtshan mo thog thag tu/ci lta ba bzhin du skyo bar ma gyur pas/phyag rgya ma dkrol bar/bzlas brjod bya'o//de nas nang par bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal 'dzum zhing /zla ba dang mnyam pa'i mdangs 
7a3(rgyud): can mthong bar rtsol cing /sangs rgyas nyid dang rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid la sogs pa'i dngos grub mchog rnams rtsol bar 'gyur ro//cho ga gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa/byang chub sems dpa' 
7a4(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po brgyad kyis yongs su skor ba dang /phyogs du de bzhin gshegs pa rnams dang /rdo rje 'chang thams cad bri bar bya'o//de na de bzhin du bsgrubs pas sangs rgyas nyid la sogs 
7a5(rgyud): pa dngos grub thams cad 'grub po//ras la bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje snyoms pa dang bcas pa bris la/de'i spyan sngar dang po phyag rgya chen pos gnas nas bzlas brjod 'bum byas nas/'bum rdzogs nas phyag 
7a6(rgyud): na rdo rje nyid thob par 'gyur ro//de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa dpa'o gcig pa bris nas/de bzhin du mthar sangs rgyas par 'gyur ro//de nas klags pa tsam gyis 'grub pa'i las rnams dkyil 'khor du zhugs pa 
7a7(rgyud): tsam gyis 'grub par 'gyur ba ni/'di ltar ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rjes su mnyes par bya ba la ni/'dod chags la sogs pa'i dbang po thams cad phul bas so//rdo rje 'dzin pa chen po mnyes par bya ba 
7a8(rgyud): la ni/rdo rje sems dpa' dang 'khrud cing 'od byed pas so//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad mnyes par bya ba la ni/dpag tu med pa'i bzlas pas so//rdo rje thams cad mnyes par bya bya la ni/rang gi snying po bzlas 
7b1(rgyud): pas so//lha laswogs pa'i sems can rjes su chags par bya ba la ni/thams cad du 'bum bzlas pas so//bdag bsrung ba la ni yungs kar dang lan gcig gis so//gzhan bsrung ba la ni rdo rje skor bso
7b2(rgyud): thams cad bsrung ba la ni yid kyis lan gcig go//mtshams dang phyogs dang dkyil 'khor thams cad bcing ba la ni/lta ba 'am yungs kar laswogs pas so//sems can rengs pas byed pa la ni/
7b3(rgyud): dbugs mi 'byin par bzlas pas so//sems can thams cad rmugs par bya ba la ni/rdo rje bcing bas so//sems can thams chad rmongs par byed pa la ni/mig btsums nas bzlas pas so//thams
7b4(rgyud): cad bsad pa la ni rdo rje bral bas so//sems can tham cad rgyas btab pa la ni/rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma'i rgya phyag rgyas so//thams cad snying 'dren cing 'byin pa la ni/yid kyis bzlas paso
7b5(rgyud): sems can thams cad skrag par bya ba la ni/rdo rje'ajigs byed kyi phyag rgyas so//nor thams cad dgug pa la ni gser gyi zlas paso/nor dang 'bru thams cad dgug pa la rdo rje rnal 'byor ma'i
7b6(rgyud): phyag rgyas so//thams cad rjes su chags pa la ni/phi wang gdung ba dang bcas pa'i ka ka'i glu len pas bzlas brjod bya'o//thams cad 'bigs pa dang /rdo rje rjes su chags par bya ba la ni/rdo rje mda'i phyag rgyaso
7b7(rgyud): thams cad dga' bar bya ba la ni phyogs thams cad kyi de bzhin gshegs pa mnyes paso//bsdug pa'i mche bstan par bya ba la ni/snying pos mnyes par mtshir bas so//ma rungs pa sgrub pa la ni rdo rje snyems paso
7b8(rgyud): brgyan thams cad 'phrog pa la ni/'bar ba'i lta bas 'phrog pas so//thams cad yid tshim pa la ni rdo rje khu tshur snying khar gzhag pas so//mun pas snang ba la ni rdo rje kha shar du ltas te skor bas so//lus
9a1(rgyud): @#//cad g.yog par bya ba la ni/zhi ba'i gnas su 'bum zlas pas so//thams cad la lan ldon pa la ni/rdo rje hUM mdzad kyi rdo rjes so//rdzu 'phrul dang thams cad bstan pa la ni rdo rje rdzu 'phrul gyi phyag rgyas so//
9a2(rgyud): phyang 'phrul thams cad bstan pa la ni/rdo rje ting nge 'dzin gyi mchog gis so//rang gi lus la sogs pa'i dngos po thams cad la sangs rgyas dang/rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa rgyu dang mi rgyu ba'i dngos po thams cad stan
9a3(rgyud): pa la ni/rdo rje rnam par 'phrul pas so//lha thams cad dbab pa la ni rdo rje skor bas so//klu thams cad rjes su 'brangs pa la ni klu'i rgyal po'i mtshor bzlas brjod pa'o//gnod sbyin thams cad mngag gzhug byas pa
9a4(rgyud): la ni/rdo rje bka' sgo bas so//'byung po thams cad 'dul ba ni/lha chen po mnan nas khros pas zlas brjod pa'o//ser ba thams cad bzlog pa la ni//rdo rje bsrung gi phyag rgyas so//klog 'bab pa
9a5(rgyud): thams cad bzlog pa ni/rdo rje khro gnyer can gyi phyag rgyas so//char dbab pa la ni klu'i mtshor bzlas brjod bya'o//char bcad pa la ni dri ma med pa chu sbyin pa'o//sprin bskyed pa la ni/klu bris nas gdugs
9a6(rgyud): pas bdug par bya'o//sprin gshig pa la ni rdo rje nam mkha'i sprin gyis so//chu med pa'i phyogs su shin du bsil ba'i chu'i phyogs rtag tu 'byung ba la ni rdo rje klu'i phyag rgyas so//gter thams cad shes par bya ba la ni snod
9a7(rgyud): du chu blugs par bya'o//gter thams cad dgo ba la ni rdo rje 'byor gyis so//gter thams cad rnyed pa la ni rdo rje nor gyis so//rgyal srid thams cad thob par bya ba la rdo rje dbang skur ba'i cho gas so//sa'i bdag por
9a8(rgyud): bya ba la ni rdo rje sems dpa' dbang skur ba'i phyag rgyas so//dkyil 'khor thams cad sgrub pa'i la ni//rdo rje 'khor lo phyag rgyas so//phyag rgya thams cad sgrub pa la ni//bde ba chen po'i dam
9b1(rgyud): tshig gi phyag rgyas so//dam tshig thams cad sgrub pa la ni/rab tu dga' ba'i rdo rje'i phyag rgyas so//dngos po sgrub pa la ni rdo rje snyems pa'i lcags kyu'i phyag rgyas so//ma grub pa thams cad
9b2(rgyud): grub pa la ni rdo rje sgrub pa'i phyag rgyas so//rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad la ni//rdo rje rig pa 'dzin pa'i phyag rgyas so//rdo rje'i thams cad sgrub pa la ni //rdo rje thams cad kyi mchog gis so phyag rgyas so//
9b3(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' thams cad sgrub pa li ni/thugs kyi rdo rjes so//rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid sgrub pa la ni/rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgyas so//sangs rgyas nyid sgrub pa la ni//rdo rje ye shes kyi phyag rgyas so//
9b4(rgyud): bde ba chen po'i don yod pa'i dam tshig chen po de kho na nyid rdo rje zhes bya ba rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po rdzogas+h+yo////de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis/yang de
9b5(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis zhi ba'i chos nyid mngon par rdzogs par byang chub sgrub pa zhes bya ba'i/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul du 'di gsungs te//rdo rje bsra ba nyid kyi phyir
9b6(rgyud): rdo rje mnyam pa nyid mngon par rdzogs pa byang chub pa ni/byang chub chen po'o//don cig pa nyid kyi phyir mnyam pa nyid mngon par rjogs par byang chub pa la ni/byang chub chen po'o//ngo bo nyid rnam par dag pa
9b7(rgyud): phyir chos kyi mngon par rdzogs par byad chub pa ni/byang chub chenapo'o//thams cad la rnam par mi rtog pa nyid kyi phyir mngon par rdzogs par bya chub pa ni/byang chub che po'o//ngo bo nyid lag na rdo rje gang la la chos
9b8(rgyud): mngon par sgrub bzhi po//'di dag nyan pa dang/'dzin pa dang/klog pa dang/sgom pa de sdig pa thams cad las spyod kyang byang chub snying po la thug gi bar du ngan song thams cad las yang dag par 'das par
10a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur ro//de nas de skad ces bka' stsal nas/ye shes kyi khu tshur yongs su 'dzin pa/don gyi gnas 'di nyid rgya cher ston cing bzhin 'dzum pa dang bcas te/chos thams cad kyi 
10a2(rgyud): mnyam pa nyid kyi snying po 'di gsungs so//a/'di gsungs tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa 'di thams cad yang dag par 'dus par mdzad nas/bcom ldan 'das kyis phyogs thams cad du rdo rje sems dpa'i tshul gyis 
10a3(rgyud): bzhugs pa dang /sems dpa' chen po brgyad phyi'i dkyil 'khor du/phyogs brgyad du bzhugaso//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i//
10a4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyos par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi de kho na nyid rdo rje zhes bya ba'i dam tshig chen po'i dkyil 'khor chen po 'di gsungs so//
10a5(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul du phyi'i dkyil 'khor bris la/de'i dbus su thig gsum gyis 'khor lo bris la bcom ldan 'das kyi thad dkar sna tshogs pad ma rab tu gnas pa'i 
10a6(rgyud): de'i 'khor lo'i lte ba la/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa rtag pa chen po'o//sa la swogs pa las bya ba gzhag par bya'o//de'i phyogs thams cad du sa laswogs pa las byas pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa 
10a7(rgyud): brgyad gzhag gam bri bar bya'o//de nas mtshon gyi thig le rnams kyis dkyil 'khor la bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs rnams rdo rje 'dzin pa dang bcas pa bri bar bya'o//phyi'i dkyil 'khor gyi phyogs bzhi 'tshaMs 
10a8(rgyud): rnamasu byang chub sems dpa' brgyad ji lta ba bzhin du gzhag la/bum pa gang ba bzhi sgo rnams su dgod par bya zhing /byang chub kyi mtshan ma rnams gzhag par bya ste/shar nas brtsams te/ne'us gang ba'i 
10b1(rgyud): dang bcas pa'i bu dang/ro drug gis bkang zhing dbyig dang gser dang/rin po che dang gos la sogs pas yongs su gang ba'i snod dang 'jam zhing shin du bde ba'i stan dang/sangs rgyas kyi sku gzugs dgos so//
10b2(rgyud): de nas de bzhin du gos gyon la de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i phyag rgya bcings la zhugs nas;bdag nyid slob dpon med par yang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi byin gyis
10b3(rgyud): rlob par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rtog pa dang/dngos grub thams cad la mi tshugs pa dang;mi gsod pa dang;sgrib pa thams cad dang bral ba thob par 'gyur ro//de nas slob ma yang gzhugo
10b4(rgyud): zhugs nas rdo rje rtse lnga pa byin la rdo rje ming gis dbang bskur ba yang byin gyis bya'o//de nas dam tshig 'di dag bgo bar bya'o//sems can kun la phan phyir dang//brtan pa thams cad grub pa'i phyir 
10b5(rgyud): rnam par mi rtog sbyor ba yis//ci bde bar ni thams cad gyis;zhes brjod do/de nas mtshan ma bzhi'i sbyor pas ji lta bas skal pa ji lta ba bzhin sbyin par bya'o//dam tshig chen po'i dkyil 'khor ro;
10b6(rgyud): de nas bsgrub pa ni ras la;bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad 'dzum pa dang bcas pa;de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi khu tshur gyi phyag rgya yongasu 'dzin par bya'o/gzhan thams cad dam tshig chen po'i
10b7(rgyud): dkyil 'khor du tshul du bya'o//de nas de bzhin du sems dpa' mchog bsgrub pa dang/dngos grub mchog rnams kyang de bzhin no//gzhan yang ras la bcom ldan 'das dpa' bo gcig pu bris te/de'i mdun
10b8(rgyud): du rdo rje tshig gis bzlas brjod 'bum byas na/ye shes kyi khu tshur yongasu 'dzin pas;mtshan mo thog thag bzlas na/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa thob cing/dngos grub thams cad 'grub pa'i byang chub sems 
11a1(rgyud): @//dpar 'gyur la de nas de bzhin gshegs par 'gyuro/de nas ma bsgrubs pas kyang dkyil 'khor du zhugs pa tsam nyid kyis las rnams 'grub par 'gyur ro//'di lta ste de bzhin gshegs pa thams
11a2(rgyud): cad la mchod pa la ni ji snyed pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i bde ba thams cad phul bas so//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad mchod pa la ni/bdag phul bas so/dkon mchog mchod pa la ni/
11a3(rgyud): me tog bzlas pas so//sems can thams cad mchod pa la ni thams cad byams pas bzlas pas so//chos thams cad bsdud pa la ni/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshig bzhi po 'don pas so//bdud
11a4(rgyud): thams cad 'dul ba la ni gtan zhing nges pa'i bzlas pas so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dbang bskur ba'i thob pa la ni rgyan thams cad phul zhing rang nyid bcings pas so//de
11a5(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cadakyi dbang bskur ba thob pa chos nyid thaMd thob pa la ni rdo rje pad ma la ni lta bas so/de bzhin gsheg+sa pa thams cad kyi las kyi dngos grub thob pa la ni thams cad rdo rje'i las
11a6(rgyud): kyi mchog gis so//sangs rgyas nyid rab tu thob pa la ni/rdo rje ye shes phyag rgya chen pos so/bde ba thams cad don yod pa'i dam tshig rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi de kho
11a7(rgyud): na nyid kyi rdo rje dam tshig ces bya ba rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+ho////de nas ma rungs pa thams cad 'dul ba'o/de bzhin gshegs pa shAkya thub pas yang chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyis rnam
11a8(rgyud): par rgyal ba bsdus pa zhes bya'o//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa bsgrub pa 'di gsungs so//'dod chags kyi spros pa med pa nyid kyis zhe sdang gi spros pa med pa nyid do//zhe sdang gi spros pa med pa yid kyis gti
11b1(rgyud): mug gi spros pa med pa nyid do/chos thams cad spros pa med pa nyid kyis shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa spros pa med pa nyid du rig par bya'o//lag na rdo rje gang la la shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi na pa'i tshul 'di nyan pa dang
11b2(rgyud): 'dzin pa dang/sgom pa des 'dul ba'i dbang gis phyir khams gsum du skyes pa'i sems can thams cad bsad kyang ngan song du skye bar mi 'gyur te/myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon
11b3(rgyud): par rdzogs par sangs rgyas par 'gyuro//de nas bcom ldan 'das la rdo rje chos nyid 'di'i don rgya cher ston cing 'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgyal ba'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya bcings nas khro gnyer dang bcas shing
11b4(rgyud): smin ma bteg te/'bar bar lta ba la pad ma'i gdong khro zhing bkod de/me chab rtsigs nas rkang pa g.yas pa'i bskum pa'i tshul du gnas pas gnas nas/rdo rje hUM zhes bya ba'i snying po 'di gsungs so//hUM/de nas
11b5(rgyud): 'di gsungs pa tsam gyis 'jig rten thams cad kyi 'dod pa'i dbang phyug la sogs pa'i 'jig rten gsum gyi bdag po rnams la yang dag par bkug ste/ltung bar mdzad nas/dkyil 'khor du
11b6(rgyud): bcug nas rnam par rgyal ba mdzad la bcings te/dbang du byas shing 'dul zhing/rjesu btul te/blangs nas yang dam tshig gis bcings nas 'jig rten gyi bdag po dbang phyug chen po'i chung ma dang
11b7(rgyud): bcas pa;zhabs gnyisu phub nas/mnan par mdzad do/de nas de rnams kyis 'jig rten gsum po thams cad du sgra chen po bsgrags te/kye rdo rje 'chang yongasu bskyab par mdzad cig/bdag bag phab rnams ma
11b8(rgyud): gzir cig/'dir khyod la bdag skyabasu mchi'o/dam tshig dang sgo ba yang dag par sgrub par bgyi'o//ma mo thams cad 'dul zhing ka la btags nas dkyil 'khor yang rnam par bshig ste/phyag rgya thaMd dang/
12a1(rgyud): @#//dam tshig kyang bral zhing /rnam par gshig pa dang /gdon thams cad kyang bzlog cing /nad thams cad dang rims tha dad gnod pa dang /dug dang ngan 'gro rnams bral zhing rnam par gshig/sgra chen po sgrags
12a2(rgyud): shing 'jigs pa chen pos 'jigs par byed pa'i tshig nyid kyis bcom ldan 'das nyid la skyabs su song ngo //bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang zhal rnam par gzhad rnam nas/de rnams dkyil 'khor du bcug te/dam tshig gis bcibs 
12a3(rgyud): nas rdo rje rin chen gyis dbang skur nas/rang gi mtshan ma rnams la rdo rje byin gyis brlabs te/rdo rje ming gis dbang skur ba yang rjes su btsal nas/sems can ma lus pa'i don du phyi rdo rje'i rigs su rab tu gzhag go/de nas 
12a4(rgyud): rdo rje khro bo'i rdo rje las/phyag na rdo rje dang 'dra ba'i dngos po bdag nyid can kha dog dang /brgyan dang mtshon cha rnam pa sna tshogs pa bsnams pa rnam par 'gyur ba'i rdo rje'i rigs su mche ba brtsigs pa //tshems kyis 
12a5(rgyud): sgros mnon pa/smin ma mi zad cing /khro gnyer gnyer ba spyan dang dbu skra dang ag tsom 'bar ba/zhal 'jigs pa //rdo rje dang lcags kyu dang /zhags pa dang mtshon cha mda' dang /dbyig 
12a6(rgyud): pa kha traM kha dang /gri rings dang /chu gri phyag na bsnams pa/khro bo chen po hUM mdzad las phyung ste/bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje dkyil 'khor gyi tshul gyis bzhugs so//lha la sogs pa de 
12a7(rgyud): rnams kyang rang gi dkyil 'khor gyis byin gyis brlabs nas/phyi'i dkyil 'khor du gzhag go//gdon la sogs pa de dag kyang dkyil 'khor gyi phyi rim na gnas so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin 
12a8(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad thugs rje chen po'i thabs khro bo'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//khams gsum mtha' dag las rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba rdo rje byin gyis brlabs pa 'di 
12b1(rgyud): gsungaso//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//rdo rje 'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ste/khams gsum rnam par rgyal zhes bya//'dul ba chen po 'dul byed pa//dkyil 'khor dang po sbyor ba yis//phyi'i dkyil
12b2(rgyud): 'khor dang po sbyor ba yis //phyi'i dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//de yi nang gi dkyil 'khor brgyad pa dag dang mtshungs //de yi dbus su khro bo che//phyag na rdo rje gzhag par bya//sangs rgyas phreng laswog+sa
12b3(rgyud): pas spras//ud dpal sngon po'i 'dra ba 'dra ba//zhal ni cung zad mche ba rtsigs//khro bcas gzhad pa'i zhal mnga' ba//g.yon rkyang ba yis legs bzhugs la//rdo rje hUM mdzad bsdus pas
12b4(rgyud): g.yon gyi zhabs kyis legs gnas pa //de yi dbang phyug che//g.yas kyi dka' thub zlog pa ma yi//nu ma'i stengasu rab tu 'jog/'bar phrer 'jigs pa'i 'od ldan pa//sa g.yog+sa
12b5(rgyud): bcas pa'i khams rnams kyi/khro bo kun du gzhag par bya//'bar phreng 'khrigs pa'i 'od ldan pa'i//'khor lo de yang gzhag par bya//'bar ba 'khrigs pa'i 'od can bri//
12b6(rgyud): phyi'i dkyil 'khor gnas rnamasu //phyi yi dkyil 'khor tshogs rnams so//khro bo'i mtshan ma bzhi po ni//gzhu dang mda' dang /ral gri dang //'khor lo de bzhin rtse gsu sgreng //sgro rnams kun
12b7(rgyud): du bri bar bya //phyi rol gnas pa'i don rnams kyis //sna tshogs gtor mas bkang ba dang /ba dan sngon pos dril btags shing //rtse mo kho dag sna tshogs pa'o//de nas 'jug pa la ni yan lag thams cad
12b8(rgyud): ras sngon po zung gis g.yogs shing //khros pas rdo rje dam tshig gi phyag rgya dang bcas pas 'dzin pa zhugs na/bcom ldan 'das kyis rdo rje'i rigs kyi slob dpon du byin gyis brlabs nas mdzad do//
13a1(rgyud): @##//de nas brtsams te sdig pa la sogs pa rnam par grol zhing/mi bsod pa dang mi tshugs pa dang/mu stegs thams cad tshar bcad pa dang/phan thogs nus shing thams cad las rgyal ba dang dngos 
13a2(rgyud): grub thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//de nas slob ma de bzhin du bcug la/snying po byin te dkyil 'khor thams cad du bya'o/de nas rdo rje rnon po rtsibs bzhi 'bar ba byin la/rdo rje ming gis dbar skur bas dbang skur
13a3(rgyud): la|rdo rje rtul shugs byin par byas te//'di ni bdug pa 'dul byed pa//lag na rdo rje rdo rje ste//ma rungs thams cad 'dul ba la//khyod kyis kyang ni rtag tu bcang//de nas de bzhin du 'byung bar bya'o//rdo rje 'di dkyil
13a4(rgyud): 'khor ro//de nas sgrub pa la ni ras la bcom ldan 'das khro bo'i dbang phyug byas la/rdo rje dkyil 'khor gyi tshogs kyang sems dpa' chen po thams cad bya'o//phyogs thams cad du ni lha la sogs pa
