18a8(rgyud): pa'o//shar lho 'tshamasu phyag rgya'i khu tshur zhes bya ba dang gnyis la ni nor gyi khu tshur zhes bya ba dang/gsum pa la ni chos kyi khu tshur zhes bya ba dang/bzhi pa la ni las kyi khu tshur zhes bya bar rang gi phyag
18b1(rgyud): rgya dang /phyag mtshan yang 'dzin par bya'o//sgo rnams su ni rdo rje bsnyems ma laswogs pa'i phyag rgya rnams bri bar bya'o//de nas dam tshig gi phyag rgya yongs su bzung ba rang nyid zhugs nas/phyag rgya thams cad kyi slob 
18b2(rgyud): dpon du 'gyur ro//de nas brtsams te dngos grub thams cad don yod pa 'thob par 'gyur ro//phyag rgya thams cad kyi ye shes dbang phyug bla na med yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu myur du mngon par rdzogs par
18b3(rgyud): 'tshang rgya bar 'gyuro//der zhugs nas phyag rgya bcings pas bcom ldan 'das las bslab par bya zhing //rdo rje ming gis dbang skur bas dbang skur la//'di skad ces brjod par bya'o//sku gsung thugs kyi
18b4(rgyud): rdo rje yi//gzugs brnyan lta bur sbyor ba yi//phyag rgya rgya kun zhes bya bar sgrags//dam tshig dngos grub kun ster ba'o//slob ma gzhug pa la de bzhin no//phyag rgya thams cad kyid kyil 'khoro
18b5(rgyud): bsgrub pa dang /dngos grub thams cad kyang de bzhin no//de nas ma bsgrubs pa'i las rnams kyang de bzhin no//phyag rgya tha mas cad bcing ba sgrub pa la ni snying khar rdo rje khu tshur gyis so//rgya phyag rgya
18b6(rgyud): thams cad sgrub pa la ni//rdo rje chen po'i khu tshur gyis so//dam tshig thams cad sgrub pa la ni/rdo rje 'dod pa'i khu tshur gyis so//sems can thams cad rjes su chags par bya ba la ni//rdo rje chags pa chen po'i
18b7(rgyud): khu tshur gyis so//thams cad dbang du bya ba la ni//rdo rje bshad pa'i khu tshur gyiso//thams cad bcing ba la ni rdo rje bsdams pa'i khu tshur chen pos so//thams cad bsdams pa la ni//rdo rje lu gu rgyud kyi khu
18b8(rgyud): tshur gyiso//thams cad lkug par bya ba la ni/rdo rje zhal zhal gyi khu tshur gyis so//thams chad btsir ba la ni//rdo rje sdams pa'i khu tshur gyis so/thams cad bsad pa la ni//rdo rje khro bo'i khu tshur
19a1(rgyud): @#//gyis so//thams cad bza' ba la ni gnod sbyin gyi khur tshur gyis so//thams cad gzhig pa ni rdo rje nges par btsir ba'i khu tshur gyis so//thams can dral ba la ni//rdo rje dril bu'i khu 
19a2(rgyud): tshur gyis so//thams cad dbab pa la ni/rdo rje 'bebs pa'i khu tshur gyis so//tham sa cad gar byed du gzhug pa la ni/rdo rje gar gi khu tshur gyis so//thamasacad smrar gzhug pa ni rdo rje gsung gi khu 
19a3(rgyud): tshur gyis so//tha mas cad rengs par byed pa la ni//rdo rje dmigs pa'i khu tshur gyis so/gzhan gyi dpung thams cad lkug pa dang /phye bor bya ba la ni rdo rje chen po phye'o//khu tshur gis so//phyag rgya thaMd
19a4(rgyud): dkrol ba la ni/rdo rje thar pa'i khu tshur gyis so/rdo rje namakha' 'gro ma thams cad phyag rgyas gzhig pa la ni/rdo rje hU mdzad kyi rdo rje khu tshur gyis so//bdug pa thams cad kyi phyag rgya dbye ba la ni hUM
19a5(rgyud): mdzad kyi rdo rje khu tshur kyiso//phyag rgya thams cad bcings pa la ni/rdo rje khu tshur gyiso//thams cad rgyas gdab pa la ni/rdo rje ma mo'i khu tshur gyiso//thams chad phye mar bya ba la ni rdo rje bzang po'i 
19a6(rgyud): khu tshur gyiso//thams can rnam par gzhig par bya ba la ni/rdo rje bdug pa'i khu tshur gyis so//thams cad gzhig pa dang /thams cad gzhom pa'i la ni/dbang po'i rdo rje khu tshur gyis so//chu'i nang du
19a7(rgyud): gnas shing chags pa la ni/rdo rje gnon po'i khu tshur gyiso/gzugs mang por ston pa la ni sna tshogs rdo rje khu tshur gyis so/mi snang ba'i dngos grub la ni/mi snang ba'i rdo rje khu tshur gyiso//rgyu ba dang mi 
19a8(rgyud): rgyu ba'i gnas dngos po rnams 'byung ba la ni/rdo rje namakha'i khu tshur gyiso//namakha' la 'gro ba'i dngos grub la ni rdo rje khu tshur gyis so//reg pa 'dzin pa'i dngos grub la ni/rdo rje ral gyi khu tshur gyis so//
19b1(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mchod pa'i dngos grub la ni/rdo rje las kyi khu tshur gis so /thams cad mkhyen pa grub pa la ni/rdo rje ye shes kyi khu tshur gyis so//nor thams cad kyi dngos grub la ni;
19b2(rgyud): rdo rje nor gyi khu tshur gyis so//phyag rgya thams cad bdag nyid la ni/rdo rje phyag rgya'i khu tshur gyis so//las thams cad kyi dngos grub pa ni/rdo rje thams cad kyi khu tshur gyis so//dkyil 'khor thaMd grub pa
19b3(rgyud): la ni/rdo rje 'dzin pa'i khu tshur gyis so//rang nyid 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar gyi dngos grub rnams ston pa la ni/rdo rje mchog gi khu tshur gyis so//rdo rje 'dzin pa la ni rdo rje snyems pa'
19b4(rgyud): khu tshur gyis so//sangs rgyas nyid la ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi khu tshur gyis so/bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog pa'i rgyal po las/phyag rgya thams cad kyi khu
19b5(rgyud): tshur gyi dam tshig ces bya ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+h+yo// O //de nas bcom ldan 'das chos thaMd spros pa med pa'i de bzhin gshegs pas yang 'khor
19b6(rgyud): lo ltar yi ge bskor ba zhes bya ba/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//ngo bo nyid med pa'i tshul gyis chos thams cad stong pa'o //mtshan ma med pa'i tshul gyis //chos thams cad mtshan ma
19b7(rgyud): med pa'o//smon pa med pa'i tshul gyis chos thabs cad smon pa med pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa yongasu dag pas chos thaMd rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal ba'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gzho nur
19b8(rgyud): gyur pas don 'di nyid rgya cher ston to//bzhin 'dzum pa'i bzhin du ral gyi nges de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd la 'debs shing shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin+pa pa'i snying po 'di gsungaso//a/de nas 'di gsungs pa tsaM gyis 
20a1(rgyud): @#//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 'dus par mdzad nas /dang po'i dkyil 'khor gyi tshul gyis bzhugs so/de nas 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas chos thams cad ngo bo nyid med par smos pa med pa'i
20a2(rgyud): ral gri zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas/'khor lo ltar skor ba'i yige zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'di gsungaso/phyi'i dkyil 'khor ci lta ba'i nang du 'khor lo bris la/a la sogs pa'i yi ge'i phreng ba
20a3(rgyud): go rim bzhin du bra bar bya'o//de nas dkyil 'khor brgyad pa'i tshul gis thig btab pa de'i lte ba la bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gzhon nu'i cha lugs 'chang zhing 'dzum pa dang bcas pa/namakha'i bcad phyag bzhi dang
20a4(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi la 'debs par gzhag par bya'o//de'i 'dun la sogs par de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams gzhag par bya'o//phyi'i 'tshams rnamasu/'tshams ma bzhi
20a5(rgyud): gzhag par bya'o//'di lta ste/ral gri dang/mdung dang lhung zed dang klegs bam rnamaso//de nas ral gri dang kregs bam gyi phyag rgya de bzhin du chos kyi slob dpon du byin gyis brlob po//de nas
20a6(rgyud): ral gri blangs te ral gri ming gi dbang skur bas dbang skur la/'di skad ces chos 'di bshad par bya ste/ral gri 'di ni rtag zung la//shes rab tshul 'di dka' smra zhing//namakha' sangs rgyas sbyor ba yis//
20a7(rgyud): 'debs na sangs rgyas myur du 'gyur//sangs rgyas bstan pa bsrung phyir dang//sems can mang po bsrung bya'i phyir//'dir bsad sdig par mi 'gyur zhing//bsod nams dag kyang thob par 'gyur//slob ma
20a8(rgyud): rnams la'ang de bzhin du/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa yongasu rdzogs pa'i dngos grub rnams kyis/myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyuro//chos kyi
20b1(rgyud): dkyil 'khor ro//bsgrub pa'ang de bzhin no//ma bsgrubs par yang de bzhin du las rnams 'grub par 'gyur ba ni/stong pa nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin thob pa la ni/thams cad mkha' 'gro bsgoms pas so//
20b2(rgyud): mtshan ma med pa'i ting nge 'dzin thob pa la na//mtshan ma dang bral ba'i ting nge 'dzin thob pa ni bsgoms pas so//smon pa med pa'i ting nge 'dzin 'thob pa la ni/smon pa med pa bsgoms pas so//rang bzhin gyis
20b3(rgyud): 'od gsal ba chos kyi ye shes thob pa la ni//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa bsgoms pas so//nyon mongs pa thams cad thog cod pa ni/chos sgoms pas so//yi ge thams cad kyi nges pa'i tshig gi
20b4(rgyud): ye shes la ni/spros pa med pa'i ye shes bsgoms pas so//gnas thams cad du yi ge chos kyi ba ma thob pa la ni/a laswogs pa'i yi ge'i ngo bo nyid bsgoms pas so//de bzhin gshegs pa 
20b5(rgyud): thams cad byang chub thob pa la ni/rdo rje ral gri'i phyag rgyas so//bde ba chen po don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las/'khor lo chen po ltar yi ge skor ba'i dam tshig ces
20b6(rgyud): bya ba rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+h+yo////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo'i nang du 'dus pa'i de bzhin gshegs
20b7(rgyud): pas yang 'khor lo chen po la 'jug pa zhes bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol du phyinapa'i tshul 'di bshad do//rdo rje mnyam nyid la 'jug pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi dkyil 'khor la 'jug par 'gyuro
20b8(rgyud): don gyi mnyam pa nyid la 'jug pa ni/'khor lo thams cad la yang 'jug par 'gyur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba nyid kyi don 'di rgya cher ston cing bzhin
21a1(rgyud): @#//rab tu 'dzum nas/rdo rje 'khor lo thams cad la 'jug pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i snying po 'di gsungs so//hUM de nas 'di gsums pa tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying
21a2(rgyud): po can khams gsum du rtogs pa'i sems can thams cad dkyil 'khor du zhugs nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi dam tshig sems dpa' rnams des par
21a3(rgyud): byung nas/dkyil 'khor gyi tshul gyi ji lta ba bzhin du zhugaso/de nas bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje khro bo 'khor lo thams cad la rab tu 'jug pa'i phyag rgya zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin 
21a4(rgyud): la snyoms par zhugs nas/dkyil 'khor thams cad la 'jug pa'idam tshig ces bya ba'i 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor 'di gsungasohaphyi'i dkyil 'khor ji lta ba bzhin du byas te/nang gi dkyil 'khor
21a5(rgyud): du rdo rje 'khor lo bris la/dbus su rdo rje 'khor lo bskor ba gzhag par bya'o//phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' thaMd du dkyil 'khor dang po'i tshul du gzhag par bya'o//phyi'i dkyil 'khor
21a6(rgyud): gyi 'tshams rnams su rje rigs kyi dkyil 'khor bzhi bri bar bya'o/sgo rnams su mtshan ma rnams gzhag par bya ste/'di lta ste/rdo rje sems dpa'i sku dang rdo rje khro bo'i sku dang /spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug gi sku dang/
21a7(rgyud): nam mkha'i snying po'i sku rnamaso//de nas 'khor lo'i phyag rgyas ji lta ba bzhin du dkyil 'khor thams cad du sangs rgyas nyid dang/rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid du 'grub par 'gyuro//de nas 'khor lo blangs la/'khor lo'i ming
21a8(rgyud): gi dbang skur ba blangs pa bya zhing/gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor bzhi'i thig btab pa'i rigs rnams la/'jug par bya'o/slob ma gzhug pa yang de bzhin no//'khor lo'i dkyil 'khoro//sgrub pa yang
21b1(rgyud): de bzhin no/ma bsgrubs par yang dkyil 'khor thams cad du 'jug pa la ni rdo rje 'khor lo ste/rang gi khar bcug la gzhag par bya'o//dkyil 'khor du 'jug pa la ni/'khor lo chen pos so//
21b2(rgyud): de bzhin du phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor 'jug pa la ni/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo'i phyag rgyas so//de bzhin du dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor du 'jug pa ni/dam tshig gi
21b3(rgyud): phyag rgyaso/las kyi dkyil 'khor du ni zhugs pa la ni/rdo rje thams cad kyi 'khor los so//de bzhin du rjes su chags pa dang/dbang du bya ba dang/don thob pa dang/rgyas gdab pa dang/bcad pa dang/
21b4(rgyud): 'jug pa dang/mchod pa dang/gdug pa 'dul ba'i las rnams la ni sems dpa' chen po rgyad kyi phyag g.yon na 'khor lo dang bcas pa rnams kyiso//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog 
21b5(rgyud): pa'i rgyal po las dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi rdo rje dam tshig ces bya ba rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+h+yo////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod pa thaMd
21b6(rgyud): kyi cho ga rgyas pa snod kyi mchod pa thams cad kyi mchog ces bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//byang chub tu sems bskyed pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'o//seMn
21b7(rgyud): gyi khams thams cad yongs su skyob pa nyid ni/de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'o //dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa thems cad kyi la mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa 'o/shes 
21b8(rgyud): rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa yi ge 'dri ba las dang/yi ge 'drir gzhug pa dang/'dzin pa dang/klog pa dang/brjod pa dang/ston pa dang/nyan pa dang/seMs pa dang/sgom pa dang/brjod par byed pa ni de bzhin gshegs thaMd
22a1(rgyud): @#//mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po namakha' mdzod don de nyid rgya chen ston/bzhin rab tu 'dzum zhing las thams cad don yod pa'i dam tshig ces 
22a2(rgyud): bya ba'i snying po 'di gsungaso//de nas 'di gsungs pa tsam gyis namakha'i khams las mchod pa thams cad kyi cho ga rgyas pa namakha'i khams ma lus ma spros par gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das namakha' 
22a3(rgyud): mdzod kyi byang chub sems dpa'i tshul gyis dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul du zhugs so//de nas byang chub semsdpa' namakha' mdzod mchod pa thams cad kyi chog rgyas par byin gyis brlob pa zhes bya 
22a4(rgyud): ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/rdo rje'i mchod pa'i mchog don yod pa'i dam tshig zhes bya ba 'di gsungaso/nyi phyi'i dkyil 'khor ji lta ba de bzhin du nang gi rin po che ka brgyad 
22a5(rgyud): byas pa dbus su bcom ldan 'das mtshon sna tshogs 'dzin pa /zhal 'dzum pa sku dang yan lag zla ba'i mdog la sogs pa'i kha dog lnga'i mdog can gzhag par bya'o//de'i thaMd du dam tshig ses 
22a6(rgyud): dpa' rnams gzhag par bya ste/'di lta ste/phyag na rdo rje dang/'jig rten dbang phyug la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' brgyad po byang chub kyi sems dang tinge 'dzin dang/dbang skur ba dang/phyag rgya 
22a7(rgyud): yongasu 'dzin pa dang/stong pa nyid smra ba dang/dkyil 'khor bri ba dang/mchod pa thams cad dang/sems can thams cad yongasu skyob pa ni/bla na med pa'i mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa phyag na 'dzin pa'i tshul gyis
22a8(rgyud): ji lta ba bzhin du gzhag go/phyi'i mtshamasu ni me tog la sogs pa rnams dang/sgo rnamasu dbyig pa dang/gser dang mu tig dang/rin po che dang/mtshan ma bzhi gzhag par bya'o//de nas de bzhin du rdo rje 
22b1(rgyud): thams cad kyi mchod pa thams cad thob par 'gyur ro//zhugs nas sna tshogs rdo rje blangs la//sna tshogs rdo rje'i ming gi dbang skur nas//mchod pa rnam pa bzhi bya ste/'di lta ste lus kyi las thaMd dang/
22b2(rgyud): phyag bya ba dang/ngag dang yid dang/sems kyi chos dbyings la swogs pas mchod cho ga rgyas pa thams cad kyis mchod par bya zhing rdo rje dang/phyag rgya mchod pas gcig tu byas pa'o//slob ma rnams 
22b3(rgyud): gzhug pa'i las kyang de bzhin no/las kyi dkyil 'khor ro/de nas sgrub pa dngos grub dag kyang de bzhin no/de nas ma bsgrubs par yang byang chub kyi sems kyi mchod pa la ni/rang gi snying khar rdo rje sgom
22b4(rgyud): paso/tinge 'dzin gyis mchod pa la ni thams cad dag pas sgos pas so//dbang skur ba'i mchod pa la ni rdo rje dbang skur ba sna tshogs so//phyag rgya'i mchod pa la ni sna tshogs rdo rje thams cad kyis so//
22b5(rgyud): stong pa nyid kyi mchod pa la ni/a la swogs pa'i yi ge'i lung stan paso//dkyil 'khor gyi mchod pa la ni/rdo rje 'khor lo sna tshogs kyiso//mchod pa thams cad la ni sna tshogs rdo rjeso//
22b6(rgyud): bdug pa 'dul bas mchod pa la ni khro bos so//me tog la swogs pa dor ba la ni bzlas pas so//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las/sna tshogs rdo rje don yod pa'i dam
22b7(rgyud): tshig ces bya ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+h+yo////de nas bcom ldanas de bzhin gshegs pa gdug pa thams cad 'dul nus pas/yang ye shes kyi khu tshur yongasu 'dzin pas sems 
22b8(rgyud): can thams cad 'dul ba ye shes kyi snying po zhes bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//sems can mnyam pa nyid pas khro bo mnyam nyid do//sems can thaMd 'dul ba nyid pas khro ba 'dul ba 
23a1(rgyud): @#//nyid do//sems can thams cad chos nyid pas khro ba chos nyid do//sems can thamasacad rdo rje nyid pas khro ba rdo rje nyid do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar sems can thams cad 'dul ba ni byang chub po//de nas 
23a2(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' bdud kyi stobs rab tu 'joms pa'i don de nyid rgya cher ston te/bzhin rab tu 'dzum zhing rdo rje mche ba'i phyag rgya dang/rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi gzugs kyi rdo rje khro bo'i gad mo zhes bya ba'i snying po 'di smra so
23a3(rgyud): hA/de nas 'di gsungs pa tsam gyis bdug pa can thams cad 'dul ba'i sems dpa' bcom ldan 'das rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi thugs las phyung ste/dang po dkyil 'khor gyi tshul du bzhug so//de nas byang chub sems pa 
23a4(rgyud): rdo rje gnod sbyin thams cad kyis 'dul ba khro bo'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/bdug pa thams cad 'dul zhing byin gyis brlob pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi dkyil 
23a5(rgyud): 'khor gsungs so//phyi yi dkyil 'khor tshul du ni//phyi yi dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//de yi nang rim nyid du ni//dkyil 'khor bzang po grub bzhi pa//sgo bzhi pa la thig bzhir ldan//tshon sna lnga ni rnam 
23a6(rgyud): par bris//ka ba brgyad kyi mdun gnas par//'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor yang dag ldan//de yi dbus su ci rigs par//rdo rje gnod sbyin gzhag bya ste//rdo rje me cha ma brtsigs pa dang/'bar ba'i phreng ba 'khrigs 
23a7(rgyud): pa'i 'od//'jigs byed kha dog sngo ba dang//ha ha zhes gzhad gsal ba'i zhal//de yi kun du gnod sbyin ni//shin du 'jigs pa gzhag par bya//de nyid dang po la swogs mchog /phyag na rdo rje la swogs
23a8(rgyud): mtshan//phyi yi dkyil 'khor 'tshams rnams su//mche ba'i phyag rgya bri bar bya//sgo'i gnas ni thams cad du//rdo rje'i mtshan ma gzhag par bya//de nas 'jug pa la swogs 'byung ste//rdo rje 
23b1(rgyud): mche ba'i phyag rgya dang/ha ha zhes gzhad pa dang bcas pa dang/bzhugs nas bdug pa thams cad 'dul nus shing myur du rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid thob par 'gyur ro//de nas zhugs nas rdo rje mche ba blangs nas
23b2(rgyud): rdo rje ming gi dbang skur bas dbang skur te/rdo rje rdo rje gnod sbyin nyid la bsnun no//de nas rdo rje gnod sbyin gya zhal nas//ha ha zhes bya ba'i sgra 'byung bar 'gyuro//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin
23b3(rgyud): gzhin gshegs pa thams cad bsrung ba po gnod sbyin gyi bdag po chen po phyag na rdo rje rtum por bdag grub pa yin no//zhes shes par bya'o//de nas phyag rgya thams cad bcing bar bya zhing sgrub par bya'o//de
23b4(rgyud): nas mchod la de bzhin du yang dag par tshim par byas nas/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul lan cig brjod la slar blang bar bya'o//de bzhin du phyag rgya bcing ba la sogs pa'i cho ga rgyas pa ni dang po
23b5(rgyud): la sogs dkyil 'khor thams cad du bya'o//phyag na rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor ro//sgrub pa dang dngos grub kyang de bzhin no//ma bsgrubs par yang dang po ji lta ba bzhin no//bdug pa thams cad
23b6(rgyud): 'dul ba la ni/rdo rje mche ba'i phyag rgyaso//sems can thams cad 'dul ba la ni/khro bos ha ha zhes rgod cing 'dul bas so//nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi 'dod chags dang bral ba las
23b7(rgyud): lhung bar bya ba la ni chos gos snam sbyar gyi sbyin sreg gis so//dbang skur ba thams cad dbrog pa la ni//khams thams cad kyi phye me'i sbyin sreg gis so//thams cad rgyas btab pa la ni//
23b8(rgyud): rdo rje khu tshur gyi phyag rgyas so//thams cad bcad pa la ni/gzugs brnyan byas nas gdub pas so//ma rungs pa thams cad dkyil 'khor rnam par gzhig pa la ni rdo rje gzungs nas 'jug par bya'o
24a1(rgyud): mchod pa la ni/khros nas bzlas brjod bya'o//thams cad skrad pa la ni 'jigs byed kyi lta bas lta bar bya'o//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog pa'i rgyal po las//rdo rje dam tshig ces bya ba'i rtog pa'i
24a2(rgyud): rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+h+yo////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa chos thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid la rdo rje don yod pa'i dam chig gi nas pas/yang chos thams cad kyi mchog ces
24a3(rgyud): bya ba shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid pas shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi pa'i mnyam pa nyid do//chos thams cad don nyid pas shes rab kyi pha ral du phyin don nyid do//thams cad chos
24a4(rgyud): nyid pas shes rab kyi pha ril du phyin pa chos nyid do//thams cad las mnyam pa nyid pas shes rib kyi pha rol du phyin pa mnyam pa nyid du rig par bya'o//de nas phyag na bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de
24a5(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi don yod pa'i dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor byin gyis brlob pa'i zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/don yod pa thams cad kyi dam
24a6(rgyud): tshig ces bya ba'i snying po 'di smraso//hU de nas 'di gsungs pa tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la sogs pa dkyil 'khor thams cad thugs las byung ste/dam tshig gi de kho na nyid rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor
24a7(rgyud): chen po'i der zhugs par gyur to//de nas phyag na rdo rjes bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs gnyis la nges par btud nas/dam tshig chen po'i dkyil 'khor 'di gsungs so//dkyil 'khor chen po bris la/dkyil
24a8(rgyud): 'khor gyi gnas brgyad du rdo rje khas gsum las rnam par rgyal ba'i la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor gzhag go/de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na  rdo rje dam tshig thams can kyi rdo rje byin gyis brlob pa zhes bya ba'i
24b1(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/dam tshig thams cad kyi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor 'di gsungs so//dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul du phyi'i dkyil 'khor bris la/de'i dbus su rdo rje'i 'khor lo bris nas
24b2(rgyud): 'khor lo'i gnas rnams su dkyil 'khor chen po laswogs pa bri bar bya'o//thams cad kyang dkyil 'khor brgyad pa'i thig btab cing/sems dpa' chen po gzhag par bya'o/phyi'i dkyil
24b3(rgyud): 'khor du phyi rol gyi rdo rje'i rigs ji ltar rjes su 'thun par bri bar bya'o//sgo rnams su rigs bzhi'i mtshan ma rnams gzhag par bya'o//de nas dam tshig chen po'i de kho na nyid kyi rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul
24b4(rgyud): gyis chog thams cad rgyas par bya'o//dam tshig chen po'i rdo rje'i de kho na nyid kyi dam tshig ces bya ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las/bde ba thams cad kyi dam tshig ces bya ba 'i rtog pa'i
24b5(rgyud): rgyal po chen po rdzogs s+ho////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rnam par snang mdzad kyis/yang sems can thams cad byin gyis rlob
24b6(rgyud): pa zhes bya ba/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun du bzang po thams cad kyi rang bzhin yin pas/sems can thams cad ni de bzhin
24b7(rgyud): gshegs pa'i snying po can no//thams cad rdo rje snying pos dbang skur ba nyid pas/sems can thams chad ni rdo rje'i snying po can no//ngag thams cad rab tu byung bas sems can thams cad ni chos kyi
24b8(rgyud): snying po can no//sems can thams cad kyi bya ba byed pa la sbyor bas/sems can thams cad las ni snying po can no//de nas phyi rol gyi rdo rje'i rigs rnams kyis ca co'i sgra phyung nas/chos
25a1(rgyud): @##//nyid kyi don rgya cher ston te/snying po phul te/de nas rdo rje dbang phyug chen pos de kho na nyid snying po 'di phul lo//tri 1 de nas dkyil 'khor bshad par bya ste/kun nas zlum pa'i thig 
25a2(rgyud): gsum pa//dkyil 'khor chen po sgo bzhi pa //'tshams rnamasu mdung rtse gsum bya'o//de'i dbus su dbang phyug chen po bri bar bya'o//de'i logs thams cad du tshogs bdag brgyad po bri bar bya'o//'di lta ste
25a3(rgyud): nag po chen po dang/dga' byed dbang phyug chen po dang /gang gi dbang phyug rnams ni phyogs su'o//'tshams rnamasu ni thod pa bzang po dang//gtum pa'i dbang phyug dang/lag na kha traM kha dang/ba la rna'i dbang
25a4(rgyud): phyug go/thig gi 'tshamasu ni ma mo'i tshogs thams cad gnas ji lta ba bzhin du bri bar bya'o//sgo rnamasu ni byug pa'i dbang phyug dang/mgo bo'i dbang phyug dang/dril rna dang/drag po chen po'o//
25a5(rgyud): phyi rol du ni 'byung po rnams dang/yid btags rnams bri bar bya'o//de nas slob dpon thal ba gcad mas lus skus shing gos gcang ma bgos te/mdung rtse gsum gyi mtshan ma gzhug 
25a6(rgyud): par bya'o//zhugs nas kyang snying po bzlas par bya'o//de nas slob ma de bzhin du bcug la lag par mdung rtse gsum pa byin nas chos bshad par bya'o//sems can kun la phan ni phyar dang//bstan pa thams cad
25a7(rgyud): sgrub phyir dang//'dod kun bdag po nyid kyi phyir//drag po'i las la gnas par gyis//de nas phyag rgya'i ye shes blangs te/gtor ma byin par bya'o//de nas brtsams te 'jigs pa med
25a8(rgyud): par dngos grub thams cad dang/phun sum tshogs pa thams cad kyi bde ba dang/yid bde ba thams cad drag pos myong nas/drag po'i 'jig rten du mchod par 'gyuro//dkyil 'khor 
25b1(rgyud): chen po bzhi pa'i mdung thung gis mtshan ma zhes bya'o//de nas ma mo'i thams cad bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/dang po mgrin gcig tu thams cad dgug pa dang/gzhug pa dang/gsad pa dang/sgrub pa'i dam 
25b2(rgyud): tshig ces bya ba rang gi de kho na nyid kyi snying po 'di 'bul lo//b+h+yo/de nas de'i dkyil 'khor bshad par bya ste/zur bzhi byang gi sgo can gyi thig btab la/dbus su nag po gar byed pa bzhin du gzhag par bya'o//de'i
25b3(rgyud): phyogs dang phyogs 'tshams su drag po'i ma mo brgyad bri bar bya ste/'di lte ste/drag mo dang/tshangs pa mo dang/khyab 'jug mo dang/gzhon nu mo dang/nag mo dang nag mo chen mo dang/sha za mo dang/srin
25b4(rgyud): mo'o//dkyil 'khor gyi phyi'i 'tshams rnams su drag po'i mkha' 'gro ma bzhi bri bar bya ste/zhi ba ma mo dang/be drun mo dang/gtum mo dang/'jigs byed ma rnams so//de rnams kyi 'tshams ni
25b5(rgyud): ba mdung rtse gsum dang/phreng ba dang 'khor lo dang/dril bu dang thod pa dang/khrag dang bcas pa dang/khar sil dang/mche ba dang/kha traM kha'o/sgo bsrungs rnams kyi thod pa dang/rus pa'i lu
25b6(rgyud): gu rgyud dang/bya rgod dang/da da dang/byi lo'/de nas phyag rgya'i slob dpon chos spyod pa gtsang ba gos gtsang ma bgos te/thams cad za ba dang/thams cad spyod pa la yang/smad pa med de/zhi
25b7(rgyud): ba'i pho nya phyag rgyas zhugs la snying po zla bar bya'o/de nas dkyil 'khor gyi dbus nas su byo chen po thos par 'gyur ro/de nas daslob dpon ma rtogs pa gzhan thams ji ltar 'ongs pa bzhugs par 'gyur
25b8(rgyud): zhing/thos par 'gyur te/bdug pa'i dam tshig ni ma rtogs so/de nas slob ma me tog thogs pas/ras kyi mig bkab la/nag pa'i phyag rgya
26a1(rgyud): bcug ste/rna bar dam tshig chen po b+h+yo zhes bya ba dang sbyar ro//de nas sgrag nas me tog dor ro//de nas gang du lhung ba de lhag par bsgrub par bya'o//gal te lhung bar ma gyur na yang dor du gzhug go/
26a2(rgyud): de nas 'jig par byed nus shing /rnal 'byor yang so sor thob par 'gyur ro//de nas gdong g.yogs bkrol la/dkyil 'khor bstan par bya'o//bdug pa la sogs pas mchod nas snying po dang //
26a3(rgyud): rang gi mtshan mi spyan par bya'o//de nas dam tshig bstan par bya ste/sems can thams cad rgyas btab zhing /dam tshig sdang ba rnams kyang bza' bar bya//sngags rnams bstan par mi bya ste//
26a4(rgyud): dam tshig rnal 'byor 'grub ster ba'o//drag po'i mdung rtse dang ma mo rnams kyi rgyas 'debs pas/gal te 'das te khyod kyi snying rtag tu 'jig par byed par 'gyur//de nas brtsams te sha chen 
26a5(rgyud): po zan de ring po mi thogs par rnal 'byor 'grub par 'gyur ro//gang za bar mi 'gyur ba de'i dngos grub med do//gal te dam tshig las 'das par gyur pa de rnams kyis kyang mi sod par 
26a6(rgyud): 'gyur ro//phyag rgya'i ye shes bslab par bya ste/phyag rgya gar dang bcas pa byas nas/gtor ma byin zhing /bza' ba dang btung ba dang /long spyod la sogs pas tshim par byas nas/phyir 'byung bar 'gyur ro//
26a7(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad ces bya ba'i phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor ro//de nas sprang rtsi byed la sogs pa ming po gsum gyis bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/mgrin gcig tu rang gi snying po kho na'i snying po 'di phul lo 
26a8(rgyud): swa/de nas de'i dkyil 'khor bshad par bya ste/zur bzhi pa dang sgo bzhi par//phyi'i dkyil 'khor bri bar bya//de'i nang du dkyil 'khor ni//gzhu'i dbyibs can bri bar bya//de'i dbus su ming po ni//mchog 
26b1(rgyud): gsum dang ni gzhag par bya//sbrang rtsi dang ni byed dang rgyal byed dang//de bzhin don thams cad grub par byed ces bya'o//cho ga ya lad bgos pa dang//de bzhin thod pa mtshan ma 'dzin//lha mo'i gos dang 'phreng ba 'chang //
26b2(rgyud): cung zad rgod cing khro ba dang//seng ge'i khri la 'dug pa dang//'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyis mdzes pa'o//dngos grub kun ster dag pa ste//gsum ni srid gsum dag dang mtshungs//sgo dang 'tshams ni thams cad du
26b3(rgyud): phyogs la swogs pa legs par gzhag/sgrub dang 'phel chen gnod sbyin dang//gtum dang khro mchog gsod pa'o//phyi rol pa nang bse mo la//dril bu phreng bcas thams cad du//bum pa gang bcas ro mchog
26b4(rgyud): bcas//'byung po ma lus gtor mar byas//klu rnams glu ru le na pa dang//rol mo gdung bar byed pa dang//de bzhin slob dpon gar byed pas//phyag rgya kun gyis 'jug par bya'o//zhugs
26b5(rgyud): nas bsgrub la dad pa yis//yang nas yang du gdung bya zhing//me tog kun gyis mchod pa yis//bsgrub dang de nyid dag kyang brjod//'jig rten gsum po srid gsum mchog/ngan song gsum
26b6(rgyud): mchod gsum rnam gsum//legs ldan spas pas grub pa ni//dngos grub kun la rtag tu'o//de yi snying po lan cig bcing//phyag rgya thams cad kyi g.yas pa'i 'dzub mo lcags kyur byas la/dgug par
26b7(rgyud): bya'o//de nas dril bu dang bcas pa'i phreng ba rnams g.yo'o//thams cad phab nas de bzhin du gzhug par bya'o//de nas dam tshig brjod par bya'o//gal te dam tshig 'das par gyur na//pha gsod 'gro ba
26b8(rgyud): gang yin dang//ma gsod 'gro ba gang yin dang//'gro ba der ni 'gro bar 'gyur//rtag tu dam tshig pa bsten par bya//dam pa min pa yongasu spang//gzhan la de nyid bsten mi bya//rtag tu ming po gsu
27a1(rgyud): @#//pa bsgrub//'di ni dam tshig go/de nas glu dang gar dang/rol mo dang rgod pa dang/dga' ba dang/za ba dang/mgu bas mel tse bya'o/phyag rgya gar gyi rol mos mchod pa yang byas la/slob dpon 
27a2(rgyud): gyis kyang phyag rgya de dang bcas pas 'byung ba dang/da ltar byung bar 'gyur ba'i ye shes brjod par bya'o//de nas brtsams te dngos grub thams cad dang/phun sum tshogs pa thams cad dang/'byor pa 
27a3(rgyud): thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor lo//de nas sring mo bzhis bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/rang gi de kho na nyid kyi snying po 'di phul lo//hU~M/de nas 'di'i dkyil 
27a4(rgyud): 'khor bshad par bya ste/dkyil 'khor kun nas zlum po thig btab la/thig bzhi la 'khor lo'i dkyil 'khor bzhi byas la/dkyil 'khor de rnams su sgo bzhir lta ba'i lha mo'i gzugs brnyan 
27a5(rgyud): gzhag par bya ste/'di lta ste/dga' mo shar du lta ba/mda' dang gzhu thogs pa la sogs pa /sgeg mo mdog bsod ma/lho phyogs su lta ba lag na ral gri dang phub thogs pa mdog nag po'o//nag 
27a6(rgyud): mo nub du blta ba lag na gser gyi gter thogs pa gser gyi mdog can no//grub pa mo zhes bya ba byang du blta ba lag na gri dang shar ka thogs pa mdog nag po'o /phyi rol gi mtshan ma'i dkod pa gzhag par 
27a7(rgyud): bya'o//de nas slob dpon dril bu bkrol zhing/lag pa g.yon pa kha traM kha dang bcas pa 'jug par bya'o/zhugs nas snying po zlas par bya'o/de nas phyag rgya dang gar gi mchod pa stab par bya'o//de nas 
27a8(rgyud): yid la 'dod pa'i dngos grub thams cad ster bar 'gyur ro//thams cad kyis mchod par 'gyur ro//mkha' 'gro ma la sogs pa gdon thams cad kyis mi tshugs pa dang/'di'i nor thams cad kyang
27b1(rgyud): bskyed par 'gyur ro//'di ltar mngon par 'dod pa thams cad kyi nges par 'byed pa dang/gter thams cad 'byin pa dang/dngos grub thams cad bsgrub pa nus par 'gyur ro//slob ma gzhug ma yang de bzhin no
27b2(rgyud): de rnams dang 'di dag thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//las kyi dkyil 'khor ro//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las/phyi rol gyi rdo rje rigs kyi rtog pa rdzogs s+ho////
27b3(rgyud): ////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas mu med pas/mthar thug cing mu med pa'i chos dang ldan pas yang/cho ga 'di mthar thug par byin gyis brlab pa'i phyir/chos 
27b4(rgyud): thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi mthar thug pas byin gyis/brlab pa rdo rje zhes bya ba/shes kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul 'di bshad do//shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa mtha' yas pa nyid pas de bzhin gash+ya 
27b5(rgyud): pa mtha' yas pa nyid do//shes kyi pha rol du phyin pa mu med pa nyid pas/rdo rje thams cad mu med pa nyid do/shes rab kyi pha rol du pyin pa du ma nyid pas/chos thams cad du ma nyid do//shes rab kyi pha rol
27b6(rgyud): du phyin pa mthar thug pa med pas las thams cad mthar thug pa med pa nyid do/gang la la shes rab kyi pha rol du phyi na pa'i tshul 'di nyan pa dang/'chang ba dang/klog pa dang/ston pa dang/brjod pa dang/sgom 
27b7(rgyud): pa des sangs rgyas dang/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thub pa mthar phyin te/sgrib pa thams cad zad nas de bzhin gshegs pa 'am/rdo rje 'dzin par 'gyur ro/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 'dus 
27b8(rgyud): par 'gyur te/chos kyi rnam grangs 'di yod pa theg pa med par myur du 'grub par bya ba'i phyir/lag na rdo rje legs so zhes bya ba byin no/sems can chen po legs so legs so//blo gros chen po legaso legs
28a1(rgyud): @#//cho ga 'di ni legs par smras//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin kun gyis//'bras bu rdo rje 'dzin pa'i mchog//rdo rje mdo sde byin gyis rlab//cho ga'i rgyal po bla na med 
28a2(rgyud): gang rnams 'di dag 'dzin pa dang //bdud la sogs pa thams cad kyis//de dag mi sod mi tshugs so//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang //dngos grub dam pa thaMd dang //sangs rgyas 
28a3(rgyud): kun gyis gsungs pa bzhin//ring por mi thogs thob par 'gyur//de skad gsungs pa gsang grub phyir//rdo rje can gyis bshad pa la//kun mchog sangs rgyas mkhas bcas pa//thams cad kyis ni mngon 
28a4(rgyud): par bstod//bde ba chen po don yod pa'i dam tshig gi rtog pa'i rgyal po las/shes kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul rdzogs s+ho////rgya gar gyi mkhan po a tsa ru shra d+ha ka ra bar ma dang /zhu 
28a5(rgyud): chen gyi la tsha ba dge slong rin chen bzang pos bsgyur te gtan la bab po////de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba'i cho nyid rnyed pa chos thams cad spros 
28a6(rgyud): pa med pa/yang bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba rdo rje chos nyid shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i sgo dpal mchog dang po'i thog ma dang tha ma dbus med pa'i dam pa 'di gsungs so//byang chub sems dpa' 
28a7(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis/'dod chags chen po'i dngos grub dam pa ni/bde ba chen po'i dngos grub dam bar 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa'ar sems dpa' chen po'i dngos grub dmapa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa 
28a8(rgyud): thams cad kyi byang chub po'i dngos grub par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi byang chub chen po'i dngos grub dmag pa ni bdud chen po'i stobs thams cad rab tu 'joms pa'i dngos grub dam pa ni/khams 
28b1(rgyud): gsum thams cad kyi dbang phyug chen po'i dngos grub dam par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi khams gsum pa thams cad kyi dbang phyugi dngos grub dam pa ni/sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa
28b2(rgyud): med pa yongasu sbyong bar 'dod pa'i rgyu'i phyir/'khor ba la yang ma chags shing brtson 'grus bar grub pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyi sems can gyi khams ma lusashing lus pa med pa yongasu bskyab pa dang/seMs can
28b3(rgyud): thams cad la phan pa dang/bde ba mchog shin du bde ba chen po'i dngos grub dam par 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/ji srid 'khor ba'i gnas su ni//mkhas mchog 'dug par 'gyur ba'i tshe//de srid mtshungs
28b4(rgyud): med sems can don//mya ngan mi 'da' byed par nus te shes rab pha rol phyin thob dang//ye shes gnas su bsgrub pas na//chos kun rnam par dag pa'i phyir//'khor ba yongasu dag par 'gyur//
28b5(rgyud): chags pa laswogs 'dul na ni//'khor ba'i bar du rtag par yang//'jig rten sdig byed de sbyang phyir//'khor ba srid du bdag nyid 'dul//ji ltar pad ma ra dmar ba//mtsho'i nyes pas mi gos pas//
28b6(rgyud): de bzhin gnas pa'i nyes pa yis//'gro la phan rnams mi gos so//'dod chags chen po bar dag pa//bde ba chen po nor che la//khams gsum dbang phyug thob pa ni//sems can don yod brtan par
28b7(rgyud): byed//nang par langs nas dpal mchog dang po shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i tshul gyi sgo'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di nyan pa dang/'dzin pa dang/klog pa dang/bsgom pa de sems bde ba dang/yid
28b8(rgyud): bde ba thams cad thob par 'gyur ro//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig dngos grub kyi mchog kyang tshe 'di la thob par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa thams kyi gsang ba'i mchog kyang 'grub ste/rdo rje
29a1(rgyud): @#//'dzin pa chen po dam pa de bzhin gshegs par 'gyur ro//bde ba chen po rdo rje don yod pa'i dam tshig dpal mchog dang po las/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i
29a2(rgyud): shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i sgo rdzogs s+h+yo////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rdo rje sems dpa' byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje gsang ba mchog
29a3(rgyud): gi dngos grub cho ga rgyas pa'i rgyal po chen po de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje gsang ba'i don yod cing /brtan pa'i cho ga rgyas btab pa'i sku gsung thugs thams cad
29a4(rgyud): kyi rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad dam tshig chen po thams cad la rab tu zhugs nas/chos nyid rtogs pa'i ye shes la rab tu zhugs pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi sgo sna tshogs
29a5(rgyud): ba'i phrin las kyis dus gsum du 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus par spros pa lags kyi mtha' can de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad chen po zla ba phyed pa bzhin du mang du gnas pa'i rdo rje rin po che/rnams kyi 'od zer
29a6(rgyud): sna tshogs pa rnam par 'bar ba'i 'od gyis gsal ba/gser gyi dril bu btags pa/sna tshogs pa'i ba dan rlung gis skyod pa rnams kyis nye bar mdzes par byas pa/sna tshogs kyi me tog gi phreng ba dang/na bza' dang/
29a7(rgyud): me long dang/mu tig gi phreng ba rab tu 'phyangs pas mdzes shing brgyan cing/sna tshogs pa'i kha khyer la gnas pa mchog tu gyur pa'i bu mo rgod pa dang/sgeg pa dang/gar dang/glu yis rab tu brgyan pa dang/stod par
29a8(rgyud): byed pa bde ba chen po rdo rje sems dpa'i gnags pa gzhal yas khang pa la zhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang/thugs rdo rje byin gyis brlabs pa'i
29b1(rgyud): dam tshig sems dpa' thams cad dang/dbang skur ba thams cad dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ting nge 'dzin las thams mchog gyur pa/khaMs gsum thams cad 'dul bar mdzad pa /thams cad dbang phyug byed pa po
29b2(rgyud): 'dod pa thams cad pa/rgya che ba'i cho ga thams cad pa/rtag pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa la gsol ba btab pa/ji srid pa'i gnas pa/de srid sems can don mchog mdzod /sems can khams ni mtha'
29b3(rgyud): yas dang//rgyal srid srid pa 'dod pa'i phyir//srid pa'i skyon rnams 'joms phyir dang//bde ba chen po 'phel ba'i phyir//gsang ba'i rtog pa mchog gsungs pa'i rtog pa rjes su gnang ba bdag stsol /de nas
29b4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas chos //thams cad de bzhin gal lhag pa'i lta ba'i dgongs nas/gdungs bya thams cad thugs rje chen po'i sna tshogs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin 
29b5(rgyud): la snyoms par zhugs te/ma lus shing lus pa med par sems can gyi bsam pa thams cad yongasu rdzogs par byed pa dang thams cad yongasu skyangs pa dang/phan pa dang bde ba dang dngos grub thams cad phun
29b6(rgyud): sum tshogs pa dang /ye shes dang las dang mchog gi dngos grub kyi rgyur 'gyur ba'i bar du /de bzhin gshegs pa nyid dang/rdo rje 'chang chen po thob /mchog gi dngos grub kyi don bde ba chen po'i rdo rje dang po ste /de bzhin
29b7(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad 'khor lo bsgyur ba nyid /de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad 'khor lo sku'i phreng ba'i cod pan gyis dbang bskur bas dbang bskur ba yin no//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang chen po ma lus pa lus
29b8(rgyud): pa med pa'i bde ba chen po mchog gi dngos grub thams cad thob par bya ba'i phyir/sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrungs bcas te sku drang por skrang te /g.yon pa snyems pa'i tshul gyis mdzad nas/g.yas pa rdo rje gsor bas rang gi
30a1(rgyud): @#//thugs khar dang po'i rdo rje mchog gi sbyor ba 'dzin cing bde ba chen po'i rdo rje zhes bya ba thams cad kyi 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs dang rnams las byung ngo//o~M mahA su kha badzra swa/
30a2(rgyud): dza hU~M ba~M hoHsu ra ta sta~M/de nas phyung ba tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad byon nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi khu tshur bzung ba rnams slar yang byang chub chen po rtogs nas/de rnams
30a3(rgyud): kyis gzhad pa chen po mngon par mdzad do//'jig rten gyi khams cad g.yos pa dang bcas pas yongs dag par 'gyuro//khams gsum rgyas pa spros pa thams cad kyang bcom ldan 'das kyi dkyil 'khor chen por
30a4(rgyud): 'gyur ro//gang gis sems can thams cad bde ba dang yid bde ba thob par 'gyuro//de nas rdo rje 'chang chen pos de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi don yod cing thogs pa las mngon par byung ba/
30a5(rgyud): rdo rje chen po phyag rgya zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te bde ba chen po rdo rje'i dam tshig ces bya ba gnod par mdzad nas/de'i sku thams cad nas dngos grub thams cad dang /bde ba drang /yid
30a6(rgyud): bde ba ster ba rtag tu rjes su 'brel pa can lhan cig tu spyod pa bde ba chen po'i spyod pa'i dam tshig sems dpa' rnams phyung nas phyogs thams cad du byung ste/mdun du rdo rje gsang ba yid las byung ba g.yasu rdo rje
30a7(rgyud): ki ki ki li'i rgyab du 'dod pa'i rdo rje/g.yon du rdo rje sgyings pa'o//mtshams rnams su mchod pa'i lha mo rnams so//phyi rol gyi kha khyer 'tshamasu lha'i bu ma rnamaso//sgo srung ba rnams ni rdo rje lcags dkyu dang /zhags
30a8(rgyud): pa dang/lcags sgrog dang/dril bu 'dzin pa'o//phyi rol du rdo rje sgo srungs kyi tshogs dang/pho nya dang bran rnamaso//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi khu tshur bcing ste
30b1(rgyud): de gzhin gshegs pa thams cad byang chub rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' rdo rje 'chang chen po la gsol ba btag te/sems can kun la bde don du//rgyal ba chen
30b2(rgyud): po 'byung ba ste//chags bral de rnams chags ldan pa//rang gi btsun mo'ang gsol mdzad pas//de phyir bdag gis khyod la gsol//bde ba chen po rdo rje che//gsang ba'i rtog pa'i dngos grub ste//'dul ba nyid ni
30b3(rgyud): bshad du gsol//de nas bcom ldan 'das don yod cing thogs pa med pa'i bka' bden pa thams cad kyi dngos grub 'dul ba rdo rje 'dod pa'i mchog ces bya ba'i rnam par gnon par mdzad nas/rdo rje gsang ba chen
30b4(rgyud): po'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po 'di gsungs so//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba//dkyil 'khor dam pa chen po ste//bde ba chen po rdo rje mchog//dmi tshig dam pa'i ming can yin//dkyil
30b5(rgyud): 'khor gnas ni 'dul byas nas//de dbus cho ga bzhin du ni//phyag rgya chen po mnyam gzhag nas//der ni dkyil 'khor sgrub par ba//gser zhing legs par sgril ba dang //'dud pa dag ni med pa yi//
30b6(rgyud): thig skud dmar pos thig btab nas//tshon gyi phye mas bri bar bya//zur bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//sgo khyud bzhis ni mdzes pa dang //kha khyer gyis ni yongasu bskor//mtshams su lta'i bu mos mdzes//dkyil 'khor dag
30b7(rgyud): gi mtshams rnams dang //sgo dang sgo khyud 'tshams rnams su//zla ba phyed la gnas pa yi//rdo rje rin chen 'od zer 'bar//rlung gis skyod pa'i dril bu mchog//ba dan sna tshogs mches pa yin//me tog dra ba me tog phreng
30b8(rgyud): ldan pa'i phyi rol dkyil 'khor bri//de'i nang du 'khor lo ni//dkyil 'khor rgyad pa dang 'dra ba//rdo rje thig gis yongs skor ba'i//rdo rje 'khor lo yang dag bri//de dbus cho ga bzhin du ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dgod
31a1(rgyud): @#//par bya;zlugs sam yang na byas pa 'am//gdungs pa'am yang na bris pa yi//bzhad pa dang bcas zla ba'i 'od//'bar ba'i 'od zer dmar pos khyab//sangs rgyas phreng ba rnam par mdzes //
31a2(rgyud): sku la tshon gyis rnam par byugs//sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrungs gis bzhugs//rdo rje snyems pa 'dzin pa'i mchog//sa ma ya stom zhes brjod nas//snying khar rnam par dgod par bya//de'i logs ni thaMd du
31a3(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po rnams bris te//tshon nam yang na bdul tshon gyis//go rims ji bzhin bri bar bya//mdun du sku mdog dmar po ni//rdo rje tshon che lasogs pa//g.yas pa dag pa'i dkar po ste//legs par 'khyud
31a4(rgyud): ba'i rdo rje can//rgyab du sku mdog ser po ste//chu srin gyis ni rgyal mtshan 'dzin//g.yon du sku mdog sngon po ste//phyag gnyis snyoms pa 'dzin lasogs//mchod pa'i snod rnams 'dzin pa'o//
31a5(rgyud): mchod pa'i lha mo bri bar bya//sgyeg mo gzhad ma glu ma dang//gar ma lha'i bu mo'i tshogs//rdo rje lcags dkyu zhags dang//rdo rje lcags sgrog dril bu rnams //sgo ni bzhi po rnams la ni//sgo srungs bzhi dag
31a6(rgyud): gi snying po 'di yin te//hU~M badzra tiSh+ThA sa ya ki phaT/hU~M badzra ki li ki li hU~M/hU~M sma ra sma ra badzra b+han d+ha hU~M/hU~M hU~M Sha badzra ni hU~M/rdo rje bran no////de nas mchod pa'i lha mo rnams kyis ni////hU~M ma d+hu ru badzra hU~M/hU~M badzre
31a7(rgyud): mu khe hU~M/hU~M shard badzra hU~M/hU~M hUM badzra she ri hU~M/de nas lha mo rnams kyis ni/hU~M badzra sa le ma hA ba ra badzra ni/oM badzra gi ti/oM ni ta badzra/de nas sgo srungs rnams kyis ni/badzra a gu shi badzra pra ti ha ra /
31a8(rgyud): hU~M badzra pa sha ga na/badzra s+pho ta du ta/a badzra gan d+ha tsi te/de ltar lha mo'i dkyil 'khor du grags pa de nyid ni/bkod na de nyid rnams kyis ni//byin rlabs pa ni nyid du 'gyur//de nas phyag na 
31b1(rgyud): rdo rje 'dzin//dkyil 'khor kun mchog byas nas su//bum pa gang dag ji snyed pa//me tog la sogs kun gyis mchod//dril bu btags pa'i ba dan rnams//mtshams rnams su yang gzhag par bya//lcug ma dang 
31b2(rgyud): bcas bum pa rnams//sgo'i phyi rol dag tu gzhag//rdo rje dang bcas bzhin dang bcas//lcug ma yongasu zlas nas ni//dri chus gang ba'i bum pa ste//phyag na rdo rje'i mdun gzhag bya//de la 'di ltar bde ba chen po'i 
31b3(rgyud): dam tshig gi bdud rtsi'i chu byin gyis rlabs pa'i snying po ni//o~M mahA su pa badzra sa ma ya/a bi shi ka/dza hU~M ba~M hA+oH/lan nyi shu rtsa gcig zlas so//chu 'di rtor ba 'am 'thungs na sdug bsngal thams cad byang ba 'am/bde 
31b4(rgyud): ba thams cad rab tu 'phel bar 'gyur ba yin no//de nas rang gi dam tshig rnams kyis/rdo rje 'chang la sogs pa sgrub par bya'o//dza hU~M ba~M hoH/zhes pas pa dang/gzhug pa dang/bcings nas/skad cig 
31b5(rgyud): dbang du 'gyur/phyag rgya chen po mnyam gzhag ste//de bzhin g.yas 'dzub lcags dkyur bcas//dgug pa 'di na lan cig gis//'grub te rab tu bzhug par bya//o~M mahA kha badzra swAhA/
31b6(rgyud): a a hi shi gri ma kha su badzra gu h+ya sa ma ya/ma nu sma ma ra bra ba d+ha sa ma ya sta~M/d+h+ri d+h+ri pa su to sh+yo anu rag to/su po sho me b+ha wa a ti tiSh+Tha ba ga wan/a na ti ni da ni sid+d+h+ya me ma tra pa te/dza hU~M ba~M hoH/de nas
31b7(rgyud): rtag tu bcom ldan 'das/dkyil 'khor du ni rang nyid byon//dgug cing bcug na brtan pa dang//dbang du 'gyur zhing 'grub pa yin//rmad du byung ba'i rdzu 'phrul du ma rnams mthong ba 'am/yang na bcom
31b8(rgyud): ldan 'das rang nyid kyis mthong bar 'gyur/de nas rang nyid rje 'dzin pa kun gyi slob dpon ni/phyag rgya thams cad grub pa/dam tshig thams cad shes pa/las thams cad nus pa/bcom ldan 'das kyis byin 
32a1(rgyud): @#//gyis rlabs pa yin no//'jug pa'i cho ga 'di yis rdo rje slob dpon rang nyid ji snyed pa'i dris byugs shing sgoms pas /rgyan dang cha lugs dang bcas pa'i me tog gi phreng bas lus shin tu rgyan pa /gos
32a2(rgyud): dmar po gnyis bgos pa/rang nyid kyis bcom ldan 'das la stod cing rdo rje dang bcas pa'i rdo rje snyems bas rang nyid 'jug par bya'o//stod pa ni drug ldan la sogs pa'i glu yis bya'o//'dod chags la sogs bde kun
32a3(rgyud): yid khyod ni//kun sdus bdag la bde ba gsol mdzod cig//rdo rje sems dgongs myur du bdag ston mdzad//bdag dgos mdzad dang bde mchog mdzad la 'dud//de nas rab tu zhugs nas dgongs su gsol bar bya
32a4(rgyud): ste|thog mtha' med pa'i sems dpa' ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba che//kun du bzang po khyod yin te//rdo rje bsnyems bdag khyab bdag yin//sems kyi sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin//de bzhin rdo rje rdo rje 'dzin
32a5(rgyud): kun mchog dam tshig dngos grub can//rdo rje sems bdag deng 'grub mdzod//de nas bcom ldan 'das de nyid kyis rdo rje slob dpon byin rlabs gyur/snga ma byung ba'i so so rang rig pa'i bde ba gnyi 
32a6(rgyud): ga la skye bar 'gyur ro//phra mo dbab pa yang 'gyur ro//de nas bdag la bcom ldan 'das 'grub par 'gyur ro//de nas de nyid du 'dug la ci 'dod pa'i zlas brjod bya'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa 
32a7(rgyud): du 'dug la rang gi bsod nams kyis byin gyis rlabs kyis 'dug pa'i dngos grub thob par 'gyur ro//de nasartsams te sems dpa' chen po thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//de rnams kyis kyang de la dngos grub thams
32a8(rgyud): cad stsol bar mdzad do//phyag rgya thams cad kyang bdag rang nyid kyis bcings na las thams cad 'grub par 'gyu ro//dam tshig thams cad kyang 'di la bstan par 'gyur ro//yid kyis 'dod pa'i las rnams
32b1(rgyud): kyang byed pa yin no//bde ba dang yid bde ba thams cad nyams su myong zhing gzhan gyis mi thub pa dang/mi gsod pas lo cig 'das nas/sangs rgyas nyid la sogs pa'i mchog gi dngos grub thob pa yin no//
32b2(rgyud): de nas sems can thams cad gzhug pa'i don rtag par bya ba yin no//rdo rje slob dpon rang nyid snga ma bzhin du lus rgyan nas/rab tu bde ba'i rdo rje'i dam tshig phyag rgya rab tu bcug te/de bzhin du de kho na 
32b3(rgyud): nyid chen po bskul bas /yang dag par bskul bas dgug par bya ste/'di skad ces brjod do//srid pa'i bde ba rnam dag phyir//sngar ni srid pa la khyod dga'//'dod pa kun gyi dngos grub che//'gro
32b4(rgyud): ba kun la'ang 'grub par bgyi//de nas mchod pa byas la /rang gi lhag pa'i lha gang yin pa de'i/dam tshig phyag rgya me tog dang bcas pa gzhug par bya ste/bcug nas rna bar/'di 
32b5(rgyud): skad ces brjod par bya'o//chags bral 'dul ba'i 'jig rten ni//chags bral ba 'di nyid kyis 'dul bar 'gyur ro//'dod chags chen po bde ba chen//rdo rje sems dpa' sbas pa 
32b6(rgyud): yin//'dod chags bral ba lta bu yi//sdug bsngal gzhan ni rang bzhin med//de bas sems can chags bral ba//sngar yang yid ni ma byed cig//de nas me tog la sogs pa/snying po   
32b7(rgyud): 'dis 'dor du gzhug go //oM badzra kha bu kha badzra sA tA pra ti sta~M//de nas gang du babs pa de ni de'i lha yin no//de nas de kho na nyid chen po brjod par bya'o//gang 'dod pa'i snying po    
32b8(rgyud): byung la sgrub du bcug la //rang nyid lhag pa'i lha'i dbang bskur nas//de'i mtshan ma lag pa gnyis su byin te /de'i rigs kyi ming gis /dbang bskur ba snying po 'dis dbang bskur bar 
33a1(rgyud): @#//bya'o//oM sarba ad ma mahA badzra d+ha ra sa mu kho b+ha wa a shad taM/a b+hi Shiny+tsa mi/g+h+ri ha na sid ti badzra he/o mo ka sid ta sta~M////de nas phyag rgya bcing ba byin gyis     
33a2(rgyud): brlabs par bya'o//de nas ci 'dod pa'i mchod pa byas shing /byed du bcug nas/ci 'dod pa za ba dang /btung bas slob ma rnams tshim par byas la /phyag rgya dang /phyag rgya'i lan dang /
33a3(rgyud): gar dang /rgod pa byed du bcug te /rang nyid kyis kyang bya'o//de nas thams cad skrad nas/brjod par bya ste/thog mthar rgya'i 'gro ba 'di dga' ba mchog la dag lus te//sems dpa' mchog la
33a4(rgyud): rdo rjer 'gyur//slar yang gshegs par mdzad du gsol////de nas snyim pa'i me tog dor nas/phyung bar bya'o//dkyil 'khor yang rang nyid kyis byugs la /gzhag na gzhan gyi 
33a5(rgyud): las thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//de nas bde ba chen po rdo rje dam tshig gi bdud rtsi'i tshul de yang bcug na tshe 'phel ba la sogs pa'i/las thams cad la dgongs
33a6(rgyud): pa yin no//de nyid kyis dbang bskur ba yang /dkyil 'khor du dbang bskur ba thob pa dang /mchog gi dngos grub kyang thob par 'gyur ro//gal te sku bri ba'i ri mo mkhan rang rang gi phyag 'tshan
33a7(rgyud): gyi phyag rgya rnams kyis/dkyil 'khor bri bar bya'o//gal te de yang mi shes na/phyag mtshan gzhag par bya'o//phyag rgya'i cho ga rgyas pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das don yod pa thogs 
33a8(rgyud): pa med pa myur du 'grub pa'i cho ga rgya che ba'i dam tshig rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/rnam par gnang ba mdzad nas /rdo rje 'chang chen pos 'di gsungs so////
33b1(rgyud): bde ba chen pos rab sgrubs dang//'dod pa chen pos rnam dag phyir//rdo rje sems dpa' grub pa ni//brtag par myur du bshad par bya//ji srid 'khor ba'i gnas su ni//de srid sems can gdon   
33b2(rgyud): par bya//mya ngan 'das pa byang chub sgrub//de nyid kyis ni byang chub rnams//ji srid srid pa la gnas pa//de srid sems can don nyid byed//thams cad dag pa'i cho ga can//ji ltar sems
33b3(rgyud): can kun gyi don//dngos grub thams cad rab sgrub pa//de ltar sbyin sogs pha rol phyin//byang chub spyod pa mchog gi ming//de bzhin gshegs ni gdul min la//sems 
33b4(rgyud): can rnams kyi don mi mdzad//'di na dngos grub rab sgrub na //dus kun tu ni thams cad mdzad//de nas sgrub 'di bshad bya ste//dngos grub shin tu brtag pa yin //gang 
33b5(rgyud): gis srid pa 'di nyid du //shin tu brtag pa'i dngos grub thob//dkyil 'khor dag tu ma zhugs kyang//dam tshig rnampar spangs pa 'am//bsod nams med kyang thams cad du //
33b6(rgyud): srid pa 'di la myur du grub//gal te dngos grub sgrub 'dod na//brtag tu yul dang ldan gyur na//gsang ba'i mchod pas mchod nas ni//rang rang phyag rgyas bsgrub par 'gyur //drug ldan
33b7(rgyud): la sogs bsnyan pa'i glu//chags pa kun kyang phyi 'dod par//bzlas pa'i don ni ci nus pas//rdo rje sems dpa' grub par bya//'di yis 'dod kun las dbang phyug//ci 'dod 'gro ba'i 
33b8(rgyud): mkha' la rgyu//dngos grub kun gyi dbang phyug grub//rga shi med cing gtso bor 'gyur//rgyal ba kun gyi mchod byas la//rang gnyen kun gyis bsnyen bkur zhing//rdo rje 'chang chen dang 'tshungs
34a1(rgyud): @#//shing /don yod kun mchog spyod pa yin//bde ba chen po rdo rje grub pa'i rgyal po de bzhin gshegs pa la sogs pa'i dngos grub thams cad rab tu ster ba rdo rje 'chang chen po byang chub sems dpa' 
34a2(rgyud): yin no//gal te de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchog gi dngos grub mngon par 'dod na de bzhin gshegs pa des/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi phyag rgya'i khu tshur yongs su
34a3(rgyud): 'dzin pas ras ros la bri bar bya'o//bzhad pa dang bcas pa dngos grub thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis yongs su bskor bas/de'i mdun du de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad kyi khu tshur yongs su 'dzin
34a4(rgyud): pas rdo rje'i tshig stong phrag brgya bzlas par bya'o//'bum rdzogs pa na mtshan phyed bdud kyi tshogs rnams 'ong bar 'gyur ro//de nas bdud thams cad kyi stobs gzhom par bcom par bya'o//de nas tho rangs
34a5(rgyud): kyi dus su de bzhin gshegs pa thams
34a6(rgyud): cad nyid dam/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad nyid thob par 'gyur ro//gal te rdo rje 'chang chen po nyid mngon par 'dod na/des zlugs ma 'am/gdungs ma
34a7(rgyud): 'am/ras ris la sogs pa bris pa'i phyag na rdo rje'i sku'i dkyil 'khor bzhin du sngar sgrub pa la/sku gzugs kyi khyad par ji ltar thob pa ltar rdo rje sems dpa' sgrub par bya'o//zla ba drug gis rdo rje 'chang chen po 
34a8(rgyud): dngos grub chen po 'grub po//gal te rdo rje 'chang che po dgug pa na/bde ba chen po'i rdo rje lcags dkyu dgug cing /ci 'dod pa sgrub cang par bya'o//zla ba gcig 'das nas thams cad gsol bar bya'o//gal te 
34a9(rgyud): rang gi phyag rgyas bdag nyid sgrub na/rang gi lha'i phyag chen bcings te/bsgoms pas bdag rdo rje sems dpar yin no//zhes bsgoms la grub pa yin no//de ji ltar mngon par 'dod pa'i zlas pas bdag nyid sgrub par bya'o
34b1(rgyud): yang dang yang du sgom zhing/zlas pa de srid bdag gis bya'o//ji srid rdo rje 'chang nyid zla ba phyed 'das nas thob par 'gyur ro//de nas 'dod pa thams cad kyi dbang phyug bsgrub par 'dod na /de bzhin du rang yid du 
34b2(rgyud): 'ong ba dang bcas par rab tu zhugs te/phyag rgya bcings la de srid zlas par bya'o//ji srid bdag 'dod pa kun gyi dbang phyug dang/khams gsum ma lus pa'i bdag por 'gyur ro//de nas bcom 'das rdo 
34b3(rgyud): rje 'chang khams gsum ma lus pa'i 'dod pa'i don yod pa'i rgyun rang bzhin thams cad grub pa'i chos nyid kyi ye shes rnam par rol par mdzad nas/ma sgrubs pa'i phyag phyag rgya'i las kyi rgyun rdo rje cho ga rgyas pa
34b4(rgyud): 'di gsungs te/ma sgrubs pa'i snying po'i las thams cad 'di lta ste/bdag nyid srung ba'i bde ba chen po'i rdo rje gsang ba'i rtsug tor gyis so//thams cad srung ba la ni ba chen po rdo rje gsang
34b5(rgyud): ba'i mdzad gsungs so//phyogs thams cad dang mtshams dang dkyil 'khor bcing bar bya'o//de la ni bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i gtsug tor gyis so//bdud thams cad 
34b6(rgyud): kyi stobs gzhom pa la ni /bde ba chen po'i gsang ba rdo rje mi skyod pa'i tshe 'phel bya ba la ni /bde ba chen po rdo rje gsangs ba'i bdud rtsis so//gzugs chen po phun sum tshogs pa ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje    
34b7(rgyud): gsang ba'i gzugs kyis so//gzi brjid dang /na tshod shin tu bzang po rab tu 'phel ba la ni/bde ba chen po rdo rjes gsang ba'i bcud kyis len gyis so//stobs dang mdog dang 'phel ba la ni /bde 
34b8(rgyud): ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba' mthu yin pas so//dbang bskur ba thams cad thob pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i cod pan gyis so//nor thams cad thob pa la ni rdo rje gsang ba'i dbyibs so//rgyal srid tho ba 
35a1(rgyud): @#//pa la ni rdo rje gsang ba'i rgyal po'i phyag rgyas so//sa ma lus pa thob la ni gsang ba'i stsal gyis so//thams cad dbab pa dang/g.yo bar bya ba dang/gar byed du gzhud pa dang/dus gsum    
35a2(rgyud): bden pa nyid du smra bar bzhug pa la ni /bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i dam tshig gis ston du bzhug pas so//pra sen thams cad sgrub pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i pra sen dbab pas so//gdon thams cad 
35a3(rgyud): las thar ba bzhin la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i gdon las thar bas so//gdug pa thams cad gzhug pa la ni/rdo rje gsang pa'i bgegs 'joms pas so//thams cad rangs par bya ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i
35a4(rgyud): rengs pas so//thams cad gnyid log par bya bar la ni /rdo rje gsang ba'i gnyid kyis so//thams cad rgyas btab pa la bde ba chen po rdo rje gsad ba'i dam tshig gi mkha'agro mas so//
35a5(rgyud): thams cad du gsang ba la ni rdo rje gsang ba'i mthar byed kyis so//thams cad sgyel ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba' hU~M mdzad kyis sothams cad slang ba la ni/rdo rje gsang pa'i dam tshig gi(sa) so 
35a6(rgyud): slangs pa'o//thams cad bcing ba la ni/rdo rje gsang (ba)'i bcings pas so//thams cad thar bar bya ba la ni/rdo rje gsad ba'i yige mus so//pra men ma thams cad las bya ba dang/
35a7(rgyud): sngon gyi gnas thams cad rjes su sbyin pa la gsang ba chen po he ru ka'i rdo rjes so//ma mo'i gdon thams cad las thar bar bya ba la ni//rdo rje gsang ba'i drag mos so//pra men ma thams cad kyis 
35a8(rgyud): rgyas btab par bya ba las thar bya ba dang/snying zos pa la snying gzhan 'byur bar 'jug pa dang/shi ba bso ba la dam chen pos gsang ba'i he ka'i rdo rjes dbang bskur bas so//gdug pa thams cad 
35b1(rgyud): kyi phyag rgya gshig pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i phyag rgya rdo rje bas so//rims thams cad rab tu zhi ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i 'bar bas lta bas so//gdug pa thams cad gzhig pa dang/bza' ba dang 
35b2(rgyud): dbab pa dang /'chi ba bso ba la ni/gsang ba chen po rdo rje nor bu'i phyag rgyas so//dug thams cad dang/sbyar ba'i dug thams cad bdud rtsir bya ba la ni/rdo rje gsang pa'i dug med pas so//
35b3(rgyud): gshed byed thams cad dang /byad dang ;ro langs dang/byur dang/gnod sbyin mo rnams kyi gzhig pa la ni/rdo rje gsang pa'i sbyin sred gis so//brtsod pa thams cad dang/'thab pa dang/'thab 
35b4(rgyud): pa dang/'thab mo dang /brtsod pa gzhig pa la ni/zhal rab tu gdangs pa dang/gis so//lams dang 'tshe ba thams cad dang/sdig pa dang /rmi lam ngan pa gzhig pa la ni/rdo rje   
35b5(rgyud): gsang ba'i zhi bas so//gzhan gyi dpung thams cad rengs pa dang/gnyid log par bya ba dang/rmongs par bya ba dang lkugs par byed pa dang/rgyas btab pa dang/gsang ba la
35b6(rgyud): ni;bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i gshed byed kyis so//'thab mo thams cad las ni rgyal ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi dpung rgyan gyis so//gdug pa thams cad gzhig pa la ni 
35b7(rgyud): rdo rje gsang ba'i rdo rje legs pas so//bgegs thams cad zhi bar byed pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i thabs sbyor gyis so//dgra bo thams cad rnam par gzhig pa la ni/rdo rje gsang pa'i rnam 
35b8(rgyud): par 'thor bas so//bdud thams cad gzhom pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba' rab tu 'dul bas so//sprin bskyed pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ma'i sprin chen pos so//char dbab pa la ni/rdo rje gsang   
36a1(rgyud): @#//ba'i sdig 'dzub kyis so//thog dbab pa dang /klog dang/rlung dang/char zhi bar bya ba la/ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i sprin srung gis so//sprin dang/mun pa dang/khug rna zhi bar bya ba la 
36a2(rgyud): ni rdo rje gsang ba'i bde ba chen po'i sprin gyis so//klu thams cad dgug pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i nor bu'i phyag rgyas so//klu thams cad rjes su 'brang bar bya ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i klu tha 'dul 
36a3(rgyud): bas so//klu thams cad 'byin pa la ni/rdo rje gsang pa'i 'don pas so//sa ma lus pa g.yo zhing 'gul ba la ni/rdo rje ba'i gsang ba'i thar pas so//chu med par chu'i phung po chu cher bsil zhing dri 
36a4(rgyud): ma med par bskyed pa la ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje klu'i phyag rgyas so//mun par nyi ma'i snang ba'i bskyed pa la ni/rdo rje nyi ma'i phyag rgyas so//rlung thams cad dbang du bya ba la ni/rdo rje rlung thams cad
36a5(rgyud): kyis so//namakha' la gzugs thams cad kun tu ston pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba 'dzin pas so//namakha' las dngos thams cad 'byung bar ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba' 'byung bas so 
36a6(rgyud): namakha' la bdag nyid kyis dngos po kun tu bstan par bya ba la ni/rdo rje gsad ba'i rdzu 'phrul gyis so//namakha' la de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang /
36a7(rgyud): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ston pa la ni/rdo rje ga sang ba'i rdzo rje gsang ba'i rdzu 'phrul gyis so//namakha' la de bzhin gsheg pa rdo rje 'dzin pa la sogs pa byang chub sesam dpa' thams cad dang
36a8(rgyud): 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad bstan pa la ni/rdo rje gsad pa'i rnam par 'phrul pas so//namakha' la shing thams cad la me tog dang /'bras bu thams cad 'byung ba la ni/rdo rje dus kyi phyag rgyaso
36b1(rgyud): sa'i cha thams cad la 'bras bu'i cha thams cad smin par 'byung ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i gshol gyi phyag rgyas so//thams cad la lha'i kha zas dang/btung ba dang/gos dang/rgyan dang/gnas 
36b2(rgyud): sar dang/bu mo gzhon nu byung ba dang/skyes pa dang/bud med dang/bran po dang/bran mo thams cad 'byung bar bya ba la ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje dam tshig gi bsod nams kyi chu bos so//g.yul du dpung 
36b3(rgyud): gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa g.yul 'gyed pa la ni/rdo rje bde ba chen po'i sdom pas so//sgyu ma thams cad bstan pa la ni/rdo rje bde ba mchog gis so//bde mchog gi phyag rgya thams cad la ni rdo rje sgyu 
36b4(rgyud): ma'i bde bas so//rmad du 'byung ba dang/cho 'phrul thams cad ston pa la ni/rdo rje rmad du byung ba'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pas so//lan thams cad gdab par bya ba la ni/rdo rje lan bstan paso//
36b5(rgyud): me rengs par bya ba la ni rdo rje shin tu grang bas so//chu 'bab par bya ba la ni rdo rje zla ba'i chu shel gyis so//dgon par grong dang gnas dang/chu bo dang/rdzing dang/bskyed mos tshal dang/zas dang
36b6(rgyud): skom dang/rtsug lag khang bsrung ba la ni rdo rje sgrub pas so//chu'i steng du bcag pa cadang /bdag nyid gzugs mang po'i dngos po ston pa dang mi snang ba dang /namakha' la 'gro ba'i dngos grub la rdo rje gsang 
36b7(rgyud): ba'i dngos grub kyi phyag rgyas so//lta sogs pa thams cad mngag gzhug par bya ba la ni/rdo rje'i gsang ba'i bka' sgo bas so//sems can thams cad dbang du bya ba la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i dbang gis so
36b8(rgyud): mi rgyu ba thams cad drang ba dang/dbab par dang/bden par smra ba gzhug pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i ngos grub chen pos so//gzugs rnyan thams cad dbab pa dang/dgug pa dang/rgod par bya ba dang /gar
37a1(rgyud): @#//byed du gzhug pa dang/rol par bya ba dang/sgo ba thams cad byed du gzhug pa dang/thams cad byer de 'ong ba dang/las thams cad byed bzhug pa la ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje dam tshig gi sgrub paso 
37a2(rgyud): thams cad rjes su chags par bar bya ba la ni/bde ba chen po  rdo rje yid las byung bas so//rgyu dang mi rgyu ba thams cad rtser bzhugs la ni/rdo rje ki li ki li gsang bas so//thams cad rten par bya ba la ni/rdo rje chu 
37a3(rgyud): sprin gyi phyag rgyas so//thams cad 'da' bya ba la ni/rdo rje snyems pas so//rmi lam gdab pa ni rdo rje ngag gis so//gzhan dag gis rang 'dod pa'i rmi lam mthong bya bar la ni/rdo rje rmi lam mthong bas so//
37a4(rgyud): rmi lam ngan pa thams cad gzhig pa la ni/rdo rje sreg lugs kyis so//rmi lam bzang ba thams cad sgrub pa rtan par bya ba la ni/rdo  rje rmi lam sgrubs paso//nad dang gnod pa thams cad bsal ba la ni/
37a5(rgyud): rdo rje sman gyis so//rims nad thams cad bzhig pa la ni/rdo rje bcud len gyis so//dus ma yin par 'chi ba bzhig pa la ni rdo rje dur bas so//dmyal ba lasogs pa'i ngan 'gro thams cad
37a6(rgyud): gzhig pa la ni rdo rje sdig pa bral bas so//sku thams cad grub pa la ni/rdo rje sku'i mchog gis so//gsung thams cad grub pa la ni /rang gi lces 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung ba'i dngos po thaMd
37a7(rgyud): bden par ston pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i ljags kyi mchog gis so//thugs thams cad grub pa la ni/rdo rje thugs bskyed p+sa'o//rdo rje thams cad kyi dngos grub ni rab tu dga' ba'i rdo rjes so//dkyil 'khor
37a8(rgyud): thams cad rab tu 'jug pa la ni/rdo rje 'khor los so//phyag rgyas thams cad bcings pas dngos grub la ni/rdo rje gsang ba'i dam tshig gi phyag rgya chen pos so//dam tshig thaMd sgrub pa la ni rdo rje daM tshig gi
37b1(rgyud): phyag rgyas so//las thams cad grub pa la ni rdo rje gsang ba'i mchod pas so//dngos grub thams cad la ni rdo rje gsang ba'i bde ba chen po grub pas so//phun sum tshogs pa thams cad la ni rdo rje ltung ba
37b2(rgyud): las gdon pa'i phyag rgyas so//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la ni/rdo rje ye shes kyi phyag rgyas so//thams can kyi mchod pas bya ba la ni rdo rje gar dbang gis so//rdo rje thams cad kyi dngos grub dam pa la ni rdo rje bde
37b3(rgyud): ba chen po'i rdo rjes so//sems dpa' chen po'i thams kyi dngos grub dam pa la na/rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba chen pos so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dngos grub ba dam pa la ni/rdo rje gsang ba mchog gi
37b4(rgyud): bde ba chen pos so//rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad kyi dngos gru ba dam pa la bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i dam tshig gi phyag rgyas so//bde ba chen po'i rdo rje gsang ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las rab tu dga'
37b5(rgyud): ba'i rdo rje gsang ba'i mchod pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das bde ba chen po rdo rje'i dam tshig gi sems dpa'i byin rlabs pa gsang ba'i chos can rnam par snang mdzad chen po de bzhin gshegs
37b6(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi khu tshur yongs su 'dzin pas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'khor los byin gyis rlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//rang gi rdo rje sems dpa' la gsol ba btabo
37b7(rgyud): bde ba chen po rab tu sgrub dang //'dod chags chen po rnam dag phyir//de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas nyid//dam pa'i chos ni bskyed du gsol//de bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang chen pos de bzhin gshegs pa
37b8(rgyud): thams cad kyi dam tshig bde ba chen po mi byed pa'i rnam par gnod pa mdzad nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi sku dang /gsung dang /thugs rje thams cad la rab tu zhugs nas/thams cad kyi bdag nyid mnyam par spros nas
38a1(rgyud): bde ba chen po'i phyag rgya gzungs 'di gsungs so//a d+hi/de nas 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//rnam pa drug tu g.yos 'gyur te//sangs rgyas sras dang bcas pa kun//kun nas 'dus shing bstan par 'gyur//
38a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor chen po yis//thams cad grub phyir kun nas bzhugs//bzhad pa chen po khyer bar gyis//rmad 'byung chen po rab tu bzhad//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa 
38a3(rgyud): rdo rje snyems pas zhugs//bde ba chen po dam tshig gsang ba zhes bya ba'i gsungs kyi dkyil 'khor 'di gsungs so//ji ltar chos kyis bris nas ni//rgyal ba'i g.yon du snyems 'dzin pa//dam tshig gi mchog gi mchog 
38a4(rgyud): bkod nas//sa ma yA staM zhes brjod nas//de bzhin gshegs pa sras dang bcas//rdo rje mtshon cha la sogs bzung //byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po//me tog la sogs phyir thal sbyar//de 
38a5(rgyud): la sogs pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'i khyad par ni 'di yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi phyag rgya dang rdo rje'i glu 'dis 'jug par bya ba'i/rdo rje sems bsdus pa'i yis//rdo rje rin chen bla na med//rdo rje chos ni 
38a6(rgyud): glur blangs pas//rdo rje las ni byed par 'gyur//de nas rab tu zhugs nas//de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi ye shes kyi gzungs bde ba chen po lcags kyus dgug par bya'o//oM badzra d+ha re su ra ma ha su khe sarba d+ha rna sa ma 
38a7(rgyud): ta ya/sarba ta thA gA ta na ye shes mA+ai mu ta pa d+hai dza hU~M baM ho/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad 'dzin pa dang /bcas pa yang dag par 'byon par 'gyur ro//yid bde ba la sogs pa'i mtshan ma 'byung ngo //cho 
38a8(rgyud): 'phrul du ma dag mthong ba yin no//de nas cho ga rgyas pa dang /sgrub pa dang /yid kyis 'dod pa'i dngos grub thams cad dang /don yod pa'i thogs pa med pa myur du dam pa grub pa yin te/de bzhin du'o//
38b1(rgyud): de nas ma grub par lan cig bzlas pas las rnams byed par 'gyur ro//thams cad bsrung ba la ni rdo rje gtsug gtor gyis so//sems can thams cad da bang du bya ba la ni rdo rje brjod 'dus kyis so//chos nyid thams cad
38b2(rgyud): thob pa la ni rdo rje chos nyid kyis so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis mchod pa'i dngos grub ni rdo rje mchod pas so//bdug pa thams cad 'dul ba la ni/rdo rje khri bas so//byang chub kyi spyod lam lus par rdzogs par bya ba la
38b3(rgyud): ni/rdo rje spyod pa'i phyag rgyas so//bdud thams cad gzhom pa la ni bdud 'joms par byed pas so//de bzhin gshegs thams cad nyid du bya ba la ni/rdo rje ye shes kyis khu tshur gyis so//bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba las
38b4(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa las thams cad kyi gsang ba chen po las chog rgyas pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i don yod cing/thogs pa med pa'i bka' brten pa dang/de bzhin gshegs pa thams
38b5(rgyud): cad kyi thugs rje chen po'i thabs bde ba chen po'i khro bo rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin snyoms par zhugs te/rang gi rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa gsol ba btab po//bde ba chen po rab grub dang
38b6(rgyud): chags pa chen po rnam dag phyir//bdug pa thams cad gzhom pa'i phyir//rang rig sprul mdzod rdo rje 'dzin//de nas bcom ldan 'da'as rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dka' rdo rje ngan
38b7(rgyud): tshig ces bya ba'i bar du dga' ba'i rdo rje khro bo 'di gsungs te/hU~M rag ta dza lA mA pra ti la yang ag ni pri ba ta re badzra ma has kha su ta ra badzra kro ta dza hU~M phaT/de nas 'di gsungs pa tsam gyis/bcom ldan 'das phyag
38b8(rgyud): na rdo rje las kun nas 'od zer dmar po 'bar ba'i snying can smin ma 'gyur zhing/khro gnyer bsdus pa sma ra se ra zhing spyan dmar ba/rdo rje mche brtsigs pas mchu'i sgros rnam par gnon pa'i zhal mnga' ba/dbu
39a1(rgyud): //skra gyen du 'bar ba dang ba dang /rdo rje gzhu la swogs pa yongasu 'dzin pa /bcom ldan 'das kyi dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul du bzhugs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang khams gsum pa'i bdug
39a2(rgyud): pa thams cad 'dul bar mdzad nas /rdo rje dam tshig chen po'i dkyil 'khor byin gyis brlabs so//dkyil 'khor chen po thig btab bya//de la dang po khros pa gzhag/su ratod b+ha hU~M/zhes bzlos/ud
39a3(rgyud): dpal sngon po'i 'od 'bar ba//rdo rje snyems 'dzin pa'i mchog/mche ba cung zad brtsigs pa'i zhal//khro dang bcas pas gzhad pa'i zhal//rdo rje dkyil mo dkrung gis bzhugs//'od zer 'bar ba 'khrug pa'i 'od
39a4(rgyud): dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul gyis ni//thams cad du ni khro bo bri//rab tu dga' ba'i rdo rje khro bo phyag rgya chen pos dang por slob dpon gyis 'jug par byaste/slob ma rnams kyang de bzhin no//
39a5(rgyud): slob dpon bzhugs nas khros pas lcags kyus de nyid kyis dgug par bya'o//hU~M gu ra ta badzra kro ta e he/ma ha su khaM badzra swAhA/sarba badzra ku la ne tra be sha la hU~M/sarba mu tra ga Na ca ban d+ha b+haM/sarba sa ma
39a6(rgyud): yam sho ku ru ho//sarba karma ne sa ta ya a/de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje rigs thams cad bcom ldan 'das dang bcas pa byon zhing grub pa yin no//slob dpon dang slob ma rnams la 'gyed pa dang /khro bo'i
39a7(rgyud): dbab pa'i mtshan ma yang 'byung ngo//gzhan yang cung zad 'byung ba yin no//de grub pa dang /dngos grub dang /cho ga rgyas pa yang de bzhin no//gal te ma grub na lan cig bzla'o//khams gsum ma lus pa las
39a8(rgyud): rgyal bar bya ba la ni/rdo rje hU~M mdzad kyi phyag rgyas so//sems can gyi khams thams cad 'dul ba ni/rdo rje khro bos gsad pas so//'jig rten gsum ma lus pa las rnam par rgyal ba la ni/'jig
39b1(rgyud): rten gsum po las rnam par rgyal ba'i phyag rgyas so//thams cad dgug pa dang/gzhug pa dang/dbab pa dang/bcings pa dang/dbang du bya ba dang/gzhug pa dang/rengs pa dang/gnyid log pa dang
39b2(rgyud): rgyas btab pa dang/bskyel ba dang/bsad par bya ba la ni bde ba chen po rdo rje khro bos so//thams cad ltung bar bya ba la ni/rdo rje phra mo'i khro bos so//thams cad dbab pa la ni/rdo rje 'debs pa'i khro bos so//
39b3(rgyud): thams cad nges par gnon pa la ni rdo rje gang rgyangs kyis so//las thams cad la nye bar dgos pa la ni bde ba chen po rdorje dam tshig khro bos so//bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba las/rab tu dga' ba las byung ba rdo rje gsang
