44b8(rgyud): po ni nor bu'i rigs la'o//de nas gsung gi phyag rgya ni/bsnyan pa'i glu'i yul thams cad kyi tshul rnams nus pa ji lta bus;klu'i rgyan rnams thams cad kyis glu blangs te/bzlas pa ni rdo rje   
45a1(rgyud): @#//sems dpa' rigs las rab tu dga' ba'i rdo rje'i ngag go//lce dang mchu gnyis shin tu g.yo ba med cing/ngos shin tu ma byar ba dbyangs dang bral ba'i rdo rje'i ngag de ni de bzhin gshegs 
45a2(rgyud): pa'i rigs la ste/rigs thams cad dgos pa'o//sprin gyi 'brug sgra'i yi ge hU~M gi sgra phra mo dang ldan pa ni/khro bo rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ngag rdo rje'i rigs la'o//ting nge 'dzin gyi ngag pad ma'i rigs 
45a3(rgyud): la ste/thams cad dag pa'i pad ma zhes bya'o//chos kyi ngag nor bu'i rigs la ste/mchod pa'i nor bu'o//de nas thugs kyi phyag rgya lnga ste/thams cad sgrub par bya ba la ni/rdo rje sems dpa'i dngos pos so//sangs
45a4(rgyud): rgyas rjes su dran pas;de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs la'o//bdag gam bdag gi mdun du gsad par bya'o//zhes bya ba ni rdo rje'i rigs la'o//sbyar bar bya'o zhes bya ba ni/pad ma'i rigs la'o//
45a5(rgyud): namakha' thams cad bsgom par bya'o//zhes bya ba ninor bu'i rigs la'o//de nas rdo rje'i phyag rgya lnga ni/rdo rje rtse mo lnga 'od zer gyi phreng bas yongs su khyab pa dang/bde ba chen po'i rdo rje'o//
45a6(rgyud): rdo rje khu tshur gyis;rdo rje sems dpa'i rigs la'o//rdo rje rtse lnga pa 'od zer dang bcas pa;de bzhin gshegs pa'i khu tshur gyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigasala'o//'bar ba'i rang bzhin rdo rje rtse bzhi pa;rdo rje khro
45a7(rgyud): bo'i khu tshur gyis rdo rje'i rigs la'o//pad ma'i steng gi de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdo rje pad ma'i khu tshur gyis pad ma'i rigs la'o//rdo rje phyed pa rdo rje rino (pa)cha'i nor bu'i khu tshur gyis nor bu'i rigs la'o//denas rig+sa
45a8(rgyud): thams cad kyi rtog pa'i gsang ba thogs pa med cing/gdon mi za ba'i de kho na nyid kyi bden pa myur du mchog gi dngos grub kyi cho ga rgyas pa;bsgrub pa thams cad kyi rgyud bshad par bya'o//zhes bcom ldan 'das don 
45b1(rgyud): yod cing thogs pa med par myur ba'i mchog gi dngos grub can rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgya chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i dam tshig go//gsung gi phyag rgya ni snying po'o//thugs kyi
45b2(rgyud): phyag rgya ni nye ba'i snying po'o//rdo rje'i phyag rgya ni rdo rje snyems ma'o//de nas phra mo'i las kyi phyag rgya'i khams chen po'i phra rab brdul rnams don mnyam pa'i snying po chen po 'di nyid kyis grub pa'i bde 
45b3(rgyud): ba dam par 'gyur//de nas bde gshegs de rnams ni//de yi tshig mchog gsan gyur nas//dang po'i dkyil 'khor grub stsol ba//de tshe de la gsol ba btab//chags dang bral ba
45b4(rgyud): 'dul ba'i phyir//chags bral stan par bya ba 'am//'dod chags thams cad rnam dag phyir//nyid kyi thugs kyi cho ga gsungs//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/rdo rje'i glu chen po gsungs 
45b5(rgyud): zhing/dkyil 'khor brtag par mdzad do//thams cad rjes su chags pa'i bde ba dam pa'i thugs/rdo rje sems dpa' khyod ni rab tu byung ba yin//rab tu dga' ba 'gro ba 
45b6(rgyud): kun gyi bde chen po//gtan 'tho di ring bdag la myur grub mngon phyogs mdzad//zhes bya ba dkyil 'khor glu len cing brtag par mdzad do//dkyil 'khor chen po sbyor ba yis//dang po'i dkyil
45b7(rgyud): 'khor brtag par bya//phyag na rdo rje'i thams cad du//rdo rje lha mo la sogs tshogs//ji ltar cho ga bzhin du ni//rgyad par bya ste rab 'jug bya//g.yon du bu mo rdo rje 'dzin//bsdus te rnal    
45b8(rgyud): 'byor bcang bar bya//rdo rje dang bcas bsnyems pa yi//phyag rgya mchog gi rang nyid 'jug//dkyil 'khor de ru zhugs nas kyang//lan gsum du ni bskor bar bya//rdo rje sems dba'i mdun 'dug
46a1(rgyud): @#//ste::::de nyid gsang ba'ang brjod par bya/////////hU~M mahA su kha badzra sA ta//dzA:hU~M:ba~M:hA+o://sA ma ya sta~M A nu ra ga to b+hi ba/sU to sh+yo mi b+ha ba d+h+ri d+h+ro me b+ha ba 
46a2(rgyud): sU to me b+ha ba/A d+hi tiSh+Ta b+ha ga wan/A d+hi ni da ni d+he na sa ha ma ta su kha badzra sa twa sa ma ya/si:gu ya sa ma ya/sid d+hi me pra yang tsa/oM ha ha A:////de nas rab tu dga' bas
46a3(rgyud): rdo rje lha mo dga' ba'i gtam bya'o////oM badzra mu ti sa ya kye ta~M/badzra ki li ki li hA+o/hU~M smra ra badzra ne b+han d+ha hU~M du shu badzri ni hU~M////de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi mdun du nye bar
46a4(rgyud): 'dug ste//de bzhin du bcom ldan 'das bdag la grub par mdzod cig//bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba brjod par bya'o////myur du thams cad rjes chags par//ki li ki li las
46a5(rgyud): kun nus //thams cad rjes su chags bran na//brtag tu legs par mnyes gyur bdag////oM mahA su kha sid d+hi badzra sA twa sid d+ha ////
46a6(rgyud): dza:hU~M:ba~M:ho://sid d+ha sa ma ya sta~M sid d+ha hU~M:////de nas bde ba chen po yi rdo rje'i chus dbang bskur zhing 'thung+sa la //rdo rje rtse lnga pa bzung ste//bdag rdo rje dpa' yino
46a7(rgyud): zhes rang gi ming brjod la//snga ma bzhin du gar gyi mchod pas mchod par bya ste//ji ltar brnyed pa'i yul dang ldan pas//slob ma dang lhan cig mchod pa khyub nas byung bar bya'o//slob ma bzhug
46a8(rgyud): par bya zhing //rang nyid kyang der zhugs bas/dkyil 'khor thams cad du zhugs par 'gyur ro//rdo rje slob dpon thams cad phyag rgya thams cad kyi bdag po //dam tshig thams cad shes pa //
46b1(rgyud): thams cad la tshar gcod pa dang/phan 'dogs par nus pa dngos grub thams cad phun sum tshogs pa'i ye shes kyi las byed pas /rang nyid 'jug pa tsam nyid kyis slob dpon ma yin pa dang/dkyil
46b2(rgyud): 'khor du ma zhugs pa dang yang skad cig nyid du bcom ldan 'das kyis byin gyis rlob par 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar bcom ldan 'das nyid nges par rang gi dam tshig gis/rdo rje
46b3(rgyud): slob dpon du gyur nas /'jug par mdzad do//las thams cad kyi cho ga rgyas pa yang mdzad pa yin no//snying po 'di'i byin rlabs kyis /dkyil 'khor du ma zhugs pa rnams /dkyil 'khor
46b4(rgyud): du zhugs pa dang/thams cad du slob dpon du 'gyur ro//lan cig zlas brjod byas pas /mi gsod pa dang /mi tshugs pa dang/thams cad su sna tshogs pa'i yi ge rnams kyi 'thad pa dang
46b5(rgyud): thos pa dang /shes pa yin no//rdo rje 'chang chen po dang bcas pa'i /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad ma lus par mdzad nas/dngos grub thams cad de la sogs pa yin no
46b6(rgyud): yongs su sbyangs pa dang/brten pa'i mdun du ma yin no//bgyegs byed pa rnams kyang /sdug bsngal bster bar byed pa ma yin no//bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang bde ba chen po rdo rje 'chang
46b7(rgyud): shin du brtan zhing shin du dgyes pa /rjes su chags pa shin tu rgyas pa/dngos grub thams cad dang /phun sum tshogs pa thams cad dang/ye shes thams cad dang/las thams cad gdon
46b8(rgyud): mi za bar myur du 'grub par mdzad par yin no//'on kyang gang gis bris sam /brir bzhug gam/lan cig brjod pa 'am/'dzin pas de ma bsgrubs shing /dkyil du ma zhugs na yang tshe 'di nyid la
47a1(rgyud): @#//ji ltar mngon par 'dod pa'i mchog gi dngos grub thams cad grub pa yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di lta ste/don yod cing thogs pa med pa /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag 
47a2(rgyud): po bdag dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad kyis kyang byed par mi nus te/thams cad rjes su chags pa nyid kyi phyir ro//sangs rgyas kyi byang chub kyang rjes su chags pa las byung ba yin no//
47a3(rgyud): 'dod pa rtan pas rjes su chags te/de mi 'dod pas ji ltar mchog gi bde ba chen po la sogs pa'i /phun sum tshogs pa'i mtha' thams cad du 'gyur ro//zhes thog ma med pa'i bcom ldan 'das 
47a4(rgyud): chags pa chen po'i bde ba chen pos gsungs so//grub na bde ba chen po ni//rdo rje chags pa skad cig ster//bsgrub pa'i dam tshig 'di yin te//'di ni kun gyis 'da' bar dka'//sdig 
47a5(rgyud): pa chen po byed pa 'am//de bzhin dkyil 'khor ma mthong ba lan cig zlas pas sdig sbyangs pas//mchog grub pas ni grub par 'gyur//rtag par gsung ba'i 'dod pa yi//
47a6(rgyud): gzugs brnyen rjes su dran bsgoms na//snying po de yang glegs bam du //thams cad nyid phyir bdag po grub//gang la gzugs brnyan med pa dang //glegs bam med par gyur na yang //yi ge hU~M ni 
47a7(rgyud): thams cad du//bris te bdag bsgrub grub par bya//zhes bya ba thogs pa med cing /don yod pa'i dngos grub can//rdo rje 'chang chen pos gsungs so//de nas rtog pa'i rgyal po 'di'i//las sgrub
47a8(rgyud): sgrub par 'dod pa na//dkyil 'khor thams cad thig btab nas//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya bri bar bya//dang po'i rdo rje zhugs nas brjod par bya//chags pa che las thogs pa che//thogs pa che las bde 
47b1(rgyud): ba che ;;bde ba che las rdo rje brtan//rdo rje las ni phyag rgya 'byung//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje yis//gzugs brnyan rab tu sbyar ba yis//phyag rgya thams cad bshad pa ste//dam tshig 'da'
47b2(rgyud): bar dka' ba'o//phyag rgya bcings pa tsam gyis ni//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'chang la sogs//yang dag bsdus par gyur na ni//mnyam pa'i 'jigs pa grub par 'gyur//ji ltar rgyal bo'i phyag rgya yi
47b3(rgyud): bka' ni 'da' bar dka' ba ste//de bzhin rgyal ba kun gyis kyang//phyag rgya 'da' bar nus pa min//sangs rgyas nyid dam rdo rje 'chang//de ltar bskal ba bye ba yis//thob min ji ltar phyag rgya mchog
47b4(rgyud): bsdus pa tshe 'di nyid la thob//ji ltar gdul bya'i 'jig rten na//de bzhin dngos su gsungs pa yin//de'i thabs ni phyag rgya ste//dngos grub thams cad byed pa mchog//de bzhin du cho ga rgyas pa dang 
47b5(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad shin tu gdon mi za'o//de nas dang po'i dkyil 'khor gyis gsang ba'i dam tshig gi phyag rgya bcing ba 'di yin te/sor mo thams cad kun sbyar nas//rdo rje thal 
47b6(rgyud): mo zhes brjod do//sor mo nang du bcings ba las //rdo rje sdems pas yang dag bshad//rdo rje sdams pa'i nang du ni//'dzub mo 'the po yang dag sbyar//g.yas pa yi ni 'theb po'i rtags
47b7(rgyud): bcug te btsir ba lcags dkyu 'dzin//mda' lnga bsdus pa bcings nas ni//dbus ma'i bcing ba nang du gnas//'the bo snyams pa'i mtha' rdo rje//dang po'i bde ba dam pa brtan//'theb 'dzub
47b8(rgyud): rtse sbyar dbus ma'i rje//mig 'bras ltar sbyar 'dzin pa'o//dbus ma'i rdo rje 'og tu ni//'the bong gis mnan btsir bar bya//rdo rje 'dren ma dang 'dra ba//srin lag 'the'u chung kha phye ba//
48a1(rgyud): @#//bskor nas pus mos gzhag pa ni//dang po'i rdo rje las byung ba'o//gyen du gtor dang 'thur du ldugs//'the bo sbyar nas bskor bas dang //rab tu rkyang zhing byug pa dang //bskor
48a2(rgyud): zhing spyi bor gnas pa dang //bskor nas snying khar gzhag pa dang//yongs su bskor zhing rgod pa yi//kha las gyen du 'byung ba dang//de bzhin gar gyis mchod pa dang//'dzub mo'i sor mo gnyis
48a3(rgyud): kyis kyang //g.yon pa'i 'dzub mo g.yas kyis bzung //'dzub mo the bo bcings pa dang//'the po tha ma'i nang du gzhag//khu tshur gyi phyag rgya rnams so//de nas don yod cing thogs pa med pa 'di
48a4(rgyud): rnams kho na'i las kyi phyag rgya bcing ba mchog tu gsang ba bla na med cing /bla ma las kyi tshogs sgrub par byed pa /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang bcas pa'i bdag gis kyang
48a5(rgyud): gnod par mi nus so//de la bde ba chen po'i rdo rje dbus ma'i las rdo rje sems dpa'i rtse mo bcu lasogs par gzhag pa dang 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba dang/rdo rje nor bu dang//ji 
48a6(rgyud): ltar 'dod pa'i dngos grub kun byed pa rnams kyis||rdo rje 'chang chen po dang /sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa' dang/rdo rje rin po che dang/mchog gi dngos grub po//rdo rje 'dren mas ni 
48a7(rgyud): mig 'bras kyi nad las so//g.yas pa dang g.yon pa'i mig gis bltas pas /thams cad rjes su chags pa'o//mig 'bras tshur 'dren pa ni dgug pa'o//gzhug pas thams cad bzhug
48a8(rgyud): pa'o//bcing ba dang 'ching ba'o//btsum pa med pas dbang du bya ba'o//rdo rje ki li ki li las dbus ma la btsum ma na dang/'khyud pa dang/btsir ba dang/rol ba dang/dbus 
48b1(rgyud): ma dang/'the po'i rtse mor bad bas bdeg pas|mthong ba dang /rol pa dang/btsir ba rnams kyis thams cad la rol pa ste/rgyu ba dang/mi rgyu ba thams cad du /lha'i gzugs kyis|
48b2(rgyud): bud med rnams 'byung ba yin no//ji srid 'di rnams dang rol cing /rdo rje sems dpa'i btsun mo la yang rol bar byed do//rdo rje chu srin mas btsir ba dang/gyen du bteg pa dang 
48b3(rgyud): myang ba dang/drung du 'gro ba dang/myur du za ba dang/rnams kyis rtan pa'i chags pas bcing ba dang/thams cad las rnam par rgyal ba dang/chu'i nang du gnas pa dang/'chag pa dang/

48b5(rgyud): ma'i rdo rje bral ba rnams kyis /khro bo thams cad 'bar ba dang/rab tu zhi ba dang/thams cad la rgyas btab pa dang/ltung ba dang /gsod pa rnams myur du grub po
48b6(rgyud): 'the po rdo rje'i nang du zhugs pa'i mchod pa rnams kyis mchod na/rdo rje 'chang chen pos kyang mchod par 'gyur ro//de bzhin du lha'i bu mo rnams kyis|'the po rdo rje'i nang du zhus 
48b7(rgyud): pa'i las rnams kyis|lha'i bu mo thams cad kyang bkug nas rol pa lasogs pa'i las thams cad la nye bar sbyar ba'o//rdo rje lcags dkyu ma lasogs pa'i nang du khu tshur gyi las rnams 
48b8(rgyud): kyis yid kyis 'dod pa'i dngos grub thams cad do////de nas dngos grub thams cad kyi rgyu ni/rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgya chen po'i dngos grub dang/dam tshig dang/ye shes dang 
49a1(rgyud): @#//las kyi dngos grub grub pa'o//don yod pas grub pa myur du thams cad kyi bdag nyid bcom ldan 'das kyis byin gyis brlabs pas yid kyis 'dod pa grub pa'o//rab tu dga' ba'i rdo rje'i
49a2(rgyud): brtags kyi rtse mo g.yo ba las thams cad do//skye gnas g.yo bas kyang de bzhin no//bde ba chen po rdo rje dbus ma'i las byed pas dngos grub thams cad do//sgyeg mo dga' ba'i rdo rje mas mi g.yo bar byed
49a3(rgyud): pas las thams cad grub par byas nas/thogs pa med pa'i dngos grub kyis grub par 'gyur ro//de bzhin du phyag mtshan rnams kyis dngos grub thams cad 'grub par 'gyur ro//dang po nyin re lan re gcig

49a6(rgyud): de nas dang po'i dkyil 'khor la sogs pa'i phyag thams cad kyi rgyud kyi cho ga rgyas pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das ni phyag rgya chen po'i rgyud ni//pad pa g.yon du dgod pa ni//zhabs kyi pad ma ci bder bri//
49a7(rgyud): rdo rje'i dkyil mo dkrung bcas nas//sku ni drang po 'dzin pa bde//bsnyems bcas rdo rje gsor nas ni//rdo rje bsnyems pa yang dag 'chang //sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje 'dzin//rdo rje sems dpa'

49b1(rgyud): ji bzhin phyag rgya'i rjes 'brangs nas//mtshan ma'i phyag rgya ldan pa yis //sems dpa' chen por rang nyid 'gyur//phyag rgya chen po'o//de nas dam tshig gi phyag rgya bcing ba ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje 
49b2(rgyud): bshad do//rdo rje mda' la sogs pa'i bzhi po 'di dag las byung ngo//'dzub mo gnyis kyis lcags dkyu'i phyi rol du mngon par phyogs pa'o//'the pos 'khyud pa'o//'the po gnyis kha phye la 'dzub

49b4(rgyud): de bzhin mtshams rnams la ;rdo rje thal mo bskor bas so//sgo'i phyag rgya rnams la ni/rdo rje sdams pa'i khu tshur gyis so//bde ba chen po mchog gi phyag rgya che//de bzhin dngos
49b5(rgyud): grub rab stsol ba//mda' sogs dam tshig sna tshogs pa'i//dngos grub chen po mchog bster ba'o//de nas chos kyi phyag ni 'di'o////ho:b+hid hU~M:d+hig hU~M:////
49b6(rgyud): de rnams kyang dngos grub thams cad bstsol ba'o//de nas las kyi phyag rgya ni//rdo rje khu tshur rdo rje med pa rnams kyis brtags bya'o//g.yon pa'i rdo rje bsnyems pas /g.yas pa'i khu tshur stod pa'i
49b7(rgyud): tshul gyis;;snying khar bzung bar bya ba ;rdo rje snyems par bya ba ste/rab tu dga' ba brtan par byed cing/dngos grub thams cad stsol ba'o//mda' gzhu stod pas rdo rje mtshon cha ma'o//'og tu 
49b8(rgyud): 'khyud pa'o //rgyal mtshan sgreng ba'o//snyems pa gnyis pa bstod pa'o//sgeg pa dang bcas/mtha' ma lasogs pas dkrol bas klog 'gyu ba'i gar g.yas su bskor bas /me tog lasogs
50a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i ka bu ta'i thal mo spyi bo nas phab /rdo rje thal mo dkrol bas de bzhin du sgeg pa dang bcas bas de bzhin du sgag pa dor bar bya'o//rdo rje gar ni nang gi 'tshams la'o//rdzogs pa 
50a2(rgyud): 'am ni ma tshang ba//yang na ji ltar brnyed pa bzhin//me tog mchog sogs ma yis ni//dngos grub mchog sogs thun mong yin//dam tshig phyag rgya'i sbyor bas mtshan mas yongs su bzung ba'o//'di 
50a3(rgyud): rnams kyang don yod pa'i las grub pa rnams so//rdo rje khu tshur gyis bdud rtsi'i chu bzla'o//'jug pa'i cho ga'i phyag rgya bshad pa ni/dkyil 'khor chen po thams cad kyi rdo rje slob dpon gyi sla ba pa'i gsang ba'i
50a4(rgyud): dam tshig ces bya ba 'jug pa'i phyag rgya gnyis pa ni/rdo rje sdems pa bcings nas ni//g.yon 'theb 'dzub mo'i skye gnas su//g.yas pa'i 'the pos stegs bcug la//btsir nas bde ba ster
50a5(rgyud): ba'o;;'dzub mo lcags dkyu bcings pa des//dkyil 'khor mchog tu 'jug par bya//yang na sems dang gang la 'jug//de der myur du 'grub par 'gyur//rab tu dga'

56a2(rgyud): brtog pa yongs su dag pa'i phyir //de la bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor rgyud ni yin te/sa la yi ge gnas bris nas//bde ba chen po zhes brjod pa//de'i dam tshig rdo rje
56a3(rgyud): ste||snying po thams cad grub byed pa'o//de la dkyil 'khor gyi bshad pa'i rgyud ni//ji ltar gnas dang ji ltar dus//ji ltar 'jig rten ji lta bu//'khor ni bkod pa bya ba ste//
56a4(rgyud): de lta bu'i dkyil 'khor bri//de nas dkyil 'khor thams cad du zhugs pa'i rnam par smin pa'i rgyud ni//rdo rje sems sogs sems dpa' yi//'khor bcas de tshe dkyil 
56a5(rgyud): 'khor du zhugs pa'i bsod nams ci 'dra ba//de nas rigs thams cad 'khor lo'i khyad par gyi rgyud ni////rdo rje 'khor lo bstan pa bzhin//lcags kyi 'khor lo dam
56a6(rgyud): pa ste//'bar ba'i 'khor lo de bzhin du//pad ma 'dab brgyad nor bu che//de nas dkyil 'khor thabs cad grub pa'i rgyud ni//dkyil 'khor brtag pa byas nas ni//nang gi rim par
56a7(rgyud): zhugs byas te//dkyil 'khor dag la||dza:hU~M:ba~MHho:sa/pra sid h+ya ma~M:zhes brjod pa bya/de nas rdo rje sems dpa' nyid kyi rgyud ni//sangs rgyas la sogs 'gro kun gyi//
56a8(rgyud): thams cad bdag nyid bcom ldan mchog//sems can 'byung phyir sems dpa' ste//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu grags//de nas dam tshig chen po ni kho na nyid bshad pa'i rgyud ni
56b1(rgyud): thog mtha' med pa'i sems dpa' ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dga' ba che//kun tu bzang po kun bdag nyid//'khor ba mya ngan 'das de bzhin//de nas thog ma dang tha ma med pa'i 
56b2(rgyud): de kho na nyid bshad pa'i rgyud ni//thog mtha' med pa thams cad ni//namakha' 'byung ba chen po'i gnas//namakha' skye ba skye med phyir //rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin no//de nas
56b3(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rab tu dga' ba bshad pa'i rgyud ni//rab tu dga' phyir nyon mongs te//'gro ba thams cad bde ba 'dod//de la bde ba rnam dag phyir//srid gsum snga rol bcom
56b4(rgyud): ldan 'byung//de nas dang po'i snying po'i de kho na nyid rga bshad pa'i rgyud ni/chags pa kun la chags pa che//'odad ldan thams cad sad par byed//chags pa 'bar
56b5(rgyud): ba dri za yi//snying po phyag rgya rdo rje'o//de nas yi ge hU~M/glu blang ba'i rgyud ni//glu las chags pa dga' las 'odad//rab tu dga' las thams cad 'byung //
56b6(rgyud): thams cad 'byung bar grub pa'i tshe/phyir//bskal pa dang po bcom ldan gsungs//de nas yi ge hU~M gis bzlas pa don gyi bshad pa'i rgyud ni//myur du thams cad rjes chags pas//
56b7(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad rjes su chags//rjes su chags par byed mchog ni//snying po'i bzlas pa'i don yin no//de nas sems rdo rje sems dpa'i rgyud ni//glu rnams ji ltar khro bo ni
56b8(rgyud): bsdus nas kun tu 'jug pa ni//rdo rje thog ni brtan gyur pa//de bzhin rdo rje bsdus pa'o//de nas rdo rje sems dpa' 'byung ba shes pa'i rgyud ni//thams cad bdag nyid sems dpa' ni
57a1(rgyud): myur du thams cad la yang zhugs//de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i rgyud ni//'dod las chags pa dga' las bde//bde las sdig pa sdig las sdug//
57a2(rgyud): sdug las ting 'dzin shin tu brtan//rdo rje nyid las sangs rgyas 'byung//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs pa 'byung bas shes pa'i rgyud ni//de bzhin gshegs pa'i    
57a3(rgyud): rigs de nyid//de nyid rdo rje rigs chen po//de nyid pad ma rigs dag pa //de nyid nor bu'i rigs mchog bshad//de nas mngon par byang chub pa'i rgyud ni//rang sems so sor rtog+sa
57a4(rgyud): pa ni//byang chub sems ni yang dag 'byung//rdo rje nyid phyir sangs rgyas nyid//bdag nyid sangs rgyas rab tu bsgom//de bzhin gshegs pa bdud nyid du shes pa'i   
57a5(rgyud): rgyud ni//rang sems nyid ni bdud nyid de//sems nyid de bzhin gshegs pa yin//rang gi sems ni 'dul ba'i phyir//sangs rgyas bdud nyid du ni 'gyur//de nas 
57a6(rgyud): brtog pa thams cad grub pa shes pa'i rgyud ni//rtog pa kun las grub bya gang//rgyu ba dang ni mi rgyu yin//rang gi lha'i rnal 'byor gyis//de dag thams cad grub par bya//
57a7(rgyud): de nas dang po'i snying po grub pa shes pa'i rgyud ni//bzlas pa'i don shes nus pa yin//rdo rje sems dpa' rab sgrub te//ji srid 'dod pa'i bzlas pa mchog//dngos
57a8(rgyud): grub chen po'i bde ba ster//de nas snying po dang ;phyag rgya dang ;bsngags dang/rig pa shes pa sgrub pa'i rgyud ni//sems dpa' gang gis snying po gang//phyag rgya 
57b1(rgyud): bsngags dang rig pa gang//bzlas pa don du sems dpa' che//bdag nyid dam ni sgrub bya ba//de nas dngos grub shes pa'i rgyud ni//rdo rje sems dpa'i dngos grub che//de bzhin
57b2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs kun gyi//rdo rje sems dpa' sems dpa' nyid//las kun mchog gi dngos grub phyir//de nas bsod nams med pa rnams kyis sgrub pa shes pa'i rgyud ni//ci 'dod
57b3(rgyud): 'khor lo bris nas ni//ci yang rung ba'i thal bas kyang//sems dpa' chen po zhag nas ni//nyin zhag bzhir ni sgrub par bya//de nas slob dpon nyid kyi sgrub pa shes pa'i rgyud ni|
57b4(rgyud): bde ba chen po rdo rje yis//gsang mchog dam tshig grong khyer du//ji bzhin rab bzhi rang nyid rab tu zhugs te/bdud rtsi chu yis dbang bskur bya//de nas me tog la sogs pa'i
57b5(rgyud): mchod pa 'bul ba'i shes pa'i rgyud ni//rdo rjes can la mchod pa kun//de gshegs pa'i rigs la dkar rdo rje bdag nyid nag po ste//dmar po pad ma nor bu sar
57b6(rgyud): de nas 'bul ba shes pa'i rgyud ni//rdzogs pa 'am ni ma tshang ba//yang na ji ltar brnyed pa ni//rdo rje gar sogs mchod pa ni//rtag tu thams cad grub par 'gyur//
57b7(rgyud): de nas dus kyi mchod pa shes pa'i rgyud ni//drug ldan la sogs snyan pa'i glu//glu ni thams cad ci 'dod pa//rdo rje 'chang ma la sogs dkrol//mchod ma dag ni rab tu bya//
57b8(rgyud): de nas gsang ba'i mchod pa shes pa'i rgyud na//rdo rje gar byas ma chod pa ni//go rims ji lta ba bzhin no//bzlas pa'i don gyis ma brjod kyang//mchog gi sgrub pa grub par
58a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur||de nas sdig pa chen po byed pa'ang shes pa'i rgyud ni//dkyil 'khor la ni ma zhugs kyang//tshul khrims 'chal ba lasogs pa//ting nge 'dzin gyi stobs kyis ni|
58a2(rgyud): sdig pa kun las grol bar 'gyur//de nas dngos grub mi thob pa'i rgyud shes pa'i chags pa/kha smad par ni mi bya ste//rnam dag de bzhin bde ba ste//bcom ldan 'das kyang chags
58a3(rgyud): pa che||'di ma dag pas grub mi 'gyur//de nas mchog gi dngos grub shes pa'i rgyud ni//ji bzhin yul dang ldan gyur nas//mchog gi dngos grub bsgrub par bya//rtog pa kun las
58a4(rgyud): rnal 'byor 'di//tshe 'di nyid la sgrub par byed//bde|ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i rtog pa'i rgyal po chen po las//brtog pa thams cad rgyud phyi ma'i cho ga rgyas pa'o//de nas bcoM
58a5(rgyud): ldan 'das rtog pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje gsang ba/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dbang bskur ba'i rdo rje rin po che/de bzhin gshegs pa tham
58a6(rgyud): cad kyi cho ga gzigs pa'i ye shes kyi dbang phyug gi rdo rje pad ma/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi sna tshogs pa'i mdzad pa'i phrin las dam pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'i rdo rje thams cad la khyab
58a7(rgyud): pa|ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i|rdo rje khams brtan pa'i dngos grub kyi cho ga rgyas pa|namakha'i khams kyi mthar thug par|chos kyi spro ba dbyings so//khams thams
58a8(rgyud): cad khyab pa'i|de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis/'khor lo'i mngon par mkhyen pa mdzad nas//dang po'i snying po gsang ba'i de kho na nyid kyi|rdo rje sgrub pa'i rgyud 'di nyid   
58b1(rgyud): gsungs so//ji srid 'khor ba'i gnas la bzhugs//de srid sems can don la mdzad //mya ngan 'das bzhugs gzi brjid can//sems can don ni nus pa ni//de phyir sems   
58b2(rgyud): dpa' dam pa rnams//'khor ba srid du 'khor bar bzhugs//sems can don ni 'khor bar bzhed//thams cad don ni mchog chos can//de bzhin gshegs pa'i mi 'dul bas//
58b3(rgyud): sems can don ni mdzad pa ste//'dir rab grub dngos grub mchog//dus kun tu ni thams cad du//rdo rje 'chang sogs la sogs pa//ji ltar bsod nams rnam smin gyis//kun la mthong 
58b4(rgyud): ba la sogs kyis//rang gi las kyi mdzad mi spyod//dkyil la ni ma zhugs kyang//srid pa 'di la myur 'grub 'gyur//shin tu mthong ba la sogs kyis//sems can thams cad rab
58b5(rgyud): rtsal mdzad//kun nas kun tu thams cad la//dngos grub thams cad rab rtsol 'gyur//rgyal po rdo rje 'chang chen po//srid pa'i dbang phyug kun gyi rtso//de phyir sgrub bya 'di bshad 
58b6(rgyud): bya//dngos grub shin tu rtag pa'o//gang gis srid pa 'di nyid du//shin tu rtag pa'i dngos grub thob//zlugs ma 'am ni rdungs ma 'am//ri mor bris sam 'dus byas pa'i//phyag na rdo rje 
58b7(rgyud): sku gzugs ni//de bzhin gshegs pa rab tu bzhugs bya'o//bde ba chen po rdo rje gsang ba'i dam tshig gi de kho na nyid grub pa'i dngos grub kyi de cho ga rgyas pa'o////de nas bcoM
58b8(rgyud): ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes kyi slar yang phyag rgya thams cad sgrub pa yi//dngos grub    kyi rgyud 'di gsungs so////nyin re snga rol dus gcig bzhin//dang 
59a1(rgyud): @#//po'i rdo rje bde ba che//'dis ni rtog pa thams cad la//phyag rgya thams cad 'dor bsdus pas//dam tshig yang dag bcings nas grub//phyi nas ma sgrubs grub par 'gyur//de n+sa 
59a2(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis rdo rje chos kyi gsang ba'i sngags sgrub pa 'di gsungs so////langs pa 'am ni 'dug pa 'am//gang dang der ni gnas pa 'am//thams cad rjes chags rnal 'byor 
59a3(rgyud): des//dngos grub thams cad thob par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis las kyi gsang ba'i de kho na nyid kyi rgyud 'di gsungs so//gsung ba'i gar mchog mchod pa 'am//yang na mchod 
59a4(rgyud): pa ci bde bar//byas te mgon po sgrubs byas na//mchog gi dngos grub myur du thob//de nas rang phyag rgyas bdag nyid bsgrub pa'i rgyud ni/phyag rgya chen po mnyam gzhag ste//rang gi 
59a5(rgyud): snying po lan cig bsgrubs//brtan shing shin tu dgyes kun chag shin tu dgyes 'gyur bdag po grub//de nas yi ge hU~M gis klu blang ba grub pa'i rgyud ni/ri b+hi ta sogs rgyan dang ldan//
59a6(rgyud): ci nus pa dang ci 'dod pa//dri za'i dang po blangs nas ni//yi ge hU~M gis grub par 'gyur//zhes bya ba bcom ldan 'das chags pa chen po nya rdo rje sems dpa' rdo rje 'chang chen pos gsungs so//
59a7(rgyud): de kho na nyid chen pos gnas pa'i dngos grub bsgrag pa'i rgyud do////de nas rtog pa thams cad kyi las kyi phyag rgya bshad par bya'o//zhes bya ba bcom ldana'adas thog ma dang 
59a8(rgyud): tha ma med pa'i nya phrin las can rdo rje sems dpa'as gsungs so//shin tu ring ba'i tshe 'phel ba ni//bde ba chen po rdo rje chos so//shin tu gzhon ba'i na tshod 'phel ba ni//bde ba chen 
59b1(rgyud): po rdo rje bcud kyis len gyis so//gzugs chen po dang/gzi brjid chen po 'phel ba ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje gzugs kyi pad ma gzi brjid kyis so//'du byed thams cad 'phel ba ni 
59b2(rgyud): bde ba chen po rdo rje 'du byed chen pos so//sems can thams cad kyi bdag nyid thams cad bsrung ba ni/bde ba chen po rdorje bsrung bas so//phyogs thams cad dang /'tshams thams 
59b3(rgyud): cad dang/dkyil 'khor bcing ba ni/bde ba chen po rde rje bskor ba ste/thams cad rdo rje chen po'i ngo bo nyid/khro bo'i rgyal po chen po/rdo rjes kyang 'jug par mi nus zhing /mi phyed par 'gyuro 
59b4(rgyud): srid pa thams cad bsrung bar ni/bde ba chen po rdo rji byin gyis brlobs pa'o//bdag nyid kyi dngos grub thams cad dam/gzhan yang rdo rje byed pa ni/bde ba chen po rdo rdzo go chas so
59b5(rgyud): 'thab mo thams cad las rnam par rgyal ba ni/rdo rje byin gyis brlabs pa'o//rgyal mtshan mchog gi dpung rgyan gyiso//'thab mo bsrung ba ni/bde ba  chen po gzhan
59b6(rgyud): gyis mi thub pas so//gzhan gyi dpung thams cad gshig pa ni/rdo rje bde ba chen po'i rdo rje 'jig par byed pas so//gzhan gyi dpang nyams pa dang /ldang ba dang /ltung ba dang /
59b7(rgyud): gsod pa ni/bde ba chen po'i rdo rje phyag rgya'i las kyis so//klog bskor bas so//rims nad thams cad lasogs pa'i/nad thams cad dang /nad chen po zhi ba ni/bde ba chen po
59b8(rgyud): rdo rje 'phrog pas so//dug thams cad gshig pa dang /dug med par bya ba ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje phyag rgya'i las kyisaso//gdon thams cad las grol ba ni/bde ba chen po 'byung po 
60a1(rgyud): @#//sbrad pas so//ma mo dang /mkha' 'gro ma dang /gdon thams cad grol ba chen po dang/gzhom pa dang/snying gzhan byung ba dang/shi ba bso ba dang/tshe 'phel ba dang/de 
60a2(rgyud): shin tu bsrung ba rnams ni/bde ba chen po|rdo rje'i phyag rgya yin lag gis so//thams cad dbab pa dang/g.yo ba dang/'ching ba dang/gar byed du gzhug pa dang/glu len du gzhug pa dang
60a3(rgyud): bsnga ma 'byung bas so sor brtag pa dang/rmad du 'byung ba dang/cho 'phrul rtan pa dang/bde ba chen po thams cad bde ba thams cad smrar gzhug pa dang/dkrol ba dang /bsrung ba dang/
60a4(rgyud): sos su bcug pa rnams ni/bde ba chen pa'i rdo rje dbab pas so//rgyu ba dang mi rgyu ba'i dngos grub thams cad la//yid kyis 'dod pa'i bra dbab pa thams cad grub pa ni/bde ba
60a5(rgyud): chen po'i rdo rje pra dbab pas so//rang gi lus dang /ngag dang/yid rdo rje/mthong ba dang/thos pa dang/smra ba dang/ston pa'i shes pa drug pa ni/bde ba chen po'i
60a6(rgyud): rdo rje'i bdag nyid bsgoms pas so//thams cad las 'das pa dang/ma 'ongs pa dang/da ltar byung ba bka' mthong ba'i yi ge/sna tshogs pa 'am/dngos po thams cad kyis rna ba'i shes
60a7(rgyud): pa rab tu 'jug pa ni/bde ba chen po rdo rje thams cad mkhyen pas so//klog dang/char dang/thog dbab pa dang/thams cad zlog pa dang/sprin bshig pa ni/bde ba chen po'i rdo rje 
60a8(rgyud): khro bo'i lta bas so//sa'i 'og thams cad las/sngar ma byung ba'i 'bru lasogs pa'i 'bras bu 'byung ba dang/nye bar len pa'i dang/chu med pa'i gnas las/chu'i dngos po 'byung
60b1(rgyud): ba dang /nye bar len pa dang/mun pa chen por snang ba chen por bskyed pa dang/nye bar len pa dang/rlung thams cad la bka' sgo ba dang /nye bar len pa dang/namakha' thams cad 
60b2(rgyud): las yid kyi 'dod pa'i dngos po thams cad mthong ba dang/nye bar len pa'i bde ba chen po rdo rje'i dbyings kyi dbang phyug mas so//thams cad gzhom pa dang/rengs pa dang/bral ba dang/
60b3(rgyud): rmugs pa dang/gnyid log par bya ba dang/rgyas btab par bya ba dang/ltung ba dang/rmongs pa dang/bsad pa dang/phyir ltog pa ni bde ba chen po'i bdug pa'i/rdo rje'i phyag rgyas so/thams
60b4(rgyud): cad rjes su chags pa dang/dgug pa dang gzhug pa dang/dbab pa dang/bcing ba dang krol ba dang bral ba dang/ltung ba dang ltung bar bslang ba dang/'dren pa dang dran du byed pa dang/
60b5(rgyud): mngag bzhug pa dang/bka' sgo byed pa dang/thams cad tshur brang ba dang/phar gtang ba dang/gsod pa dang/slar bso ba laswogs pa'i las thams cad byed pa dang/dbang du byed pa'i bsgrub pa ni/bde
60b6(rgyud): ba chen po'i rdo rje thams cad bsgrub pas so//rigs thams cad kyi mchog gi dngos grub laswogs pa'i mchog dngos grub thams cad dang/mchog gi skur laswogs pa'i thams cad dang/phun sum tshogs pa thams cad dang/
60b7(rgyud): thams cad rjes su chags pa laswogs pa'i bde ba dang/yid bde ba thams cad rab tu gzhug pa dang/mchod pa chen po thams cad laswogs pa'i las thamasas cad myur du grub pa'i cho ga rgyas pa ni/bde ba chen po'i rdo rje mchog
60b8(rgyud): gi dngos grub kyis so//de la bde ba chen po'i rdo rje gsang ba'i dam tshig gi dang po'i mchog dam tshig las kyis phyag rgya'i rgyud ni 'di yin te/bde ba chen po'i rdo rje ni rdo rje sems dpa' tsher ster ba'o//'di'i las
61a1(rgyud): @#//kyi phyag rgya thams cad bcing ba'o//dbus ma'i rdo rje btung bya'o//gtso mo'i 'od zer byas pas gdong g.yogs sbyangs te gdong pa bkrol bar bya'o//bdag nyid la kun tu bskor nas//snying kar bgrol bar
61a2(rgyud): bya'o//gtsug tor dang phyogs thams cad dang/dkyil 'khor skor ba'o//snying khar yid kyis brgyab dang phrag pa dang/spyi'i dang pral bar gzhag la rdo rje khu tshur gnyis kyis phreng ba bcings la go cha bya ste/mdun
61a3(rgyud): pa byas te/nas rdo rje'i gnas rnams su slar yang phreng ba byas pas//rgyab tu mdud pas byas te dar byangs phyar bya'o/snying kar dang pral ba dang/'grin pa dang /spyi 'or gzhag par bya'o//dbus ma'i dar mchog
61a4(rgyud): go;byab pa'i gyen du kha ltas pa'i dbus ma dang/'jigs pa'i gdong gis lta bas so//'bar ba bzhi'i tshul gyis dong shig khros pa'o/rang gi snying khar bcings te zhi ba'i lta bas rtse phrog par bye ba'o
61a5(rgyud): lta bas phrog par bya'o//dbab pas myur du grol bar bya'o/yongasu bskor bas dkral bar gzhag ste/dbus ma'i rdo rje sor mo'i rtse mo gnyis bskyod par byas na ci ltar bzhin du las byed pa'i snying kar
61a6(rgyud): gzhag ste/dbus ma rdo rje'i las ni 'di lta ste/snying bkar bstod pa dang/bskyed pa dang 'ching ba dang/gar byed du bcug pa dang/'grin pa la sogs pa dang/rdo rje rtse mor bcing ba dang/kha 'bub pa dang/'grin pa
61a7(rgyud): gsal ba dang/bcings pa myur du bkrol ba dang/bsrung ba dang byab pa'i las so/snying kha kha sbyor bskyod pa'o/nyin re zhing snga rol du lan cig lus yongasu sbyang ba dang/'grin pa gsal ba'i ngag dang snying kar gzhag pa dang/
61a8(rgyud): phyag rgya la lta ba ste/nyin re snga rol du lan cig dbang po thams cad la kun tu skor bar bya'o//dbus ma rdo rje byed pa ni khro bo'i rdo rje'i lta bas so//dbus ma'i rdo rje'i las ni 'og nas byung ste/nye bar longs spyod pa dang
61b1(rgyud): thams cad la dgug pa byed pa dang/dbab pa'i sbyin pa dang/'bar ba'i me stag byed pa dang/nye bar len pa dang rlung gi tshul dang/nye bar len pa dang/namakha' sgom pa las du ma'o//dbus ma'i rdo rje'i lus ni
61b2(rgyud): phyag rgyas gzhom pa dang/rtse mo gnyis snying khar btsar ba dang/sen mo brung ba dang/mig reg pa dang/brutsum pa dang/sor mo mnyam pas snying kar rdeg pa dang/ltung ba dang glod par dang byed pa dang/phan tshun
61b3(rgyud): rdeg pa dang/rang gi snying khar rang bzhin du gzhag pa'i las so//dbus ma'i las so//dbus ma'i rdo rje las ni mnga' ma stod pa dang/lcags gnyis kyis dkug pa dang/g.yon pa la g.yas pa bcug pa dang
61b4(rgyud): rtse mo skyod pa dang/gdub bur bcing dang/myur du grol ba dang /sor mnyam por myur du bral ba dang/ltung bar byed pa dang/'og nas rdeg pa dang /g.yon pa nas g.yas pas brang ba dang
61b5(rgyud): las thams cad la sbyar bar byed pa dang/'og na skyod cing /gyen du grong ba dang/tshur 'dren pa dang/phar btang ba dang/g.yon dang g.yas kyi brang por rdeg pa dang/rang gi
61b6(rgyud): snying kar rtse mor 'dren pa dang/las thams cad la nges par sbyor bar byed pa'i las dang/snying khar 'khor lo bzhug pa dang/dbang bskur ba'i phreng ba dang/dar spyangs bcing bar byed
61b7(rgyud): pa dang/snying khar las brang bas te/bde ba thams cad yongs su sbyang ba dang/gar gyis blangs te /spyi bor dkrol ba'i las so//bde ba chen po rab bsgrub dang//chags
61b8(rgyud): pa chen po rnam dag phyir//don yod thogs med sna tshogs pa yi//phyag rgya'i rgyud ni rab du bshad//rdo rje 'dzin pa chen po yis//sangs rgyas sras bcas bdag nyid kun//
62a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sems can thams cad myur grub phyir//don yod dngos grub kun stsol ba'o//dkyil 'khor bdag tu ma bzhugs pa//sdig pa'i las ni kun dang ldan//de dag kyang ni bsgrub 'gyur te/bcings

63a1(rgyud): @#//thams cad gnas//bde ba chen mchog gi rdo rje mchog gsang ba'i//dngos grub tshe 'dir de dag rnam la brtan//sems can don du rgyal ba las dag phyir//rnam par spyad par mdzad de 'di yin

79b3(rgyud): gri ka ra//dbang phyug dam pa drag po sogs//gtum po chen po 'dul bar mdzad//phyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbyibs rnams su//mdor na ming po gsum la sogs//lha la sogs pa'i tshogs thams cad//legs par bri 'am bzhag
79b4(rgyud): par bya//'tshamasu lha mo rab bshad pa//de rnams kyis ni sgo dbus su//rdo rje lcags kyu sogs khros bcas//dkyil 'khor chen po'i tshul gyis ni//go rims bzhin ni ci bzhin no//cho ga rgyas pa ci bzhin bya//khros pa
79b5(rgyud): rdo rje gsor ba dang//khro gnyer 'dzum dang bcas pas 'dud//de nas khros zhing slob dpon che//rdo rje can mchog rab zhugs la//rdo rje hUM mdzad kyis sdig 'dzub//hUM mdzad rab tu sbyar bar bya'o//snyems dang bcas pas
79b6(rgyud): rgyas btab nyid//de bzhin khro bas 'dul ba la//rdo rje 'chang chen dgyes pa yis//bdug pa nyid la rab tu dgyes//de nas me tog blangs nas ni//'khros pa bdag po che la gtor//rdo rje 'bar ba'i lcags kyu ches//yang
79b7(rgyud): dag par ni dgug par bya//oM ma hA badzra dzra la/a na larga ya hi dza/sarba badzra ku la pra pe she ya hUM/sarba mu traM ga nan b+han d+ha na tra ta/sarba saM yan sad+ha ya phaT/sarba duSh+Ta na da ha pa tsa b+ha smi ku ru ma hA kro d+ha ag ni badzre

80b8(rgyud): phyag rgya bcing//bdag nyid nges par bsregs nas ni//ji srid 'di nyid du khro bo chen po'i dngos grub thams cad 'grub par 'gyur//de nas dam tshig bsgrub pa ni//rnam pa bzhir ni rab byed cing//thams cad bde ba'i phyir

84b6(rgyud): oM i sid+hi bu saM b+ha ba sid+ha sa/sid+hi mahA badzra rad na/ba ba Ta Ta traM/'di ni bzlas pa tsam gyis ni//mkha' snying dngos grub thams cad dang//phun tshogs kun la shin du ni//'grub 'gyur 'di la the tshom med

84b8(rgyud): Ta Ta si d+ha ho traM//de nas bcom ldan myur du ni//byon nas dngos grub thams cad dang //'byor pa shin du g.yo ba yi//don ni rab tu 'grub par 'gyur//de nas slob ma phyag rgya ni//me tog bcas pa mchog

86a2(rgyud): khams gsum po//'jig rten gsum po khams gsum po//'jig rte gsum bdag thams cad la//'jig rten gsum mchog 'dul mdzad pa//cho ga bzhin du sgom gyur cing//sems can don phyir cho ga
86a3(rgyud): bzhugs//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rnams//rtag tu rnam par gnas pa yin//mi mnyam pa'i sten gyur na//dug nyid du ni nye bar 'gro//bdud rtsi nyid kyis bsten gyur na//bdud rtsi nyid du 'gyur ba'ang

86a8(rgyud): byang chub snod ma yin//de rnams sbyang phyir bcom ldan 'das//rdo rje 'chang chen rang nyid ni//gzugs ni gsum po 'ad mdzad nas//phyis ni sangs rgyas nyid mi 'gyur//de nas tshangs pa la sogs lha//

86b6(rgyud): 'dir ni ma spyad dam//snying stobs rdul dang mun pa nyid//tshangs pa drag po g.yung drung ste//ming po gsum po 'di nyid ni//nam yang bsnyes par mi bya'o//gang zhig 'di rnams bsgom pa slob//yang dang yang du zhugs

87a1(rgyud): @#//brtan par byed pa'i rgyu yin te//dga' dang phra dog bu med ni//rab tu drag po'i grub kun ster//gang zhig 'jig rten kun du bshad//tshogs kun gyis mchod lha mo rnams//zhi ba 'jigs ma de bzhin gser//

87b1(rgyud): ci bde bar ni rtag tu mchod//sring mo bzhi'i rgyud do//de nas ston pa rdo rje 'dzin//sa'i dbang phyug kun gyi mchod//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug che//bsgrub pa dang ni rab bsgrub byed//'dod chags sogs

87b3(rgyud): yang dag bri//sgo khyud klu'i gdengs ka mchog//mtshams kyi rtse mchog 'jug par byed//de'i dbus su klu rgyal ni//mtha' yas gdengs ka bcas pa ni//klu'i rigs ni brgyad po dag//shes rab can gyis

88b3(rgyud): 'di rnams kyi snying po 'di bshad par bya ste/oM badzra na ra ya/na tshin pra ti ha na/badzra tsakra na ho/ba ga ban badzra ta thA g+ha ta/dza hUM baM ho/badzra d+ha ra ta/badzra ta thA g+ha ta hUM/badzra kro ta mahA yo gi ni gi ni shwa ra

88b6(rgyud): sa na sa te sa ba ka na/oM d+hi shri shu d+he m+li ti dza ya swAhA//badzra pa ra mi/oM badzra yo gi ri mahA b+hir+ya tI lo ga bi dza ya/sha ra badzra kro ta a/ga sa ma ye/badzra d+ha ri ni maM suM ti/tad+ya thA/oM hi li mi li le badzra
88b7(rgyud): ka mu ka ye swAhA//badzra sa ma ye na ma ye na sa bye na ba ti/bud+ha bo d+hi/ra nud ta re/badzra d+harma pra ye ke na sa te na sid maM/oM tu ra te ra tud ta re swAhA//badzra d+ha mo ta/oM kam badzra ni sid r+ya sid r+ya shud d+ha ya sa b+ha ba
88b8(rgyud): ta ga ga nod b+ha ba/ga ga ni b+hi shud d+he/sarba a pi pra ya sa ri pu ra ni swAhA//phyi'i dkyil 'khor gnas rnams su//rdo rje 'dzin pa rang phyogs gnas//lha dbang dang ni ma mo nyid//'jig rten skyong rnams dgod par bya/
89a1(rgyud): @#//chogs kyi dbang phyug rdo rje yi//rol mo che chung brdung byed pa//phyi'i dkyil 'khor kha khyer dang//sgo dang 'tshamasu'ang de 'dra'o//sgo rnams kun du 'jug pa ni//rang gi phyag rgya rab tu gzhug//
89a2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor dam par lnga pa ni//dang po'i sbyor bas 'jug par bya'o//de nas dbus su 'dug nas ni//dang po lcags kyu che de nyid//snying po 'di ni brjod par bya//'gugs pa 'di ni brjod par bya//
89a3(rgyud): su kha badzra sa ta sid+hi hUM/a hUM hri traM/de nas gsang ba chen po yis//snying po bzang po 'di brjod pas//rdo rje 'chang chen rgyal po//sarba ta thA ga ta na sa ma ya/a ka ra sha ya dza/badzra ku la/d+ha baM/da ma yid
89a4(rgyud): twa tra be sha ya hUM/sarba lo ge ra ram pra be sha ye b+han d+ha na baM/ya ma sarba rad na di baM/ma hA ma ni ra dza naM/ba shi ku ru ho/hUM A hUM hri traM/de na gsang ba chen po yis//snying po bzang po 'di brjodapas

89a6(rgyud): grub kun gyis grub par 'gyur//de nas go rims ci bzhin du//dkyil 'khor thams cad bsgrub pa dang//dngos grub de rnams nyid dang ni//thams cad kyang ni bsgrub par bya//bde ba thams cad mi 'gyur gnas
89a7(rgyud): bde ba chen po zhes brjod do//rdo rje 'di ni grub lasogs//'di ni tshe 'dir 'grub par 'gyur//de ltar sna tshogs pa'i don sbyang ba'i don gyis cho ga 'gro ba thams cad kyis dpag tu med pa'i 'dul ba thams cad
89a8(rgyud): sna tshogs pa//mnyam pa med pa'i phul du byung ba/de bzhin gshegs pa lasogs pa'i rigs thams cad 'dzin pa'i bde ba'i mchog byed pa/rgyal ba thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs pa/srid pa'i bde ba
89b1(rgyud): mchog byed pa gsungs pa yin no//dpal mchog dang po rtogs pa'i rgyal po las;rtog pa thams cad rgyud phyi ma'i rtsa ba'i rgyud kun gyi mchog dbang phyug dam pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi cho ga rgyas pa rdzogs 
89b2(rgyud): s+ho////sangs rgyas kun gyi gsang ba che//dam pa mchog gi grub pa dang//bde ba chen po'i gzhal yas khang//dngos grub kun gyi lus byas pa//de bzhin gshegs mchog chen po yi;
89b3(rgyud): rin chen rgyal po sogs kyis brgyan//bde ba chen po chags pa yi//klu dang rol mo brjod chags pa//bla med dga' ba chen po yi//mchod pa kun gyis rab tu mchod//dga' ba chen po dpal gyi gnas 
89b4(rgyud): rang gi bzhugs gnas mchog de la//rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po mchog//mi 'gyur mchog ni yang dag bzhugs//rdo rje sems dpa' bdag rang nyid//rab du dga' ba mi 'gyur mchog//kun du
89b5(rgyud): bzang po 'khor ba dang//byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs//dngos grub chen po kun gyi mchog//rdo rje snyems pa bdag pI bdag//kun mchog chags pa chen po'i//dngos grub dbang
89b6(rgyud): phyug rjes grub pa//klu dang rol mo thog mtha' med//mchog ni bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs//hUM/de nas 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//rdo rje thams cad 'dzin pa ni//gsang ba lnga'i bdag nyid che//
89b7(rgyud): phyag rgya'i lha mo 'byung bar 'gyur//rtag tu chags dang dngos grub che//rnam rgyal dbang phyug lhag pa lha//phyag rgya dam pa bzhi pa ste//sems dpa' byin gyis brlabs pa'i sku//de rnams kyi snying po 'di yinate
89b8(rgyud): kun mchog so sor rtog pa dang//dngos grub kun gyi bde ba'i mchog//kun mchog rnam rgyal mthong ba 'am//thams cad dbang phyug mchod pa che//dngos grub rnams dang phun tshogs dang//dga' ba dag 
90a1(rgyud): @#//dang bdag po rnams//rdo rje lha mo thog mtha' med//rdo rje sems dpa' sku'i mchog ces bya'o//de nas mchog tu gsang ba'i mi rnams kyis//dga' ba chen po dpal rdo rje don yod dga' ba'i dam tshig
90a2(rgyud): gis 'dod pa'i rdo rje ma dang/dga' ba chen po dpal don yod la chags pa'i dam tshig gi rdo rje ki li ki li dang/dga' ba chen po dbal rdo rje don yod la chags pa'i dan tshig rnam par rgyal ba'i rdo rje ma dang/dga'
90a3(rgyud): ba chen po dpal rdo rje donayod la chags pa'i dam chig rdo rje 'dod pa'i dbang phyug ma'o//'di rnams gsang ba'i mtshan dag ni//mkhas pas gang gi dad pas brjod//de ni phyag rgya thams cad kyi//dngos grub kun
90a4(rgyud): bdag bdag por 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' mchog tu gsang ba gsungs pa yin no//de nas 'di sgrub bshad par bya//dngos grub shin tu rtag pa ste//sdig pa che la dga' ba
90a5(rgyud): dang//grub pa gang zhig ma grub kyang//rdo rje sems dpas gsungs pa bzhin//de dag kyang ni myur 'grub 'gyur//nyin re 'am ni zla re 'am//de bzhin lo re re re la//ci mngon mngon 'dod 'grub
90a6(rgyud): par bya//de rnyed pa dang bcas bde bas//rang sal bcom ldan bri bar bya//kun bdag dam tshig dbang phyug ste//gang zhig bris par gyur na ni//mchog tu rtag pa'i thar pa thob//bzhad pa dang bcas bzlas pI
90a7(rgyud): 'od//sna tshogs pad ma'i 'od 'bar ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi dbang bskur sogs//sna tshogs brgyan dang na bzar ldan//sems dpa'i dkyil mo dkrung gis bzhugs//rdo rje snyems pa'i mchog 'chang ba//
90a8(rgyud): g.yon pa dril bu mchog snyems pa//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu rtag//de yaM logs na bzhugs pa rnams//de'i 'od kyis dkyil 'khor rten//rab tu dga' dang bde ba'i sku//gsang ba chen po dgod par bya//dang po
90b1(rgyud): pad ma ltar dkar dmar//gzhan ni sa la sha ltar dkar//gzhan ni ud pal 'dab ltar sngo//rdo rje snyems ma ser po 'dzin//dang po dang ni 'dzin dag dang//g.yas kyi logs la brten pa ste//gsum pa dang ni bzhi
90b2(rgyud): pa dag//g.yon gyi logasu bzhugs pa yi//rdo rje ki li ki li ma//dbus kyi rang lha dges pa spro//de'i log la rdo rje che//mda' ni gsor ba la brtson pa//snyems pa'i phyag gis yongs mkhyud pa'i//chu srin
90b3(rgyud): rgyal mtshan sgreng dang bcas//de yi logasu bzhad dang bcas//mchod pas bdag po la brdeg pa//sangs rgyas rin chen dbang bskur mtshan//thams cad spyan gyis phyag rgya gzigs//rang la bdag ni thugs btad pa
90b4(rgyud): ras ris 'di ni mi thong ba yis//rtag pa'i bsod nams thob par 'gyur//mchod pa dang ni rtags pa yis//mchog tu dga' ba'i thar pa thob//zhes bya ba bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas gsungaso
90b5(rgyud): de ltar mchog tu dga' ba rab tu 'dod pa'i mchog tu dga' ba dag pa dam pa'i dngos grub 'gyur//gsal zhing rnam par rgyal ba'i ras ris chen po 'di/thams cad gzigs rnams mchod pa chen po'i dpal
90b6(rgyud): 'dir rol//'khor lo de'i rtags byas nas//mchog du gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor 'dir//rnam par dben pa'i khang pa 'am//yang na skyed mos tshal sogasu//de bzhin gshegs mchog chen po yi//ras ris
90b7(rgyud): sogs kyi brgyan//dril bu dang bcas yid dga' ba//bla re phul bas mchog gyur pa'i//rdo rje klu'i mchod sogs dang//klu dang rol mos rnam 'phrul dang//me tog spos sogs rab 'byor pa//mar me'i spyon pas
90b8(rgyud): yang dag mchod//khang pa sum rtseg dkyil 'khor du//mchog gi gsang ba grub par bya//yang na kho na bdag nyid mchog//rang gi lhag pa'i lhar bsgom bya//de nas 'khor ni ma bzhugs kyang//rang nyid kyis
91a1(rgyud): @#//ni rtags bzhugs nas//rdo rje kun 'chang slob dpon ni//dngos grub kun la 'grub par 'gyur//rang nyid khrus byas dri zhim sgos//ji snyed brgyan dang gos dang ldan//sna tshogs gos kyis
91a2(rgyud): yang dag klubs//phreng bcas kha ni dri zhim pa//rdo rje sems dpa' sems dpa' yis//me tog spos dang dri snod bcas//rdo rje klu ni 'di dag gis//gar byed grong khyer 'jug par bya;;de nas
91a3(rgyud): mchog du gsang ba'i dpal//dam tshig gsum 'jug pa la mchog du gsang ba'i klu'i mchod pa mchog du grub pa 'di yin te/dngos grub kun gyi bde ba nyams dga' ba'i//thar pa'i grong du bcom
91a4(rgyud): ldan bde chen 'jug//mchog tu bde ba'i dam pa'i dngos grub kyis//dza hUM baM ho/rab tu grub par mdzod//rab tu bzhug te g.yon pa yis//rdo rje mig gis mgon la 'dun//g.yon pa snyes
91a5(rgyud): pa cung zad bzhad//bstod cing mdzes pa'i mig btsugs pa//de la mchod du gsang ba dam pa'i dpal dam tshig gsum gyi phyag rgya 'tshal ba'i me tog gi sil ma dor ba'i mchog tu 
91a6(rgyud): gsang ba rdo rje klu'i mchod pa mchog tu grub pa 'di yin te/mchog du bde ba'i sems ni rol par ldan//sgeg pa'i phyag gis bcom ldan 'das la 'dud//dza hUM baM ho//he he he he he/mgon po snyem pa'i
91a7(rgyud): me tog bzhesu gsol//de nas me tog sil ma gtor//rdo rje sems dpa'i dbang bskur bas//rdo rje dril bu'i phyag rgya dang //ldan pa'i don bsal byin par bya//de la mchog tu bsad pa dam pa'i
91a8(rgyud): dpal dam tshig gsum gyi rdo rje sems dpa' dbang bskur ba'i mchog tu gsang ba'i rdo rje'i klu'i dag pa mchog tu sgrub pa 'di yin te/oM/sangs rgyas kun ldan rin chen mchog gis brgyan//lha'i cod
91b1(rgyud): pa na dang bcas khyod la 'dud//rdo rje 'chang bdag rin chen dbang bskur bas//rdo rje sems dpa' sa ma ya sto/ho/de nyid kyis ni rdo rje bzung//chos kyi dril bu dkrol bar bya//dam tshig gis ni phyag rgya dkrol;
91b2(rgyud): byin gyis brlabs nas snying po bzlas//de la sogs pa thob bzung bas//rdo rje sems dpa'i gnyis pa 'dra//rdo rje thams cad 'dzin rgyal po//rdo rje can gyis byin brlabs 'gyur//zhes bya ba mchog tu 
91b3(rgyud): gsang ba dam pa'i dpal dam tshig gsum gyis bdag la byin gyis brlabs pa bsgrub pa'i grub pa gcig bu'i cho ga mchog du grub pa bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin sems dpa' gsungaso//de nas 'dir
91b4(rgyud): bsgrub bshad bya ba//dngos grub shin du rtag pa yang//gang gis bsgrub po dam pa dang//sdig chen byed pa rnams kyang ni//rdo rje sems dpa' gsungs pa bzhin//ma bsgrubs rnams kyang
91b5(rgyud): 'grub par 'gyur//lugs ma 'am ni bdung bam 'am//'dus byas pa 'am bris pa 'am//dang po'i sems dpa' bzhugs gsol nas//de'i mdun du cho ga bzhin//rtag tu rdo rje dril
91b6(rgyud): 'chang zhing//dngos grub mchog ni grub par bya//thams cad dgug dang mchod pa che//bdag byin brlab sogs bsgrub rnams so//de ci ltar mchog du gsang ba dam pa'i dpal ngam tshig gsum gyi
91b7(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad kyi mchog dgug pa'i rgyud yin/rang lhag lha'i sbyor ba las/thams cad dgug pa'i sbyor ba yis//yan lag mchog gi rol bcas pa//gsang bya zhing ni dgug par bya//de nas de
91b8(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchog gi dngos grub mchog du gsang ba'i dam tshig gi klu'i mchod pa 'di yin te/dza/mchog dang po rdo rje 'chang//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba rab rtag rab tu dga' ba khyod
92a1(rgyud): @#//bdag sangs rgyas so sor rtogs//hUM /mchog rdo rje las ni mchog tu mi 'gyur zad med srid pa rab bsgrub bde dang yid bde dam tshig bdag la deng grub mdzod//baM mchog bdag gi sku 'dzin rnal
92a2(rgyud): 'byor thams cad rnam rgyal bsgreng ba rab mchog dag pa bde bar 'gyur ba'i pad ma'i rgyal po kya//ho rab mchog ni mchog gi dngos grub dam pa thams cad dbang phyug dam par bdag gyur cing//rtag bdag
92a3(rgyud): 'phel ba namakha'i snying po khyod//hu lu hu lus bsgrubs pas 'bad par mdzad//dam tshig chen po yang dag dgug pa khyod//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi dam tshig ste//rab tu dga' ba mchog bdag po//
92a4(rgyud): de la gsang ba dam pa'i dpal dam tshig gsum gyi dngos grub thams cad kyi mchog chen po'i cho ga ji ltar yin//rang gi lha'i rnal 'byor ji bzhin du//mchod pa kun mchog sku rnams kyis//mchodade
92a5(rgyud): sngags pas klu rnams kyis//rol mo'i rnam 'phrul gyis rjes mchod//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchog gi dngos grub kyi mchod pa chen po'i cho ga'i mchog tu gsang ba'i dam tshig gi
92a6(rgyud): rdo rje klu'i mchog pa ni/'di yin te/thams cad rjesu chags pa bde ba dam pa'i thugs//rdo rje sems dpa' khyod ni rab dga' yin//bde ba chen po gsal mthong bdag la brten gyur cig//myur du so sor khyen nas
92a7(rgyud): rab tu 'dud pa bsgrubs//de la mchog tu gsang ba dam pa'i dpal//dam tshig gsum gyi dngos gru ba thams cad byin gyis brlabs pa'i bsgrub pa chen po ji ltar yin//rang lha'i sbyor ba ji bzhin du//phyag rgya kun
92a8(rgyud): gyis byin brlabs pa//de nyid chen po'i mchog gis ni//rang nyid bsgrub ste snying po bzla//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi mchog gi dngos grub byin gyis brlabs pa'i gsang ba chen po'i dam tshig gi
92b1(rgyud): rdo rje klu'i mchod pa mchog tu grub pa 'di yin te/oM/namakha' skye ba mtshan ldan phyir//thog mtha' tha ma med pa'i mchog//sems dpa' rdo rje che rang bzhin//rdo rje sems dpar bdag grub mdzad//sku mchog
92b2(rgyud): dngos grub chen po ste//dbang phyug chen po lhag pa'i lha//rdo rje 'dzin pa kun gyi rgyal//mi 'gyur mchog tu bdag grub mdzad//nyes pa mi mnga' rtag pa khyod//'dod chags kyi ni rjes chags pa//'dod
92b3(rgyud): chags chen po dga' ba che//bcom ldan de nyid kyis grub mdzad//shin du dag pa kun gyi mchog//gdod nas grol ba de bzhin gshegs//kun du bzang po kun bdag nyid//byang chub sems dpa' bdag
92b4(rgyud): grub mdzad//dngos grub chen po kun gyi mchog//dbang phyug chen po lha pa'i lha//grub pa'i rdo rje cher bstod pa'i//rdo rje snyems bdag bdag mdzad cig//dpal mchog dang po'i bcom ldan khyod//
92b5(rgyud): gang gis don dang klu'i bstod//de ni dngos grub kun gyi mchog//dbang phyag chen po nyid grub 'gyur//zhes bya ba mchog gi de kho na nyid kyis mchog gsang ba'i klu chen po mchog du grub pa
92b6(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang gis gsungs so//dngos grub cho ga 'di yis ni//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag rtogs//kun mchog dngos grub chen po'o//dbang phyug nyid ni rab bsgrub po//kun nas 'od zer
92b7(rgyud): ston dang sogs//so sor ston pa'i rmad byung rnams//rdo rje tshig gi rang ltas las//dor 'tshar gyur nas mthong bar 'gyur//rtog pa kun gyi rnal 'byor che//dngos grub dbang phyug bde rnams kyi//myur du
92b8(rgyud): rang gi byin gyis brlabs//rdo rje slob dpon gyis thob po//ces bya ba bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' mchog dang po gsungaso//de nas mchog tu gsang ba'i dpal dam tshig gsum gyi lha thams cad
93a1(rgyud): @#//kyi dag la byin gyis brlabs pa'i bsgrub pa'i rgyud ji ltar yin//rang lha rnal 'byor ci bzhin du lha kun bdag nyid sku rnams kyis/lha'i rnal 'byor nyid kyis/rang lhag lha'i nyid bsgrub par bya
93a2(rgyud): de nas mchog du gsang ba dam pa'i dam tshig gsum gyi kun mchog dngos grub chen po'i dbang phyug chen po nyid lasogs pa'i dngos grub thams cad kyi las kyi tshogs mchog tu mi 'gyur ba dam pa'i bla na
93a3(rgyud): med pa'i dga' ba dang/rab tu dga' ba dang/bde ba dang/yid bde ba dang/thams cad kyi dngos grub dam pa'i cho ga rgyas pa ni rjesu bsgrub pa ni 'di yin te/rang lha'i rnal 'byor ci bzhin du//rdo rje sku
93a4(rgyud): mchog sku rnams kyi//klu dang rol mo'i cho ga ni//rdo rje nyid kyis rjes chags 'gyur//rang lha'i sbyor ba ji bzhin du//dbyangs kyi rtsa bas rnam sad pa//klu dang rol mo cho ga ni//dril bu nyid kyi rjesu brjod//

93a6(rgyud): sad pas//rang gi lha'i dam tshig gi//thad zhi gyur 'grub par 'gyur//mchog du gsang ba dam pa'i dpal//dam tshig gsum gyi klu chen po//rol mo'i cho ga mchog du gsang//mchog tu grub
93a7(rgyud): pa bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' gsungaso//de nas rdo rje gnas ci bzhin du//ci snyed pa dang ci bde bas//me tog spos kyis mchod nas ni//khang pa 'am bskyed mos tshal so gsu//bdag nyid
93a8(rgyud): ci 'dod rab sgrub na//ci snyed brgyan dang gos dang ldan//sna tshogs gos kyis yang dag klubs//me tog phreng bcas kha dri zhim//lugs ma 'am ni brdungs ma 'am//'dus pa 'am ni ri mo bris//
93b1(rgyud): dang po'i 'khor lo de 'dun du//cho ga bzhin du bzhugs nas gsol//rang lha'i rnal 'byor ji bzhin du//rdo rje dril bu rab sad pas//thams cad dgug dang mchod pa che//bdag byin brlabs sogs sgrub par
93b2(rgyud): bya//sngon du lan cig bsgrub byas nas//de nas ci 'dod rdo rje pa//ci 'dod pa dang ci smra ba//thams cad za zhing thams cad zad//langs pa 'am ni 'dug pa 'am//rab tu dged dam smra ba 'am//ci bzhin
93b3(rgyud): de bzhin de dang der//ci 'dod par ni de bzhin gzhag//gsang ba mchog gi 'khor lo ni//me tog sogs kyis mchod nas ni//ci bzhin du ni rjes grub bya//'dis ni 'dod pa kun dbang phyug//'gro dang
93b4(rgyud): 'ong dang 'dod na 'phur//dngos grub kun gyi dbang phyug 'grub//rga shi med pa'i gtso bor 'gyur//rdo rje sems dpa' grub chen po//phyag rgya kun gyi dbang phyug bdag//rdo rje kun 'dzin
93b5(rgyud): rgyal po ni//rdo rje mtshon cha thams cad 'dzin//mi thub pa dang mi gsod pa//thams cad byed cing kun gyi brten//sems can kun gyi rgyal po che//tshar gcod rjes su bzung bar nus//
93b6(rgyud): kun mchog rjes su chags pa che//'dod pa grub pa bde ba'i mchog//kun las rnam rgyal grub 'gyur te//kun mchog dbang phyug dam pa yin//dngos grub chen po kun gyi mchog//dbang phyug chen po'i
93b7(rgyud): go 'phang gnas//rgyal po rdo rje kun 'dzin pa//sangs rgyas thaMd rab grub 'gyur//de ltar mchog tu rjes chags dga' ba pad ma'i rab che//mchog du gsang ba grub pa'i bla na med pa'i dngos grub rtsol
93b8(rgyud): mchog gi rgyal mtshan bsgrengs pas gang zhig rjes su bstan pa ste//mchog gi dngos grub dbang phyug chen po nyid des nyams myong pa//zhes bya ba btsom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//
94a1(rgyud): @#//dpal mchog dang po las gsang ba chen po dam pa'i dam tshig gsum dang/dngos grub thams cad dgug pa dang/mchod pa chen po dang/bdag la byin gyis brlabs pa lasogs pa grub pa'i
94a2(rgyud): cho ga rgyas pa'o//gsang ba'i mchog gi dges pa dang//sangs rgyas kyi rjes ston dang//rdo rje sems dpa' rang gi gnas//thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs//thog mtha' med pa'i sems dpa' ni//
94a3(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' dga' ba che//kun du bzang po kun bdag nyid//rdo rje snyems tshul bdag po'i bdag//'di ni rang byung bcom ldan 'das//lhag pa'i lha ni gcig pu nyid//ces bya ba 'grub par 'gyur ro//
94a4(rgyud): de ltar mchog tu rjes chags dga' bar pad ma che//mchog tu gsang ba bla na med pa'i dngos grub rtsol//mchog gi rgyal mtshan bsgreng ba'i gang zhig rjesu ston pa ste//mchog gi dngos grub
94a5(rgyud): bde ba chen po nyid du nyams myong ba//zhes bya ba bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' gsungaso//dpal mchog dang po las dga' ba chen po'i dpal rdo rje don yod pa'i dga' ba'i dpal dam
94a6(rgyud): tshig rnal 'byor thams cad kyi dbang phyug de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dpal rang gi dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor mchog gsang ba yongasu dag pa rdzogas+ho////de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd daM

94a8(rgyud): gsang ba'i dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor sngags lan brjod pas dpyod du bcug ste/oM sarba yo ga tsid ta/u ba ta ya mi/'di brjod pa tsam gyis de bzhin gshegs pa thad dang mi 'bral zhing phyir mi ldog pa
94b1(rgyud): dang ;bdud thams cad zil gyis gnon pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad nyid du yang rtogs par bya'o zhes bya ba yang gsungaso//byang chub sems ni bla med pa 
94b2(rgyud): me skyes pa ni byas nas ni//snying khar rdo rje rab bzhag nas//su ra ta sa ma ya staM ho//snying pos rdo rje ci bder bsgrubs//'di yis rdo rje bskul ba ni//'dod pa kun las grub par 'gyur//de nyid bskul
94b3(rgyud): bas myur du ni//byang chub sems ni bla na med//kun mchog dngos grub chen po phyir//rdo rje sems dpa' nyid du 'gyur//zhes bya ba'i mchog bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' gsungaso//gar byas spyi bor
94b4(rgyud): thal mo sbyar//gos kyi gdong ni g.yogs pa rnams//ci 'dod dngos grub 'dod pa yi//slob ma thams cad bzhug par bya//ci 'dod dngos grub sems dpa' yis//slob ma me tog dor bzhug bya//phyag
94b5(rgyud): rgya gang la de babs pa'o//de de'i lha mo bsgrub par bya//de n+sa gdod g.yogs dgrol byas la//rnam pa kun gyi yon blangs nas//sangs rgyas rin chen dbang bskur gyis//dam tshig gsum n-i
94b6(rgyud): gsal bar byin//de la rdo rje chen po'i man chus dbang bskur te/'di ltar brjod par bya'o//'di ni khyod kyi dmyal ba'i chu//dam tshig 'das nas bsreg par byed//dam tshig bsrungs na dngos grub ni//rdo rje bdud
94b7(rgyud): rtsi chu 'di bsgrubs//rdo rje dril bu'i phyag rgya ni//gal te dkyil 'khor ma thod smras//skye bo 'dul ba'i nang na gnas//dad pa med pas bzhad bgad smod//zhes bya bas mna' bsgeg go//de la sangs rgyas kyi
94b8(rgyud): rin po che'i dbang bskur ba'i cho ga 'di yin te//rdo rje chu yis dbang bskur nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku'i mchog dbang bskur ba yis//gnasu gzhag ste/oM sarba ta thA g+ha ta ma hA sa ma ya
95a1(rgyud): @#//a ti pa ti stoM//a b+hi shin+tsa maM/mahA badzra d+ha ra zhes bya ba'i sngags 'dis rdo rje lan du byin par bya'o/'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad nyid//rdo rje 'chang gi rdo rje ste//rdo rje 'chang gi brtul zhugs che//khyod
95a2(rgyud): kyis kyang ni rtag tu bcad//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi//byang chub mchog ni sems chen po//yongasu mi spang bar grub pa rtag tu shin du gsang ba de 'di yin te//oM badzra karma/d+hi d+hiM shri/badzra na naM/
95a3(rgyud): sa ma ya staM/b+hur bu swA//gang la rdo rje'i ming btags pa de la kye'i sgra dang por sbyar bar bya'o//de'i ming yang ci srid byang chub snying po'i bar mthar thug pa'i bar gyi de bzhin gshegs pa yin yang ming du
95a4(rgyud): 'gyuro//de la mchog tu gsang ba dam pa'i dpal dam tshig gsum sbyin pa ni 'di yin te/thog mtha' med pa'i sems dpa' ni//rdo rje sems dpa' dga' ba che//kun du bzang po kun bdag
95a5(rgyud): nyid//rdo rje snyems tshul bdag po'i bdag//zhes bya ba bcom ldan 'das dpal mchog dang po'i skyes bu mchog gi de kho na nyid kyi gsang ba lnga'i de kho na nyid bshad par byas nas/rdo rje sbyin par
95a6(rgyud): bya'o//'dis bcom ldan 'das ci srid pa'i bdag po dam pa kun gyi mchog//dbang phyug gi dam pa de bzhin gshegs pa nyid chos kyi mchog gi bka' yang dag par rtsol bar mdzad pa'i srid pa rang bzhin nyid kyis dag//

95b1(rgyud): bsgrub par byed pa dang/dbang du byed pa dang/rab dang bar byed zhes bya ba bsgrub par 'gyuro//de nas srid pa dag pa thabs kyi mchog//rtog pa thams cad kyi gsang ba chen po dam tshig la rtag tu bsten par bya'o

95b6(rgyud): 'gyur ba'i tshe//ji srid mtshungs med sems can don//mya ngan mi 'da' byed par nus//gsang ba dam pa dam tshig thabs//bsod nams byin gyis brlabs grub pa//rtag tu dngos grub

107a1(rgyud): mo'i mchod pa sogs kyi 'gon la mtsho de//phul ba de yis rdo rje bla ma che//'dod pas bdag dang gzhan rnams mchod par bya//myos byas myos byed btung ba ni//ci 
107a2(rgyud): 'dod par ni bsten pa yis//rdo rje nyi ma me 'bar spogs//ci bzhin rjes 'gro'i bzlas pa bzla//rang sang cho gas blangs pa'i//klu dang rol mo gar rnams dang //sna tshogs rgya'i mchod pa dang 
107a3(rgyud): khams gsum rnam rgyal la sogs pa//bud med tshogs dang bcas pa'i//mchod pa rgya chen thams cad dang //ci 'dod pa'i longs spyod kyis//mchod pa byang na rab tu grub//zhes bya ba mchod du

107a7(rgyud): yang ni 'jig med byed//drag po dbang po da nye dbang zla//nyi ma mkha' 'gro tshogs kyis 'khor//khros nas yi ge hU~M tsam gyis//ma lus par ni thal byed//rnal 'byor mkha' 'gro ma'i 
107a8(rgyud): dkyil 'khor//mchog tu zhugs te mthong nas ni//sdig pa kun las rnam grol ste//khyed rnams dang ni rab tu gnas//slar khyed 'chi ba yod min te//khyed bar ni ngan 'gro med//
107b1(rgyud): gzhan gyis mi thub bsod cing//cis kyang mi 'jigs dga' bar gyis//shin du srid pa'i dngos grub rnams //ston pa'i mchog du gyur pa yis//dam tshig sdom pa grub pa 'di//rtag du yang ni srung bar
107b2(rgyud): bya//sangs rgyas kun gyis 'thun par gsungs//dam pa'i rtag pa'i bka' yin no//byang chub sems ni spang mi bya//dam tshig de bzhin dam tshig dang//slob dpon smad par mi bya ste/
107b3(rgyud): gang phyir rdo rje 'dzin kun te//sems can kun la phan phyir dang//theg pa thams cad grub pa'i phyir//gdug pa 'dul ba dag la zhes//rdo rje mchog ni 'dzin pas gsungs//shin
107b4(rgyud): du gdug cing gtum pa dang//bdag po grogs po bu rnams kyang//phyag rgya'i rnal 'byor mngon spyod kyis//shin du gnyid ni log par bzhug//rtag tu rab gi dam tshig srung/rtag

107b6(rgyud): nams tshogs yin te/tshogs las mngos grub mchog du 'gyur//dong skyed ba 'bras bur bcas/gang phyir 'di la rab tu gnas/dam tshig rgyal po dang mnyam

108a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sbyor thob nas khyod grub yin//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpas gsungs so//dpal mchog dang po las//mchog tu gsang ba'i rdo rje rnal 'byor gyi//mkha' 
108a2(rgyud): 'gro ma'i dkyil 'khor gi cho ga mchog grub pa'o//de nas 'phags pa spyan sa ras gzigs kyi dbang phyug gi mchog tu gsang ba 'gro ba 'dul ba'i cho ga ni//ras la bcom ldan 'das bri bar bya//bdag 
108a3(rgyud): mar gar gi dbang phyug gtso//glog gi 'khor lo yongs 'gyur ba'i//'khor ba'i rnam 'phrul gar rnams kyis//pad ma laswogs phyag 'tshal bri//phyag rgya 'khor lo ltar mdzad pa//de'i logs
108a4(rgyud): ni thams cad la//khro gnyer la sogs bri bar bya//drag mo laswogs phyag mtshan gzugs//pad ma'i lha mo pad mar bcas//dkyil 'khor der ni brtags byas nas//gsang ba'i dkyil

108a6(rgyud): dag pa'i//rol mo ba'i gar ni mdzad pa yin//zhes bya ba'i ni mchog gi rdo rje ste//ci ltar pad ma dmar ma po 'di//gnas kyi nye bar sna tshogs kyi//mtshon gyi nges pas mi gos pa//de bzhin

108b1(rgyud): namakha'i snying po'i tha don thams cad grub pa'i cho ga ste//nyi ma'i dkyil bri nas ni //rangs ris de nas blo gros che //ljang ku be du rgya ltar mtshungs //namakha'i snying po bri bar bya//de'i pad

108b5(rgyud): de yang 'dod par grub pa'i phyir//'dod las thar ba bde ba che/zhes bya'o//sbyin min rnams ni sbyin bya'o//'dod pa kun ni bde ba rnams //bdag nyid dang ni gzhan rnams la//bsam
108b6(rgyud): pa thams cad rdzogs phyir ro//zhes bya ba'i tshig go/dam tshig 'di yis mchog du ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba che//myur du yang dag dam pa mchog/mi 'gyur mchog ni grub par
108b7(rgyud): mdzad//de phar ltar gsang ba mchog gi tshul dag ni ci bzhin cho ga mchog du rgyas pa ste//mchog chen des ni rtog pa thams cad kyi//snying po khyed par dngos grub par 'gyur//dpal mchog dang po
108b8(rgyud): las rtog pa thams cad kyi grub pa'i cho ga'o//de nas grub chen thams cad kyi//sgrub pa'i mchog ni bde ba che//rdo rje sems dpa' phyag rgya che//rtog pa kun mchog grub pa'i phyir
109a1(rgyud): @#//snod ma yin pa'i sems can ni//sdig pa thams cad byed rnams la//mthong ba la swogs sems can don//sems can chen pos mdzad pa min//mthong 
109a2(rgyud): med long ba de rnams ni//bsod nams med swogs rtogs ldan la//rdo rje sems swogs sems dpa' mchog/mthong ba'i thabs kyis rgyun rnams ni//rdo rje sems swogs 
109a3(rgyud): sems dpa' mchog/sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje yis//dam tshig mchog gi bcu+ing pa ste//dam tshig 'da' bar dka' ba'o//rdo rje sems swogs sams dpa'i//gang phyir sku ni brtan pa

109a7(rgyud): rdo rje 'dzin de ltar bskal pa bye bas kyang //thob par mi 'gyur myur du ni//ci ltar phyag rgya mchog bzung ba'o//ci ltar 'dul ba ldan 'jig rten ni//de ltar dngos grub gsungs pa yin//de'i thabs

109b1(rgyud): mdzad zhes bya'o//de nas 'di gsungs so//nas bcom ldan 'das kyis mkhas shing mchog du phra ba dang/zab pa dang rgya che ba dang/bsam gyi mi khyab pa mchog dag pa thog ma dang tha ma med pa//mi zad 
109b2(rgyud): cing zad pa med pa//don yod cing thogs pa med pa//shin du mchog tu dga' ba ma lus pa'i bde ba chen po dang//yid mi bde ba ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sems can gyi khams dngos grub chen
109b3(rgyud): po'i dbang phyug chen po mchog du 'gyur pa'i dngos grub thams cad kyi mchog rdo rje dbab pa rnams kyi rab tu sang bar mdzad nas//bde ba chen po'i rdo rje gsang ba'i don yon pa'i mchog gi gsang ba
109b4(rgyud): zhes bya ba'i mtshon cha mchog 'di mchog du gsang ba'i cho ga rgyud phyi ma'i mchog byin gyis rlabs par mdzad pa'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dam tshig gi bdag

109b6(rgyud): bzlas//kun gyi phyag rgya rab grub pa'i//bdag byin brlab pa cig pus grub//ces bya ba ni mchog du grub pa'o//de nas mchog tu grub gsang ba bdag la byin gyis rlabs cig bu grub 
109b7(rgyud): pa'i cho ga mchog du grub pa ni//lan cig dam tshig gsum mchog du gsang ba dam pa'i khang par ni//rdo rje la swogs thob bzung na//rdo rje can gyis byin rlabs 'gyur//de nas shin
109b8(rgyud): du mchog du ba'i bdag la byin gyis rlab pa cig pu grub pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'i khyad pa'i shin du mchog tu grub pa ni rdo rje dril bu kun bdag nyid//sna tshogs phyag rgya'i skur bdag ldan//phyag rgya
110a1(rgyud): @#/ O /kun gyi rnal 'byor bdag/rdo rje sems dpa' nyid du 'gyur//grub pa'i cho ga 'di yis ni//rdo rje sems dpa' yang dag bsdus//kun mchog dngos grub chen po'i//

110a3(rgyud): byin rlabs don yod phyir//myur du 'bad med dngos grub rnams //las kyi tshogs kyi grub pa'i//dngos grub kun myur 'grub par 'gyur//'di yis cung zad mi grub med //dngos grub

110a7(rgyud): gi brtson pa yin zhe na//smra ba rang gi lhag pa'i lha rjes su dran par sbyor bas rnal 'byor ro//slar yang gzhan gyi ni yang dag par bsdus pa dang//yang dag par kun du sbyor ba dang//yang dag

110b6(rgyud): lhag pa'i lha'i ni gang gi//phyag rgya gang dang gang bcing ba//de dang de'i rnal 'byor gyis//phyag rgya de dang de'i sbyor bya zhes bya'o//de nas mchog du gsang ba'i rab mchog gi rjes su sgrub pa'o//cho ga
110b7(rgyud): rgyas pa mchog du shin du grub pa ni//bdag ni sangs rgyas thams cad nyid//gzi brjid can ni nyid kyi nga yin//rang gi lha'i rnal 'byor gyis//de phyir bdag nyid brtag par bya//phyag

112a7(rgyud): nye bar mi bzad pas//sdug sngal ldan pas myur du skams//sdug sngal gyi sems can g.yengs 'gyur//rnal 'byor la ni sbyor ba'is//de phyir ci 'dod pa'i la spyod pa//kun zad bzhin
112a8(rgyud): thams cad byed//ci 'dod bya ba la sbyor ba//ci 'dod par ni spyod par spyod//gang dang gang du 'chags pa dang//rgod pa 'am ni smra ba 'am//gang dang de ru ci bzhin de//dkyil 'khor

178b3(rgyud): dkyil 'khor byung ste//dkyil 'khor zlum po'i dkyil 'khor gyi gra'i srad bu'i dbus su dbang phyug chen po bri//der gzhan dag kyang rim gyis thams cad du bri 'am//gzhag pa 'am thar na mtshon gyi thig le yang gzhago/
178b4(rgyud): de nas thog mar slob dpon gyis gsum pa'i rtog gi phyag rgyas bcings la 'jug go/g.yon logs nas rdo rje slob ma yang bcug ste/de nas phyag rgya yang sgrub po//de phyin chad thams cad 'grub po//cing 'jig rten

178b8(rgyud): par tshad du byas la 'god do//de nas slob dpon bdud rtsi 'khyil pa'i phyag rgyas bzhugs nas phyag rgya bcings pa la slabs so//de nas thams cad dgug pa dang /dbang du bya ba dang /brtan 'pho ba dang srung ba grub
179a1(rgyud): @#//po//rdo rje khro bo'i rtog pa'o//de nas rdo rje srin po las tshogs kyi gtso bos snying po 'di rnams phul lo//'u~M ma ta pa la ka ma ha ga na ba de bi ba pi pa ru d+ha ri na bsarba du sh+sha nan phaT//'u~M mA lA d+ha rA tsA lA tsA
179a2(rgyud): la ya hU~M phaT//'u~M rtar bur rta ra pa shi ku ru sarba swad ta nan swAhA//'u~M dza ya bi dza ya hU~M phaT//de nas 'dir dkyil 'khor 'byung ste//gzhu lta bur bris la der rdo rje gzhag go/g.yasu gnyis g.yon du yang gnyis so//

179a6(rgyud): phyag rgyas bcug la las 'di slab po//mi dge ba'i sems can thams cad mthung shig phrogs shig/srogs shig/de nas phyag rgya bzhi bcings pas slab po//'jig rten gsum las nam rgyal

179a8(rgyud): par rgyal ba'i rtog pa chen po'i 'dir rang gi snying po 'di la phul lo//hU~M ma ra ha dang shod ka ta bi ka ta d+ha sarba kha na kha na hU~M phaT//hU~M sarba mar dan sa hU~M phaT//'u~M bi ka tse te'i ka dang shra/b+hin d+ha hU~M phaT/'u~M ma ha ni har

179b2(rgyud): gsum yas rnam par rgyal ba'i rtog pa chen po'i las dran gyi rtog pa'o//de nas ma thams cad kyis ku bi sgra thon te gar gi mchod pa chen pos bcom ldan 'das la mchod nas rang gi snying snying po phul lo//de nas rdo rje'i blon
179b3(rgyud): mo dag gis smras pa 'u~M bad badzra kro ha ral hU~M phaT/'u~M bad badzra tsag kra tshin hU~M phaT/'u~M shan ti hi/'u~M bad dza ma shrI ha na tshin d+ha hU~M phaT/'u~M bumar a bi sha g+ha na ti ka/ra Na ra Na tsA la ya sarba du shan hU~M phaT/de nas
179b4(rgyud): de dag gi dkyil 'khor grub bzhi pa//de'i sgo bzhi sko 'khor lo laswogs pa bri'o//de'i dbus su rdo rje khro mo me lag na rdo rjes gsal phur can gzhag go/dam tshig gi chog ni chog de bzhin no//'jig

179b7(rgyud): po las cho ga ni de bzhin no//'di ni khro bo'i dkyil 'khor ro//'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgya la ba'i rtog pa chen po las rdo rje khro mo rnams kyi rtog pa'o/de nas tshogs ma mo rnams kyis smras pa/'u~M kha 
179b8(rgyud): rga ma ri Ni hU~M/'u~M a sha bi ba d+he hi ra mo hi/'u~M pa sha sa ma ye hU~M/'u~M sarba ra ma ni ra ti pre ye ho//de nas de dag gi dkyil 'khor bzlum po ste//sgo bzhi pa dbus bu se ral srad bu byas te thig bzhi btab la rim
180a1(rgyud): @#//pa bzhi bri'o//gzhan yang phyi'i dkyil 'khor de bzhin du bzlum por thig bzhi rim du btab la de'i dbus su gnod sbyin gdong bris dngos bzhi ni rims kyis de shar phyogs nas rtsams nas bri 'am

180a8(rgyud): po'o//rdo rje bran mo rnams kyis smras pa /'u~M bA rA hi bad dzra mu khI brid te da d+ha ra pa ta lan sa la ya mnu smra ra e b+hi shA hU~M phaT/'u~M ka la pa li mA lA d+ha ra ni ka Ta ka Ta ma Ta ma Ta kha da bi ba ma ra ni hU~M 'u~M bu ta ni 
180b1(rgyud): u tshur sh+mi rung dre kha da sarban sho shA ya swAhA//hU~M ma ra ka ra mu khi ha ra ha ra hU~M phaT//dkyil 'khor gru bzhi la sgo bzhir bris nas//de dbus su rdo rje bris te de bzhin gar gyis mchod do//'jig rten gsum las

180b5(rgyud): pa rtag tu chags pa de dag gi don gyi phyir gang gis se na de dag bya ba thams cad byed bzhin pa dang/brtson 'grus med pa dang /dka' bar spyad pa yang med par bde ba rtag tu tshe 'di nyid

181a1(rgyud): //phyag rgya bcings pa ci lta bu/de rnams kyi ni de nyid gang/byin gyis rlabs stsogs ji lta bu//sgrub pa rnams kyang ci lta mthong //dngos grub 'dod pa ci lta bu//ras la ri mo ci ltar bri//sku gzugs tshan ni ci tsam

181a4(rgyud): po yin//sems can kun sems gar pa yin//rtags pa'i sems dpa' chen po zhes bya'o//mnyam pa gzhag pa tinge 'dzin /nges par rdo rje nyid du 'gyur ro//de la smras pa sos gzugs med pa ci

181a8(rgyud): ba gang zhe na /sems dpa' kun gyis bdag ces na /bdag nyid la yang gnaso zhes /byin gyis brlabs nas sems dpa' bdag yin zhes nga rgyal bsgom par bya//de nyid bcom ldan 'das sems dpa' ste/rdo rje
